pāśupatasūtra, 1

athātaḥ paśupateḥ pāśupataṃ yogavidhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ  || 1.1 ||

* hitārthamakhilaṃ yena sṛṣṭaṃ brahmādikaṃ jagat | praṇamya taṃ paśupatiṃ śirasā sadasaspatim | arthātiśayasampannaṃ jñānātiśayamuttamam | pañcārthaṃ kriyate bhāṣyaṃ kauṇḍinyenānupūrvaśaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.1:1
* āha vakṣyati bhagavān pañcārtham  || bhāṣya 1.1:2
* athāsyādisūtraṃ kimiti  || bhāṣya 1.1:3
* atrocyate athātaḥ paśupateḥ pāśupataṃ yogavidhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ iti  || bhāṣya 1.1:4
* etatprathamasūtraṃ śāstrādāvuccāryate  || bhāṣya 1.1:5
* tadanantaraṃ padavigrahaḥ kriyate  || bhāṣya 1.1:6
* tadupayoginaṃ yogavidhiṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti  || bhāṣya 1.1:7
* aṣṭapadaṃ sūtram  || bhāṣya 1.1:8
* tatra atha ataḥ iti dve pade naipātike  || bhāṣya 1.1:9
* paśupaterityetatpadaṃ parigrahārthenoccāryate  || bhāṣya 1.1:10
* pāśupatamiti taddhitam  || bhāṣya 1.1:11
* yogavidhimiti sāmāsikam  || bhāṣya 1.1:12
* vi āṅiti dve pade | khyāsyāma ityākhyātikam  || bhāṣya 1.1:13
* āha kiṃ prayojanaṃ padavigrahaḥ kriyate  || bhāṣya 1.1:14
* taducyate arthaprasiddhyartham  || bhāṣya 1.1:15
* kasmādarthāprasiddhiḥ padānām  || bhāṣya 1.1:16
* yasmātpṛthagarthānīha padāni bhavanti  || bhāṣya 1.1:17
* yasmādevaṃ hyāha  || bhāṣya 1.1:18
* yathā vivṛtagātro' pi śirasi prāvṛto naraḥ | nābhivyaktiṃ vrajatyevaṃ sūtraṃ vigrahavarjitam  || bhāṣya 1.1:19
* evamarthaprasiddhyarthaṃ padavigrahaḥ kriyate  || bhāṣya 1.1:20
* āha uktaḥ padavigrahaḥ prayojanaṃ ca  || bhāṣya 1.1:21
* idaṃ tu vācyamatha śāstrādiḥ kaḥ iti  || bhāṣya 1.1:22
* atrocyate athātaḥ paśupaterityeṣa tāvacchāstrādiḥ  || bhāṣya 1.1:23
* tatra śāstraṃ tantraṃ grantho vidyā ca  || bhāṣya 1.1:24
* granthārthayostadadhigamopāyatvāt || bhāṣya 1.1:25
* pārimāṇyamathaśabdādiśivāntaṃ pravacanam  || bhāṣya 1.1:26
* saṃkhyā pañcādhyāyāḥ pañca brahmāṇi adhikaraṇaṃ ca  || bhāṣya 1.1:27
* athaśabdātaḥśabdavyākhyānavacanasnānaśayanādyupadeśācca śiṣyācāryayoḥ prasiddhiḥ  || bhāṣya 1.1:28
* kaivalyagatānāmapi duḥkhitvadarśanātkāryakāraṇapratyakṣadarśī vipratvādupāyopeyapratyakṣadarśitvācca praśnaprativaktā aiśvaryāvasthaścaiva muktatvānmano'manaḥsaṃsthaśca kāmitvādataḥ sarvācāryaviśiṣṭo'yamācārya iti  || bhāṣya 1.1:29
* tathā brāhmaṇagrahaṇātstrīpratiṣedhādindriyajayopadeśācca uktaṃ hi | bādhiryamāndhyamaghratvaṃ mūkatā jaḍatā tathā | unmādaḥ kauṇyaṃ kuṣṭhitvaṃ klaibyaṃ gudāvartapaṅgutā  || bhāṣya 1.1:30
* ādirahitaḥ paṭvindriyo brāhmaṇaḥ śiṣyaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.1:31
* sa cānyaviśiṣṭo'yaṃ śiṣya iti  || bhāṣya 1.1:32
* tathā devādibhyaśca krīḍādharmitvātkrīḍānimittā īśvarapravṛttiḥ  || bhāṣya 1.1:33
* anugrahārthā cācāryasya pravacanavaktṛtve pravṛttiḥ  || bhāṣya 1.1:34
* tathā bhajanacodanaprasādaśivatvalipsopadeśādduḥkhāntārthinaḥ śiṣyasyehopasadanapravṛttiḥ  || bhāṣya 1.1:35
* na tu dharmārthakāmakaivalyārthātreti  || bhāṣya 1.1:36
* tathā kāmitvādindrakauśikādibhyaścācāryo divyo niratiśayakrīḍaiśvaryasvābhāvyādityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.1:37
* codanopasadanasaṃskāravaśyādiduḥkhairabhibhūtatvāccādivyā indrakauśikādyāḥ śiṣyā iti  || bhāṣya 1.1:38
* tathā śiṣṭaprāmāṇyātkāmitvādajātatvācca manuṣyarūpī bhagavān brāhmaṇakāyamāsthāya kāyāvataraṇe avatīrṇa iti  || bhāṣya 1.1:39
* tathā padbhyāmujjayinīṃ prāptaḥ | kasmāt | śiṣṭaprāmāṇatcihnadarśanaśravaṇācca | atyāśramaprasiddhaṃ liṅgamāsthāya pravacanamuktavān bhasmasnānaśayanānusnānanirmālyaikavāsograhaṇādadhikaraṇaprasiddhyarthaṃ ca svaśāstrokte āyatane śiṣyasambandhārthaṃ śucau deśe bhasmavedyāmuṣitaḥ | ato rudrapracoditaḥ kuśikabhagavānabhyāgatyācārye paripūrṇaparitṛptyādyutkarṣalakṣaṇāni viparītāni cātmani dṛṣṭvā pādāvupasaṃgṛhya nyāyena jātiṃ gotraṃ śrutamanṛṇatvaṃ ca nivedayitvā kṛtakṣaṇamācāryaṃ kāle vaidyavadavasthitamāturavadavasthitaḥ śiṣyaḥ pṛṣṭavān bhagavan kimeteṣāmādhyātmikādhibhautikādhidaivikānāṃ sarvaduḥkhānāmaikāntiko'tyantiko vyapoho'styuta neti | athoktaparigrahādhikāralipsāsu parāpadeśenopadeśe sacchiṣyasādhakapāṭhaprasiddhyarthaṃ kāraṇapadārthādhigamārthaṃ cātmani parāpadeśaṃ kṛtvā bhagavānevoktavānatheti | atra pūrvaprakṛtāpekṣāyāmathaśabdaḥ | katham | śiṣyeṇodīritaṃ pūrvaṃ praśnamapekṣyoktavānatheti | evamayamathaśabdaḥ pṛṣṭaprativacanārtho'sti | sa duḥkhānta ityarthaḥ | āha kiṃ parīkṣitāya śiṣyāya duḥkhāntaḥ pratijñātaḥ utāparīkṣitāyeti | ucyate parīkṣitāya | yasmādāha iti | atra ataḥśabdaḥ śiṣyaguṇavacane | yasmādayaṃ brahmāvartadeśajaḥ kulajaḥ paṭvindriyo vividiṣādisampannaḥ śiṣyaḥ | pūrvaṃ cātrārthato'taḥ śabdo draṣṭavyaḥ | atha sa duḥkhāntaḥ kutaḥ prāpyate kena vābhyupāyeneti | taducyate paśupateḥ | prasādāditi vākyaśeṣaḥ | atra paśūnāṃ patiḥ  || bhāṣya 1.1:40
* paśupatiḥ | atra paśavo nāma siddheśvaravarjaṃ sarve cetanāvantaḥ | kāryakaraṇāñjanā nirañjanāśca paśavaḥ | āha kiṃ teṣāṃ paśutvam | ucyate anaiśvaryaṃ bandhaḥ | kāraṇaśaktisannirodhalakṣaṇamasvātantryamanaiśvaryaṃ bandho'nāḍiḥ | bandhaguṇa ityupacaryate | tatkathaṃlakṣaṇamiti cet | taducyate paśyanātpāśanācca paśavaḥ | tatra pāśā nāma kāryakaraṇākhyāḥ kalāḥ | tāśca kalā upariṣṭādvakṣyāmaḥ | tābhiḥ pāśitāḥ baddhāḥ saṃniruddhāḥ śabdādiviṣayaparavaśāśca bhūtvāvatiṣṭhante ityato'vagamyate'svātantryamanaiśvaryaṃ bandhaḥ | kāryakaraṇarahitasya paśutvaṃ nivartata iti cet | taducyate saṃhṛtānāmapi punaḥ punaḥ saṃbandhagrahaṇācchāstre | kiṃ cānyat | paśyanācca paśavaḥ | yasmādvibhutve'pi citsamavetatve'pi ca śarīramātrameva paśyantyupalabhanti ca na bahirddhāni | kāryakaraṇarahitāśca na kāryakaraṇaṃ pratipadyate tyajanti vā | dharmādharmaprakāśadeśakālacodanādyapekṣitatvācca | ataḥ suṣṭhūktaṃ paśyanātpāśanācca paśavaḥ | yasmāduktaṃ sāṃkhyayogena ye muktāḥ sāṃkhyayogeśvarāśca ye | brahmādayastiryagantāḥ sarve te paśavaḥ smṛtāḥ  || bhāṣya 1.1:41
* patiḥ kasmāt | āpti pāti ca tān paśūnityataḥ patirbhavati | tān kenāpnoti kena rakṣati | tato vibhuśaktyā | yasmāttatrāpi śaktimasyānantāṃ nātivartante | vipratvāccāsyānantā jñānaśaktiḥ aparimitā | tayā aparimitayā aparimitāneva pratyakṣān paśūnāpnotīti patiḥ | tathā pālayatīti prabhuśaktiḥ | kasmāt | tacchandātteṣāṃ pravṛttinivṛttiḥ sthitiriṣṭāniṣṭasthānaśarīrendriyaviṣayādiprāptirbhavati | tatparidṛṣṭānāṃ tatpracoditānāṃ cetyarthaḥ | evaṃ  || bhāṣya 1.1:42
* paśupateriti kāryakāraṇayoḥ prasādasya coddeśaḥ | tasmātprasādātsa duḥkhāntaḥ prāpyate | na tu jñānavairāgyadharmaiśvāryatyāgamātrādityarthaḥ | āha kutrasthasya kadā kīdṛśasya vā sa bhagavān prasīdatīti | ucyate yadānena tu tatprāptaṃ bhavati | āha kiṃ taditi | ucyate pāśupatamatra paśupatinoktaṃ parigṛhītaṃ paśupatimadhikṛtya cārabhyata iti pāśupatam | yathā vaiṣṇavaṃ mānasamiti | kiṃ taditi | ucyate yogam | atrātmeśvarasaṃyogo yogaḥ | sa punaḥ puruṣasyādhyayanādinaimittikatvādanyatarakarmajaḥ sthāṇuśyenavat | codanādhyayanādivacanādmeṣavadubhayakarmajaḥ | yasmātsati vibhutve anadhikārakṛtatvādviyogasya | viyuktasyaiva ca saṃyoga upadiśyate | viṣayaraktaviraktavatkriyāyoge | iha tu samādhilakṣaṇe yoge saṃniyama iti | āha kiṃ parijñānamātrādeva tadyogaḥ prāpyate | ucyate | yasmādāha tatprāptau vidhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ | atra yogasya vidhiḥ yogavidhiriti ṣaṣṭhītatpuruṣasamāsaḥ | atra sūkṣmasthūlasabāhyābhyantarasalakṣaṇavilakṣaṇakriyāsu vidhisaṃjñā yajñavidhivat | na tu senāvanādivat | kasmāt | kriyāṇāṃ kṣaṇikānāṃ samudāyāsambhavāt | yadyevaṃ vidhiḥ kasmāt | vidhāyakatvādvidhiḥ | upāyopeyabhāvācca | vidhimiti karma | eva saduḥkhāntaḥ kāryaṃ kāraṇaṃ yogo vidhiriti pañcaiva padārthāḥ samāsata uddiṣṭāḥ | te vyākhyeyāḥ | vyākhyānameteṣāṃ vistaravibhāgaviśeṣopasaṃhāranigamanāni | tasmādanyadvyākhyeyamanyadvyākhyānam | yasmādāha vyākhyāsyāmaḥ | atra viḥ vistare vibhāge viśeṣe ca bhavati | tatra vistara iti pratyakṣānu  || bhāṣya 1.1:43
* mānāptavacanamiti pramāṇānyabhidhīyante | tatra pratyakṣaṃ dvividhamindriyapratyakṣamātmapratyakṣaṃ ca | indriyapratyakṣamindriyārthāḥ śabdasparśarūparasagandhaghaṭāhāḥ vyākhyānatāpamūtrapurīṣamāṃsalavaṇaprāṇāyāmaiḥ siddham | ātmapratyakṣaṃ tadupahārakṛtsnatapoduḥkhāntādi vacanātsiddham | yathā prasthena mito vrīhiḥ prasthaḥ | paramārthatastvindriyārthasambandhavyañjakasāmagryaṃ dharmādharmaprakāśadeśakālacodanādyanugṛhītaṃ satpramāṇamutpadyate | ātmapratyakṣaṃ tu cittāntaḥkaraṇasambandhasāmagryam | anumānamapi pratyakṣapūrvakaṃ cittātmāntaḥkaraṇasambandhasāmagryaṃ ca dharmādharmaprakāśadeśakālacodanādismṛtihetukamutpattyanugrahatirobhāvakālādi | taiścottarasṛṣṭikartṛtvamanumīyate kāraṇasya | ato notsūtram | tacca dvividhaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ sāmānyatodṛṣṭaṃ ca | tatra dṛṣṭamapi dvividhaṃ pūrvavaccheṣavacca | tatra pūrvadṛṣṭo'yaṃ ṣaḍaṅgulīyakaḥ sa eveti pūrvavat | viṣāṇādimātradarśanādgauriti śeṣavat | sāmānyatodṛṣṭamapīha gatipūrvikāṃ deśāntaraprāptiṃ dṛṣṭvā cāsyādityādigatiprasiddhiḥ | traikālye'pyarthādhigame nimittaṃ pramāṇam | āgamo nāma ā maheśvarādgurupāramparyāgataṃ śāstram | āgamo'laukikādivyavahāraheturācakṣitaḥ smṛtaḥ | rudraḥ provāca vacanātsiddhiḥ | eṣvevopamānārthāpattisambhavābhāvaitihyapratibhādīnāṃ vyākhyāyamānānāmantarbhāvaḥ | evametāni trīṇi pramāṇāni | pramāpayitā bhagavāṃścodakaḥ | pramātā puruṣaḥ | prameyāḥ kāryakāraṇādayaḥ pañca padārthāḥ | pramitiḥ saṃvit | saṃvitsaṃcintanaṃ sambodho vidyābhivyaktirityarthaḥ | uddeśanirdeśādhigamācca virvistare bhavati | vibhāgo nāma padapadārthasūtraprakaraṇādhyāyādyasaṃkaraḥ | viśeṣo  || bhāṣya 1.1:44
* nāma sādhyasādhanavyatirekaḥ | āṅiti vyākhyāmaryādāyāṃ bhavati | padātpadaṃ sūtrātsūtraṃ prakaraṇātprakaraṇamadhyāyādadhyāyamā bodhādā parisamāpteriti maryādāvasthasyaiva ca vakṣyāmaḥ | khyā prakathane | pratītāpratītābhiḥ saṃjñābhiḥ vedādivihitābhivyatirekeṇa ca vyākhyāsyāmaḥ | syā ityeṣye kāle | yāvadayamācāryo gṛhasthādibhyo'bhyāgataṃ pūrvamataḥśabdātparīkṣitaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ vratopavāsādyaṃ mahādevasya dakṣiṇasyāṃ mūrtai sadyojātādisaṃskṛtena bhasmanā saṃskaroti utpattiliṅgavyāvṛttiṃ kṛtvā mantraśrāvaṇaṃ ca karoti tāvadeṣyaḥ kālaḥ kriyate | ma iti pratijñāyāṃ bhavati | utthānādigaṇe samyagvvāvasthitasya vyākhyeyavyākhyānayorbhagavāneva kramaśo vaktā | sthūlopāyapūrvakatvātsūkṣmavidheyādhigamasya pūrvāśramaniyamapratiṣedhārthamatyāśramayamaniyamaprasiddhyarthaṃ ca vidhiḥ prathamaṃ vyākhyāyate | ityatrāyaṃ padārthopanyāsaḥ parisamāpta iti  || bhāṣya 1.1:45
* atrāha pratipannāṃśo yathāvidhi prathamaṃ vyākhyāyate | idamidānīṃ cintyam | athāsya kaścādiḥ kiṃ madhyaṃ ko'ntaḥ katyaṅgo vā vidhiriti | taducyate bhasmanādyo nindāmadhyo mūḍhāntaśca vidhiḥ | sa ca tryaṅgo dānayajanatapoṅga iti | tatkathamavagamyate yasmādidamārabhyate  || bhāṣya 1.1:46

**********************************************

bhasmanā triṣavaṇaṃ snāyīta  || 1.2 ||

* atra bhasma vāmadravyaṃ yadagnīndhanasaṃyogānniṣpannam  || bhāṣya 1.2:1
* tatparakṛtaṃ pārthivaṃ bhuktaṃ dīptimat || bhāṣya 1.2:2
* grāmādibhyo bhaikṣyavadbhasmārjanaṃ kartavyam  || bhāṣya 1.2:3
* snānaśayanānusnānakṛtyabandhutvānniṣparigrahatvādahiṃsakatvādutkṛṣṭameva śuci prabhūtaṃ grāhyaṃ sādhanatvāt || bhāṣya 1.2:4
* alābhe svalpamapi grāhyam  || bhāṣya 1.2:5
* ādhāro'pyalābucarmavastrādiratra prasiddhaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.2:6
* āha kiṃ tena bhasmanā kartavyam  || bhāṣya 1.2:7
* taducyate bhasmaneti tṛtīyā karaṇārthe kartuḥ kriyāmādiśati yathā vāśyā takṣaṇaṃ buddhyā pidhānam  || bhāṣya 1.2:8
* āha atha kasmin kāle sā kriyā kartavyeti  || bhāṣya 1.2:9
* triṣavaṇamiti  || bhāṣya 1.2:10
* dviguḥ samāsaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.2:11
* trīṇīti saṃkhyā  || bhāṣya 1.2:12
* savanamiti kālanirdeśaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.2:13
* pūrvasaṃdhyā madhyāhnasaṃdhyā aparasaṃdhyeti saṃdhyātrayam  || bhāṣya 1.2:14
* triṣavaṇaṃ trisaṃdhyaṃ trikālamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.2:15
* āha triṣavaṇaṃ kimanena kartavyam  || bhāṣya 1.2:16
* taducyate snāyīta  || bhāṣya 1.2:17
* atra snānaṃ śaucakāryeṇa śarīreṣvāgantukānāṃ snehatvaglepamalagandhādīnāṃ bhasmanāpakarṣaṇaṃ kartavyam  || bhāṣya 1.2:18
* snānaṃ tu bhasmadravyagātrasaṃyojanam  || bhāṣya 1.2:19
* paramārthatastu snānādi puṇyaphalasaṃyogadharmātmavacanādātmaśaucamevaitat || bhāṣya 1.2:20
* kevalaṃ snānādyakaluṣāpahatapāpmādivacanātkāryakaraṇavyapadeśenātmaśaucaṃ vyākhyāyate  || bhāṣya 1.2:21
* ita ityetadājñāyāṃ niyoge ca  || bhāṣya 1.2:22
* niyogatvānnigataṃ niyatatvānnigama ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.2:23
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.2:24
* pratitantrasiddhatvādahiṃsakatvānniḥśreyasahetutvācca  || bhāṣya 1.2:25
* bhasmanā sneyaṃ na cādbhirviparītatvādityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.2:26
* kiṃ snānamevaivaṃ bhasmanā kartavyam  || bhāṣya 1.2:27
* ucyate  || bhāṣya 1.2:28
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 1.2:29

**********************************************

bhasmani śayīta  || 1.3 ||

* atra bhasma tadeva  || bhāṣya 1.3:1
* niruktamasya pūrvoktam  || bhāṣya 1.3:2
* bhasmani ityaupaśleṣikaṃ saṃnidhānam  || bhāṣya 1.3:3
* śaya ityupaśamasya viśrāmasyākhyā  || bhāṣya 1.3:4
* ita ityetadājñāyāṃ niyoge ca  || bhāṣya 1.3:5
* bhasmanyeva rātrau svaptavyaṃ nānyatretyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.3:6
* uktaṃ hi | yathā mṛgā mṛtyubhayasya bhītā udvignavāsā na labhanti nidrām | evaṃ yatirdhyānaparo mahātmā saṃsārabhīto na labheta nidrām  || bhāṣya 1.3:7
* kiṃ ca viśeṣārthitvāt || bhāṣya 1.3:8
* viśeṣārthī cāyaṃ brāhmaṇaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.3:9
* uktaṃ hi | na viśeṣārthināṃ nidrā ciraṃ netreṣu tiṣṭhati | hayānāmiva jātyānāmardharātrārdhaśāyinām  || bhāṣya 1.3:10
* tasmātparivṛṣṭe bhūpradeśe divā parigrahaṃ kṛtvā bhasmāstīryādhyayanādhyāpanadhyānābhiniviṣṭena pravacanacintanābhiniveśaiśca śrāntena bāhūpadhānena sadyojātādisaṃskṛte bhasmani rātrau svaptavyamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.3:11
* kimarthamiti cet || bhāṣya 1.3:12
* ucyate tapo'rthaṃ bhūpradeśe śaucārthaṃ viśrāmārthaṃ vā  || bhāṣya 1.3:13
* samaviṣamanimnonnatāyāṃ bhūmau yāmaṃ yāmadvayaṃ vā svaptavyamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.3:14
* āha kiṃ snānaṃ śayanaṃ ca bhasmanā prayojanadvayamevātra kartavyamutānyadapi  || bhāṣya 1.3:15
* savanāntasthasyāsyāśaucakaṃ prāptasya nirghātakaṃ kimiti  || bhāṣya 1.3:16
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 1.3:17

**********************************************

anusnānam  || 1.4 ||

* mantrādisnānavat || bhāṣya 1.4:1
* atra anu iti pṛṣṭhakarmakriyāyāṃ bhavati  || bhāṣya 1.4:2
* anupānānugamanavat || bhāṣya 1.4:3
* snānaṃ tu bhasmadravyasaṃyojanameva  || bhāṣya 1.4:4
* savanatraye snānasyāntareṣu bhuktocchiṣṭakṣutaniṣṭhīvitamūtrapurīṣotsargādinimittakamaśaucakamabhisamīkṣya tadanusnānaṃ kartavyam  || bhāṣya 1.4:5
* kimarthamiti cetśaucārthaṃ liṅgābhivyaktyarthaṃ ca  || bhāṣya 1.4:6
* sneyamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.4:7
* āha kiṃ bhasmaivaikaṃ liṅgābhivyaktikāraṇam  || bhāṣya 1.4:8
* bhaktivivṛddhau vā apratiṣiddhasya sādhanaṃ kimiti  || bhāṣya 1.4:9
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 1.4:10

**********************************************

nirmālyam  || 1.5 ||

* atra bhasmavallokādiprasiddhaṃ nirmālyam  || bhāṣya 1.5:1
* niriti nirmuktasyākhyā  || bhāṣya 1.5:2
* mālyamiti puṣpasamūhaparyāyaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.5:3
* tatparakṛtaṃ kāraṇamūrtyāropitāvatāritaṃ niṣparigrahaṃ padmotpalādyam  || bhāṣya 1.5:4
* bhaktivivṛddhyarthaṃ liṅgābhivyaktyarthaṃ ca taddhāryamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.5:5
* āha bhasmanirmālyena tasya liṅgaṃ vyaktaṃ bhavatīti kva siddham  || bhāṣya 1.5:6
* taducyate iha  || bhāṣya 1.5:7
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 1.5:8

**********************************************

liṅgadhārī  || 1.6 ||

* atra yathānyeṣāmapi varṇāśramiṇāmāśramaprativibhāgakarāṇi liṅgāni bhavanti  || bhāṣya 1.6:1
* tatra gṛhasthasya tāvadvāsastrayaṃ vaiṇavī yaṣṭiḥ sodakaṃ ca kamaṇḍalu sottaroṣṭhavapanaṃ yajñopavītādi liṅgam  || bhāṣya 1.6:2
* tathā brahmacāriṇo'pi daṇḍakamaṇḍalumauñjīmekhalāyajñopavītakṛṣṇājinādi liṅgam  || bhāṣya 1.6:3
* tathā vānaprasthasyāpi karīracīravalkalakūrcajaṭādhāraṇādi liṅgam  || bhāṣya 1.6:4
* tathā bhikṣostridaṇḍamuṇḍakamaṇḍalukāṣāyavāsojalapavitrasthalapavitrādi liṅgam  || bhāṣya 1.6:5
* evamihāpi yadetatpāśupatayogādhikaraṇaṃ liṅgamityāśramaprativibhāgakaraṃ bhasmasnānaśayanānusnānanirmālyaikavāsādiniṣpannaṃ svaśarīralīnaṃ pāśupatamiti laukikādijñānajanakaṃ tat || bhāṣya 1.6:6
* līyanālliṅganācca liṅgam  || bhāṣya 1.6:7
* taddhārayan liṅgadhārī bhavati  || bhāṣya 1.6:8
* daṇḍadhārivadityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.6:9
* āha athaite snānaśayanānusnānādayo'rthāḥ kva kartavyāḥ  || bhāṣya 1.6:10
* kuto vā nirmālyasyārjanaṃ kartavyam  || bhāṣya 1.6:11
* kutrasthena vā taddhāryam  || bhāṣya 1.6:12
* kṛtaliṅgena vā kva vastavyam  || bhāṣya 1.6:13
* taducyate āyatane  || bhāṣya 1.6:14
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 1.6:15

**********************************************

āyatanavāsī  || 1.7 ||

* atha bhasmanirmālyavallokādiprasiddhamāyatanam  || bhāṣya 1.7:1
* āṅiti maryādāyāṃ bhavati  || bhāṣya 1.7:2
* yasmādete gṛhasthādayaḥ prayataniyataśucisādhvācārāḥ svasvamaryādayopatiṣṭhante yajanti ca śāntikapauṣṭikādibhiḥ kriyābhiriti  || bhāṣya 1.7:3
* yajanāccāyatanam  || bhāṣya 1.7:4
* tasmin parakṛta āyatane vastavyamiti vāsī ityāyatanaṃ ca parigṛhṇāti  || bhāṣya 1.7:5
* bhūpradeśe ākāśe vṛkṣamūle bahiḥ prādakṣiṇyena vā yatra kvacitprativasan śiṣṭamaryādayā āyatanavāsī bhavati  || bhāṣya 1.7:6
* pulinavāsavadvasedityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.7:7
* puṇyaphalāvāptiścāsyāśu bhavati  || bhāṣya 1.7:8
* uktaṃ hi | grāme vā yadi vāraṇye puṇyasthānaṃ hi śūlinaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.7:9
* āvāso dharmatṛptānāṃ siddhikṣetraṃ hi tatparam  || bhāṣya 1.7:10
* āha tasminnāyatane prativasatā kāḥ kriyāḥ kartavyāḥ  || bhāṣya 1.7:11
* kiṃ snānādyā upalepanādyā vā  || bhāṣya 1.7:12
* āhosviddṛṣṭā asyānyā vaiśeṣikyaḥ kriyāḥ kartavyāḥ  || bhāṣya 1.7:13
* yathā cānyā vaiśeṣikyaḥ kriyāḥ kartavyāḥ prayojanaṃ ca vakṣyāmaḥ -  || bhāṣya 1.7:14

**********************************************

hasitagītanṛttaḍuṃḍuṃkāranamaskārajapyopahāreṇopatiṣṭhet || 1.8 ||

* atha triṣu snānakāleṣu sadyojātādisaṃskṛtena bhasmanā japatā snātvā japataivāyatanamabhigantavyam  || bhāṣya 1.8:1
* abhigamya ca yatpūrvaṃ japati tatpratyāhārārthaṃ japyamomomoṃ  || bhāṣya 1.8:2
* hasitādīni tu kṛtvā yatpaścājjapati tanniyamārthaṃ japyam  || bhāṣya 1.8:3
* tadatra hasitaṃ nāma yadetatkaṇṭhauṣṭhapuṭavisphūrjanamaṭṭahāsaḥ kriyate taddhasitam  || bhāṣya 1.8:4
* gītamapi gāndharvaśāstrasamayānabhiṣvaṅgeṇa yatra bhagavato maheśvarasya sabhāyāṃ gauṇadravyajakarmajāni nāmāni cintyante tat || bhāṣya 1.8:5
* saṃskṛtaṃ prākṛtaṃ parakṛtamātmakṛtaṃ vā yadgīyate tadgeyam  || bhāṣya 1.8:6
* nṛttamapi nāṭyaśāstrasamayānabhiṣvaṅgeṇa hastapādādīnāmutkṣepaṇamavakṣepaṇamākuñcanaṃ prasāraṇaṃ calanamanavasthānam  || bhāṣya 1.8:7
* nigamakāle niyamārthaṃ geyasahakṛtaṃ nṛttaṃ prayoktavyam  || bhāṣya 1.8:8
* ḍuṃḍuṃkāro nāma ya eṣa jihvāgratālusaṃyogānniṣpadyate puṇyo vṛṣanādasadṛśaḥ saḥ  || bhāṣya 1.8:9
* ḍuṃḍuṃkaraṇaṃ ḍuṃḍuṃkāraḥ  || bhāṣya 1.8:10
* kāraśabdo ḍuṃḍuṃkārasyopahārāṅgāvadhāraṇārthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.8:11
* nama iti  || bhāṣya 1.8:12
* nāpyoṣṭhīyaṃ kartavyaṃ nopāṃśu  || bhāṣya 1.8:13
* mānasaṃ tu namaskaraṇaṃ namaskāraḥ  || bhāṣya 1.8:14
* kāraśabdo vācikopāṃśupratiṣedhārthaṃ mānasopahārāṅgāvadhāraṇārthaṃ cetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.8:15
* japyaṃ nāma sadyojātādiṣvakṣarapaṅktyāṃ manasā bhāvasya saṃcāravicāraḥ  || bhāṣya 1.8:16
* tajjapyam  || bhāṣya 1.8:17
* upeti viśeṣaṇe kriyopasaṃhāre samastatve ca  || bhāṣya 1.8:18
* upaharaṇādupahāro vrataṃ niyama ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.8:19
* upahriyate nivedyate niyogamātrakartṛtvātsādhakenetyupahāraḥ  || bhāṣya 1.8:20
* upatiṣṭhet || bhāṣya 1.8:21
* atropetyabhyupagame  || bhāṣya 1.8:22
* abhyupagatena vidhisthena praṇatavinatenetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.8:23
* tiṣṭhedityaikāgryaṃ pratyāhārābhāvasthitimevādhikurute  || bhāṣya 1.8:24
* sarvakaraṇānāṃ vṛttau pratyāhāraṃ kṛtvā kāyikavācikamānasikābhiḥ kriyābhirupahāraṃ kṛtvā bhṛtyavadupahāreṇa stheyam  || bhāṣya 1.8:25
* apasavyaṃ ca pradakṣiṇamupariṣṭādvakṣyāmaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.8:26
* āha kasya nirmālyaṃ dhāryam  || bhāṣya 1.8:27
* kasya vā āyatane vastavyam  || bhāṣya 1.8:28
* kva copastheyamiti  || bhāṣya 1.8:29
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 1.8:30

**********************************************

mahādevasya dakṣiṇāmūrteḥ  || 1.9 ||

* atra mahānityabhyadhikatve  || bhāṣya 1.9:1
* sarvakṣetrajñānāmabhyadhika utkṛṣṭo vyatiriktaśca bhavatītyabhyadhikaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:2
* ṛṣirvipraḥ adhipatiḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:3
* sadāśivatvamabhyadhikatvaṃ ca pravakṣyāmaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:4
* atra deva iti divu krīḍāyām  || bhāṣya 1.9:5
* krīḍādharmitvāt || bhāṣya 1.9:6
* agnyuṣṇatvavat || bhāṣya 1.9:7
* krīḍāvāneva bhagavān vidyākalāpaśusaṃjñakaṃ trividhamapi kāryamutpādayati anugṛhṇāti tirobhāvayati ca  || bhāṣya 1.9:8
* uktaṃ hi | apracodyaḥ pracodyaistu kāmakārakaraḥ prabhuḥ | krīḍate bhagavān lokairbālaḥ krīḍanakairiva  || bhāṣya 1.9:9
* devasya iti ṣaṣṭhī  || bhāṣya 1.9:10
* svasvāmibhāvaḥ sambandhaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:11
* parigrahārthamevādhikurute  || bhāṣya 1.9:12
* atra dakṣiṇeti dikprativibhāge bhavati  || bhāṣya 1.9:13
* ādityo diśo vibhajati  || bhāṣya 1.9:14
* diśaśca mūrtiṃ vibhajanti  || bhāṣya 1.9:15
* mūrtināma yadetaddevasya dakṣiṇe pārśve sthitenodaṅmukhenopānte yadrūpamupalabhyate vṛṣadhvajaśūlapāṇinandimahākālordhvaliṅgādilakṣaṇaṃ yadvā laukikāḥ pratipadyante mahādevasyāyatanamiti tatropastheyam  || bhāṣya 1.9:16
* dakṣiṇāmūrtigrahaṇātpūrvottarapaścimānāṃ mūrtīnāṃ pratiṣedhaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:17
* mūrtiniyogācca mūrtyabhāve niyamalopaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:18
* bhaikṣyānupayogānnirghātānāmuktatvāccetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:19
* vidhirityupadiṣṭānāmarthānāṃ bhasmasnānopadeśādapsu snānādīnāṃ pratiṣedhaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:20
* bhasmaśayanopadeśādviṣayaśayanādīnāṃ pratiṣedhaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:21
* āyatane vasatyarthopadeśāccheṣavasatyarthapratiṣedhaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:22
* hasitādyupadeśāccheṣopahārapratiṣedhaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:23
* nirmālyopadeśātpratyagrāṇāṃ mālyānāṃ pratiṣedhaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:24
* bhasmanirmālyaliṅgopadeśāccheṣaliṅgapratiṣedhaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:25
* mahādevagrahaṇādanyadevatābhaktipratiṣedhaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:26
* dakṣiṇāmūrtigrahaṇātpūrvapaścimānāṃ mūrtīnāṃ pratiṣedhaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:27
* evaṃ dakṣiṇāmūrtirityukte asya brāhmaṇasya pūrvaprasiddhā niyamā niyamaiḥ pratiṣidhyante  || bhāṣya 1.9:28
* kīlakapratikīlakavatpurāṇodakanavodakavacceti  || bhāṣya 1.9:29
* atredaṃ bhasmaprakaraṇaṃ samāptam  || bhāṣya 1.9:30
* āha niyamābhidhānādeva hi saṃśayaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:31
* yatra yamāstatra niyamāḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:32
* mithunamevaitadyasmāt || bhāṣya 1.9:33
* ato na saṃśayaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:34
* yamā asmin tantre ke cintyante  || bhāṣya 1.9:35
* ucyate prasiddhā yamāḥ ahiṃsādaya iti  || bhāṣya 1.9:36
* atra tvanyeṣām  || bhāṣya 1.9:37
* ahiṃsā brahmacaryaṃ ca satyāsaṃvyavahārakau | asteyamiti pañcaite yamā vai saṃprakīrtitāḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:38
* akrodho guruśuśrūṣā śaucamāhāralāghavam | apramādaśca pañcaite niyamāḥ saṃprakīrtitāḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:39
* tadvadasmākaṃ na bhavati  || bhāṣya 1.9:40
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.9:41
* niyamanivṛttidarśanāt || bhāṣya 1.9:42
* asmin hi tantre kālāntaritā niyamā nivartante  || bhāṣya 1.9:43
* kathamā dehapātādyamānāṃ na nivṛttirasti  || bhāṣya 1.9:44
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.9:45
* hiṃsādidoṣāt || bhāṣya 1.9:46
* tasmādahiṃsādyā daśa sarve te yamāḥ pratyavagantavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:47
* āha yadyevaṃ nigamanivṛttau bhraṣṭaniyamasya patanaprasaṅgaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:48
* ucyate avasitaprayojanatvānna patanaprasaṅgaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:49
* kiṃ ca yamānāṃ prādhānyāt || bhāṣya 1.9:50
* uktaṃ hi patati niyamavān yameṣvasakto na tu yamavānniyamālaso'vasīdet || bhāṣya 1.9:51
* iti yamaniyamau samīkṣya buddhyā yamabahuleṣvatisaṃdadhīta buddhim  || bhāṣya 1.9:52
* tasmānna patanaprasaṅgaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:53
* ataḥ prasiddhā yamā ahiṃsādayaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:54
* āha kiṃ prasiddhā iti kṛtvā gṛhyante āhosvicchakyameteṣāṃ yamānāṃ sarvajñoktaśāstrataḥ sadbhāvo vaktum  || bhāṣya 1.9:55
* ucyate yadyanyatra prasiddhā iti kva  || bhāṣya 1.9:56
* tatra cintyate  || bhāṣya 1.9:57
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.9:58
* kṛtopadeśāt || bhāṣya 1.9:59
* yasmāduktaṃ sūtrataḥkṛtamityatra  || bhāṣya 1.9:60
* kṛtāpratiṣedhā kṛtsnā hiṃsā tantre pratiṣiddhā draṣṭavyetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:61
* sā ca hiṃsā trividhā bhavati  || bhāṣya 1.9:62
* duḥkhotpādanamaṇḍabhedaḥ prāṇanirmocanamiti  || bhāṣya 1.9:63
* tatra duḥkhotpādanaṃ nāma krośanatarjanatāḍananirbhartsanādibahubhedo'pi caturvidhasyāpi bhūtagrāmasya manovākkāyakarmabhirabhidroho na kartavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:64
* evamahiṃsā bhavatyeteṣāṃ jantūnām  || bhāṣya 1.9:65
* aṇḍabhedo nāma dāhatāpadhūmoparodhaparihārārthamagnikaraṇādānasampradānapratinidhānasaṃdhukṣaṇādīni na kuryātnaiva kārayet || bhāṣya 1.9:66
* tathā prāṇanirmocanaṃ nāma vastraśikyabhasmādhārabhaikṣyabhājanādīni muhurmuhurvivecayitavyāni  || bhāṣya 1.9:67
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.9:68
* prāṇino hi sūkṣmacāriṇaḥ kṣiprameva vilayaṃ prayānti  || bhāṣya 1.9:69
* tasmātsūkṣmairaṅgapavitraiḥ pakṣmacāmaratālavṛntairvastrāntarairvā muhurmuhurvigranthodakena vā  || bhāṣya 1.9:70
* hariteṣu tṛṇeṣu na saṃsikte bhūpradeśe bhavati  || bhāṣya 1.9:71
* vasantagrīṣmahaimāntikānaṣṭau māsān bhikṣurvicakramet || bhāṣya 1.9:72
* dayārthaṃ sarvabhūtānāmekatra varṣāsu vaset || bhāṣya 1.9:73
* varṣābhedaṃ tu yaḥ kuryādbrāhmaṇo yogadīkṣitaḥ | prājāpatyena kṛcchreṇa tataḥ pāpātpramucyate  || bhāṣya 1.9:74
* śārīraṃ dṛśyate yatra bhayaṃ kasyāṃcidāpadi | durdine rāṣṭrabhaṅge vā varṣāsvapi vyatikramet || bhāṣya 1.9:75
* nāsūryaṃ ca vrajenmārgaṃ nādṛṣṭāṃ bhūmimākramet | paripūnābhiradbhiśca nityaṃ kuryātprayojanam  || bhāṣya 1.9:76
* saṃvatsarakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ matsyabandhasya yadbhavet | ekāhāttadavāpnoti apūtajalasaṃgrahī  || bhāṣya 1.9:77
* dṛṣṭipūtaṃ nyasetpādaṃ vastrapūtaṃ jalaṃ pibet | satyapūtāṃ vadedvācaṃ manaḥpūtaṃ samācaret || bhāṣya 1.9:78
* hiṃsakāstu nivartante brahmatvamapi ye gatāḥ | tasmādapūtamudakaṃ nopayuñjīta yogavit || bhāṣya 1.9:79
* atha naṣṭe pavitre ca gṛhṇīyāttriṣu vai sakṛt | nadīprasravaṇe caiva gṛhastheṣu ca sādhuṣu  || bhāṣya 1.9:80
* kāṇḍāni yāni gṛhyante kandāścaiva prarohiṇaḥ | bījāni caiva pakvāni sarvāṇyetāni varjayet || bhāṣya 1.9:81
* yadā na kuryāddrohaṃ ca sarvabhūteṣu dāruṇam | karmaṇā manasā vācā brahma saṃpadyate tadā  || bhāṣya 1.9:82
* yo na hiṃsati bhūtāni sthāvarāṇi carāṇi ca | ātmānamiva sarvāṇi so'mṛtatvāya kalpate  || bhāṣya 1.9:83
* na yajñadānairna tapo'gnihotrairna brahmacaryairna ca satyavākyaiḥ | na vedavidyādhyayanairvratairvā prāpyaṃ phalaṃ yadyahiṃsakasya  || bhāṣya 1.9:84
* yo dadyātkāñcanaṃ meruṃ kṛtsnāṃ caiva vasuṃdharām | samudraṃ ratnapūrṇaṃ vā na tulyaṃ syādahiṃsayā  || bhāṣya 1.9:85
* ityevamahiṃsā tantre siddhā  || bhāṣya 1.9:86
* tathā brahmacaryaṃ ca tantre siddham  || bhāṣya 1.9:87
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.9:88
* strīpratiṣedhātindriyajayopadeśācca trayodaśakasya karaṇasyānutsargo brahmacaryamityuktam  || bhāṣya 1.9:89
* viśeṣeṇa tu jihvopasthayoriti  || bhāṣya 1.9:90
* atrāha viśeṣagrahaṇaṃ kiṃprayojanam  || bhāṣya 1.9:91
* trayodaśakasya karaṇasyānutsargo brahmacaryamityuktvā jihvopasthayorviśeṣagrahaṇaṃ kiṃprayojanaṃ kriyate  || bhāṣya 1.9:92
* ucyate pradhānatvāt || bhāṣya 1.9:93
* tanmūlatvāditarapravṛtteḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:94
* tanmūlā hītareṣāṃ pravṛttirbhavati  || bhāṣya 1.9:95
* katham  || bhāṣya 1.9:96
* jihvendriyaviṣaye upasthendriyaviṣaye vā saktaḥ trayodaśabhiḥ pravartate  || bhāṣya 1.9:97
* ata etaduktaṃ viśeṣeṇa jihvopasthayoriti  || bhāṣya 1.9:98
* jihvopasthanimittaṃ hi patanaṃ sarvadehinām | tasmādamitravatpaśyejjihvopasthaṃ hi mānavaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:99
* athavā manaḥpūrvakatvātsarvavṛttīnāṃ tannigrahātsarvavṛttīnāṃ nigrahaḥ kṛto bhavati  || bhāṣya 1.9:100
* uktaṃ hi | mano hi mūlaṃ sarveṣāmindriyāṇāṃ pravartane | śubhāśubhāsvavasthāsu tacca me suvyavasthitam  || bhāṣya 1.9:101
* punarapyuktam | indriyaiḥ prasṛtairduḥkhamindriyairnibhṛtaiḥ sukham | tasmādindriyarūpebhyo yacchedātmānamātmanā  || bhāṣya 1.9:102
* indriyāṇi hi tatsarvaṃ yatsvarganarakāvubhau | nigṛhītavisṛṣṭāni svargāya narakāya ca  || bhāṣya 1.9:103
* ato janma ato duḥkhamato mṛtyubhayaṃ tathā | indriyāṇāṃ prasaṅgādvai tasmādetān jayāmahe  || bhāṣya 1.9:104
* indriyāṇāṃ prasaṅgena doṣamṛcchatyasaṃśayam | saṃniyamya tu tānyeva tataḥ siddhiṃ niyacchati  || bhāṣya 1.9:105
* rajjureṣā nibandhāya yā strīṣu ramate matiḥ | chittvaināṃ kṛtino yānti naināṃ tyajati duṣkṛtī  || bhāṣya 1.9:106
* strīhetornirgamo grāmātstrīkṛte krayavikrayaḥ | striyo mūlamanarthānāṃ naināṃ prājñaḥ pariṣvajet || bhāṣya 1.9:107
* viṣamagnirasirbāṇaḥ sphuṭaṃ kṛtvā vibhīṣikā | māyā rūpavatī hyeṣā yāṃ striyaṃ manyate janaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:108
* amedhyapūrṇe kṛmijantusaṃkule svabhāvadurgandha aśauca adhruve | kaḍebare mūtrapurīṣabhājane ramanti mūrkhā na ramanti paṇḍitāḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:109
* mādyatīti striyaṃ dṛṣṭvā surāṃ pītvā na mādyati | tasmāddṛṣṭimadāṃ nārīṃ dūrataḥ parivarjayet || bhāṣya 1.9:110
* adhomukhenādaṃṣṭreṇa jaghanāntaracāriṇā | sarvaśāstrācikitsyena jagaddaṣṭaṃ bhagāhinā  || bhāṣya 1.9:111
* lomaśena kurūpeṇa durgandhena kucarmaṇā | hariṇīpadamātreṇa sarvamandhīkṛtaṃ jagat || bhāṣya 1.9:112
* dīptāṅgārasamā nārī ghṛtakumbhasamaḥ pumān | ye prasaktā vilīnāste ye sthitāste divaṃ gatāḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:113
* yathāgniredhaḥsaṃvṛddho mahājyotiḥ prakāśate | tathendriyanirodhena svātmajyotiḥ prakāśate  || bhāṣya 1.9:114
* brahmacarye sthitaṃ dhairyaṃ brahmacarye sthitaṃ tapaḥ | ye sthitā brahmacaryeṇa brāhmaṇā divi te sthitāḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:115
* kṣīraṃ pibanti madhu te pibanti somaṃ pibantyamṛtena sārdham | mṛtyoḥ purastādamarā bhavanti ye brāhmaṇā brahmacaryaṃ caranti  || bhāṣya 1.9:116
* ityevaṃ brahmacaryaṃ tantre siddham  || bhāṣya 1.9:117
* tathā satyaṃ tantre siddham  || bhāṣya 1.9:118
* tacca dvividham  || bhāṣya 1.9:119
* tadyathā paridṛṣṭārthabhūtārthaṃ vacanaṃ vāksatyaṃ ceti  || bhāṣya 1.9:120
* tatra paridṛṣṭārthabhūtārthaṃ vacanaṃ satyaṃ tantre siddham  || bhāṣya 1.9:121
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.9:122
* vyākhyānopadeśātvidvadupadeśācca  || bhāṣya 1.9:123
* tathā vāksatyamapi tantre siddham  || bhāṣya 1.9:124
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.9:125
* vāgviśuddhyupadeśāt || bhāṣya 1.9:126
* iha svaśāstroktaṃ bhāṣato'nṛtamapi satyamāpadyate  || bhāṣya 1.9:127
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.9:128
* śuddhivṛddhikaratvāt || bhāṣya 1.9:129
* yasmādāha | svargamanṛtena gacchati dayārthamuktena sarvabhūtānām | satyenāpi na gacchati satāṃ vināśārthamuktena  || bhāṣya 1.9:130
* punastvāha | gobrāhmaṇārthe'vacanaṃ himasti na strīṣu rājanna vivāhakāle | prāṇātyaye sarvadhanāpahāre pañcānṛtānyāhurapātakāni  || bhāṣya 1.9:131
* satyaṃ brūyātpriyaṃ brūyānna brūyātsatyamapriyam | priyaṃ ca nānṛtaṃ brūyādeṣa dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:132
* yathā hi teṣāmeva bhūtānāṃ hitamanṛtamapi satyamāpadyate evamihāpyasmākaṃ svaśāstroktaṃ bhāṣatāmanṛtamapi satyamāpadyate  || bhāṣya 1.9:133
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.9:134
* vidhivihitatvāt || bhāṣya 1.9:135
* ityetadapi tatra siddham  || bhāṣya 1.9:136
* tathā asaṃvyavahārastantre siddhaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:137
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.9:138
* avyaktapretonmattamūḍhopadeśāt || bhāṣya 1.9:139
* neha loke avyaktapretonmattamūḍhāḥ saṃvyavahāraṃ kurvanti yasmādato'trāsaṃvyavahārastantre siddhaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:140
* saṃvyavahāraśca punardvividhaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:141
* tadyathā krayāvakrayasaṃvyavahārā rājakulasaṃvyavahāraśceti  || bhāṣya 1.9:142
* ata ekatareṇāpyatrādhikṛtasyātmapīḍā parapīḍā cāvarjanīye bhavataḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:143
* tatra yadyātmānaṃ pīḍayati tenehaiva loke duḥkhī bhavati  || bhāṣya 1.9:144
* syātparaṃ pīḍayati tatrāpyasyādharmo duḥkhādiphalaḥ pracīyate  || bhāṣya 1.9:145
* tenāmuṣmin loke tīvraṃ duḥkhamanubhavati  || bhāṣya 1.9:146
* tasmādubhayathāpi saṃvyavahāro varjanīyaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:147
* bhavati hyapi | yaśca pāpaṃ prakurute yaśca pāpaṃ praśaṃsati | sahāyaścopabhoktā ca sarve te samakarmiṇaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:148
* uktaṃ hi | vikraye tu mahān doṣo vikrayātpatate yataḥ | eṣa eva kraye doṣastasmāttaṃ parivarjayet || bhāṣya 1.9:149
* pracchannaṃ kurute pāpaṃ na me jānāti kaścana | mucyate janavādebhyastasmātpāpānna mucyate  || bhāṣya 1.9:150
* punarapyuktam | ādityacandrāvanilo'nalaśca dyaurbhūmirāpo hṛdayaṃ yamaśca | ahaśca rātriśca ubhe ca saṃdhye dharmo hi jānāti narasya vṛttam  || bhāṣya 1.9:151
* nārambhaśīlo na ca dambhaśīlaḥ śāstropadiṣṭāni karotyadīnaḥ | yameṣu yukto niyameṣu caiva munirbhavatyeṣvajaro'maraśca  || bhāṣya 1.9:152
* tathā asteyaṃ tantre siddham  || bhāṣya 1.9:153
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.9:154
* avāsopadeśātanutsṛṣṭānnapratiṣedhācca  || bhāṣya 1.9:155
* iha vidyamānasyāpyekasya vāsaso malavadavasthitasyāvāsopadeśātparigrahaparityāga upadiśyate  || bhāṣya 1.9:156
* kiṃcānyadapi  || bhāṣya 1.9:157
* parityaktānāmannapānādīnāmupayogo dṛṣṭo yasmāt || bhāṣya 1.9:158
* ato'trāsteyaṃ tantre siddham  || bhāṣya 1.9:159
* steyaṃ ca punaḥ ṣaḍvidham  || bhāṣya 1.9:160
* tatra adattādānamanatisṛṣṭagrahaṇamanabhimatagrahaṇamanadhikārapratigrahaḥ anupālambhaḥ aniveditopayogaśceti  || bhāṣya 1.9:161
* adattasya grahaṇamadattādānam  || bhāṣya 1.9:162
* anatisṛṣṭagrahaṇaṃ nāma bālonmattapramattavṛddhadurbalānāṃ vittāpaharaṇam  || bhāṣya 1.9:163
* anabhimatagrahaṇaṃ nāma kīṭabhramarapakṣipataṃgādīnāmanabhipretadravyāpaharaṇam  || bhāṣya 1.9:164
* anadhikārapratigraho nāma iha śāstre anabhyanujñātānāmarthānāṃ gobhūhiraṇyadvipadacatuṣpadādīnāṃ grahaṇam  || bhāṣya 1.9:165
* anupālambho nāma kuhakakalkanaḍambhavismāpanavardhāpanādibhirupāyaiḥ parebhyo hiraṇyācchādanopayogaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:166
* aniveditopayogo nāma bhakṣyabhojyalehyapeyacoṣyādīnāmanyatamaṃ yatkiṃcidgurave'niveditamupayuṅkte sa ucyate aniveditopayoga iti  || bhāṣya 1.9:167
* evaṃ ṣaḍvidhaṃ steyam  || bhāṣya 1.9:168
* asya ṣaḍvidhasyāpi steyasya parivarjanamasteyamāhurācāryāḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:169
* bhavati hyapi | yadetaddhanamityāhuḥ prāṇā hyete bahiścarāḥ | sa tasya harate prāṇān yo yasya harate dhanam  || bhāṣya 1.9:170
* uktaṃ hi | sarvasvaparimoṣṭā ca jīvitāntakaraśca yaḥ | dvāvetau samakarmāṇau tasmātsteyaṃ vivarjayet || bhāṣya 1.9:171
* na stenasya paro loko nāyaṃ loko durātmanaḥ | śaṅkitaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ drohātmā pāpa eva saḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:172
* mṛdamāpastathā yānaṃ pattraṃ puṣpaṃ phalānyapi | asaṃvṛtāni gṛhṇīyātpavitrārthīha kāryavān  || bhāṣya 1.9:173
* nadyaśca vāpyaḥ kūpāśca taṭākāni sarāṃsi ca | asaṃvṛtāni gṛhṇīyātprājāpatyena karmaṇā  || bhāṣya 1.9:174
* ityevamasteyaṃ tantre siddham  || bhāṣya 1.9:175
* akrodhastantre siddhaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:176
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.9:177
* śūdrapratiṣedhātatitāpopadeśācca  || bhāṣya 1.9:178
* ihādhyātmikādhibhautikādhidaivikānāṃ sarvadvaṃdvānāṃ manasi śarīre ca upanipatitānāṃ sahiṣṇutvamapratīkāraśceti yasmātkṛto'trākrodhastantre siddhaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:179
* krodhaśca punaścaturvidhaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:180
* tadyathā bhāvalakṣaṇaḥ karmalakṣaṇaḥ vaikalyakaraḥ udvegakaraśceti  || bhāṣya 1.9:181
* tatra bhāvalakṣaṇo nāma saḥ yatrāsūyādveṣamadamānamātsaryādayo bhāvāḥ pravartante  || bhāṣya 1.9:182
* karmalakṣaṇo nāma yatra kalahavairasaṃpraharaṇādyā bhāvāḥ pravartante  || bhāṣya 1.9:183
* vaikalyakaro nāma yatra pāṇipādanāsākṣyaṅgulipraharaṇādayo bhāvāḥ pravartante  || bhāṣya 1.9:184
* udvegakaro nāma yatra svātmānaṃ parātmānaṃ vā prāṇairviyojayati  || bhāṣya 1.9:185
* ityevaṃ caturvidhaḥ krodhaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:186
* asya caturvidhasyāpi krodhasya parivarjanamakrodhamāhurācāryāḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:187
* tasmāddeśajātikulakarmasambandhanindāyāṃ karaṇakriyāyāṃ kāryanindāyāmāhāranindāyāṃ vādhikṛtena krodho na kartavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:188
* tatra deśanindā tāvadbhavati  || bhāṣya 1.9:189
* tadyathā yatra bhavān jātastatra deśe brāhmaṇā eva na santīti yadi kaścidadhikṣepaṃ kuryāttatra krodho na kartavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:190
* tatraitatsyādevamabhihite tīvraduḥkhaṃ mānasamabhivyajyate  || bhāṣya 1.9:191
* kathamatra krodho na bhaviṣyatīti  || bhāṣya 1.9:192
* ucyate na bhaviṣyati  || bhāṣya 1.9:193
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.9:194
* parisaṃkhyānasāmarthyāt || bhāṣya 1.9:195
* iha manuṣyaloke deśo'yaṃ nāma mātāpitṛhetukaḥ aupacayikaḥ kāryapiṇḍaḥ śarīrākhyaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:196
* sa tasmādbhavaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:197
* kṣetrajñastu cetanaḥ sarvagataḥ śuciḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:198
* asya cāsmākaṃ cāntaramaviditam  || bhāṣya 1.9:199
* aparidṛṣṭārthe bhavānetadvā brūyāt || bhāṣya 1.9:200
* ataḥ krodhanimittāsaṃbhavātparisaṃkhyānasāmarthyena krodho na kāryaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:201
* evaṃ śeṣeṣvapi draṣṭavyam  || bhāṣya 1.9:202
* bhavati hyapi | śṛṅgavānnakhavān daṃṣṭrī vikṛto rudhirāśanaḥ | rākṣaso vā piśāco vā krodhiṣṇurjāyate naraḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:203
* punaścāha | kaṅkagṛdhrasṛgāleṣu daṃśeṣu maśakeṣu ca | pannageṣu ca jāyante narāḥ krodhaparāyaṇāḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:204
* vidviṣṭaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ bahvamitro'lpabāndhavaḥ | krūradharmā durācāraḥ krodhiṣṇurjāyate naraḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:205
* kruddhaḥ karoti pāpāni kruddhaḥ pāpāni bhāṣate | kruddho bhavati nirlajjastasmātkrodhaṃ vivarjayet || bhāṣya 1.9:206
* tathā coktam | yatkrodhano japati yacca juhoti yadvā yadvā tapastapyati yaddadāti tatsarvam | vaivasvato harati pūrtamamuṣya sarvaṃ mithyā śrutaṃ bhavati tasya śamo'pi tasya  || bhāṣya 1.9:207
* dhanyāste puruṣavyāghrā ye buddhyā krodhamutthitam | śamayanti mahātmāno dīptamagnimivāmbhasā  || bhāṣya 1.9:208
* yato rūpaṃ tato jñānaṃ yato jñānaṃ tatastapaḥ | yatastapastataḥ siddhiryataḥ siddhistataḥ kṣamā  || bhāṣya 1.9:209
* kṣamā sarvaparaṃ mitraṃ krodhaḥ sarvaparo ripuḥ | kṣamāvatāmayaṃ lokaḥ paro lokaḥ kṣamāvatām  || bhāṣya 1.9:210
* etasmātkāraṇātkṣantavyamityevamakrodhastantre siddhaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:211
* tathā guruśuśrūṣā tantre siddhā  || bhāṣya 1.9:212
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.9:213
* vyākhyānopadeśādvidvadupadeśācca  || bhāṣya 1.9:214
* iha coktaṃ vidhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ iti  || bhāṣya 1.9:215
* atrāṇiti maryādāyām  || bhāṣya 1.9:216
* ma iti pratijñāyāṃ bhavati  || bhāṣya 1.9:217
* mayi vartate  || bhāṣya 1.9:218
* mayi tiṣṭhatīti  || bhāṣya 1.9:219
* yadi ceṣṭe vatsyasi yadi ceṣṭe sthāsyasi tataste vakṣyāmaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:220
* tatreṣṭamityaṣṭāṅgaṃ brahmacaryaṃ maryādāmadhikurute  || bhāṣya 1.9:221
* tadyathā utthānapratyutthānābhivādanagurukāryahitakārī anuttarottaravādī pūrvotthāyī jaghanyasaṃveśī preṣitāpreṣitasarvakāryakṛtajñaḥ sarvaniveditātmā dakṣo dākṣiṇyānuraktaḥ snānodvartanasaṃvāhanādibhiḥ kriyāviśeṣaiḥ chāyevānugato nityamidaṃ kṛtamidaṃ kariṣye kiṃ karavāṇīti bhūtvā gurave'haraharvartitavyam  || bhāṣya 1.9:222
* yastu vidyāṃ guroradhikṛtya bahubhyaḥ samprayacchati anenāsya vidyāyā dānena guravaḥ śuśrūṣitā bhavanti  || bhāṣya 1.9:223
* kṣīṇe ca brahmacarye niyataṃ guruṣu yadgauravaṃ tadbrahmacaryam  || bhāṣya 1.9:224
* bhavati hyapi | gururdevo guruḥ svāmī gururmātā guruḥ pitā | yasyaivaṃ niścito bhāvaḥ śreyastasya na dūrataḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:225
* agnisūryendutārābhiścākṣuṣo'rthaḥ prakāśate | bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ bhaviṣyaṃ ca guruvākyaiḥ prakāśate  || bhāṣya 1.9:226
* deśakairgamyate'dhvānaṃ deśakairgamyate'rṇavaḥ | deśakairgamyate svargo gururmokṣasya deśakaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:227
* amṛtasya pradātāraṃ yo guruṃ hyavamanyate | ṣaṣṭivarṣasahasrāṇi narakaṃ paryupāsate  || bhāṣya 1.9:228
* guroryatra parīvādo nindā yatra pravartate | karṇau tatra pidhātavyau gantavyaṃ vā tato'nyataḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:229
* ācāryaṃ pūjayedyastu sarvāvasthaṃ hi nityaśaḥ | pūjitastena bhavati śivo vai nātra saṃśayaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:230
* ācāryamūrtimāsthāya śivo jñānaṃ prayacchati | tasmādvai nāvamantavya ācāryaḥ śreya icchatā  || bhāṣya 1.9:231
* granthārthaviduṣe nityaṃ yogamārgānudarśine | sarvārthenāpi kartavyaḥ paritoṣo vijānatā  || bhāṣya 1.9:232
* ṛcaṃ vā yadi vārdharcaṃ pādaṃ vā yadi vākṣaram | sakāśādyasya gṛhṇīyānniyataṃ tatra gauravam  || bhāṣya 1.9:233
* liṅgakartrī yathā mātā śāstrakartā yathā pitā | prabodhakṛdgurusteṣāṃ tadevāyatanaṃ mahat || bhāṣya 1.9:234
* ityevaṃ guruśuśrūṣā tantre siddhā  || bhāṣya 1.9:235
* tathā śaucaṃ tantre siddham  || bhāṣya 1.9:236
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.9:237
* bhasmasnānopadeśāt || bhāṣya 1.9:238
* tacca śaucaṃ trividham  || bhāṣya 1.9:239
* tadyathā gātraśaucaṃ bhāvaśaucamātmaśaucaṃ ceti  || bhāṣya 1.9:240
* tatra bhasmasnānopadeśātprasiddhaṃ bhasmanā gātraśaucam  || bhāṣya 1.9:241
* āha yaduktaṃ prasiddhaṃ bhasmanā gātraśaucamiti etadevāyuktam  || bhāṣya 1.9:242
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.9:243
* pūrvottaravyāghātāt || bhāṣya 1.9:244
* iha purastāduktaṃ prasiddhā yamā ahiṃsādayo bhavanti  || bhāṣya 1.9:245
* yadiha bhūyo'pi aprasiddhaṃ bhasmanā gātraśaucamityabhidhīyate  || bhāṣya 1.9:246
* tasmādidaṃ pūrvottaraṃ na saṃgacchati  || bhāṣya 1.9:247
* vyāhataṃ ca bhavati  || bhāṣya 1.9:248
* eṣa doṣa ityataḥ pūrvottaravyāghātāt || bhāṣya 1.9:249
* tatra yaduktaṃ prasiddhaṃ bhasmanā gātraśaucamityetadayuktam  || bhāṣya 1.9:250
* ucyate nāyaṃ doṣaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:251
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.9:252
* prasiddhidarśanāt || bhāṣya 1.9:253
* ihānyatrāpi prasiddhaṃ bhasmanā gātraśaucamiti  || bhāṣya 1.9:254
* evaṃ hyāha | saṃsargajāśca ye doṣā ye cānye pitṛmātṛjāḥ | annapānakṛtāścaiva saṃkarā dehamāśritāḥ | sarvāṃstān dahate bhasma asthimajjāgatānapi  || bhāṣya 1.9:255
* punaścāha | keśakīṭopapannāni duṣṭānnāni ca yāni vai | bhasmanā spṛṣṭamātrāṇi bhojyānyāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:256
* punarapyuktam | madyaṃ pītvā gurudārāṃśca gatvā steyaṃ kṛtvā brahmahatyāṃ ca kṛtvā | bhasmoddhvasto bhasmarāśau śayāno rudrādhyāyī mucyate pātakebhyaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:257
* yaḥ snānamācarennityamāgneyaṃ saṃyatendriyaḥ | kulaikaviṃśamuddhṛtya sa gacchetparamāṃ gatim  || bhāṣya 1.9:258
* evamanyatrāpi prasiddhaṃ bhasmanā gātraśaucam  || bhāṣya 1.9:259
* tasmādyuktaṃ vaktuṃ prasiddhā yamā ahiṃsādaya iti  || bhāṣya 1.9:260
* tathopasparśanaprāṇāyāmajapyaiḥ akaluṣamatirbhavatīti bhāvaśaucaṃ tantre siddham  || bhāṣya 1.9:261
* bhāvamantargataṃ duṣṭaṃ na snānamapakarṣati | bhāvaśuddhiḥ parā śuddhiḥ śeṣaṃ śṛṅgāramārjanam  || bhāṣya 1.9:262
* mṛttikānāṃ sahasreṇa jalakumbhaśatena ca | na śudhyanti durātmānaḥ pāpopahatacetasaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:263
* satyaṃ śaucaṃ tapaḥ śaucaṃ śaucamindriyanigrahaḥ | sarvabhūtadayā śaucamadbhiḥ śaucaṃ tu pañcamam  || bhāṣya 1.9:264
* śaucameva paraṃ teṣāṃ yeṣāṃ notpadyate spṛhā | pratigrahe tathārambhe indriyāṇāṃ ca gocare  || bhāṣya 1.9:265
* yasmādāha | sarvasvamapi yo dadyātkaluṣeṇāntarātmanā | na tena dharmabhāgbhavati bhāva evātra kāraṇam  || bhāṣya 1.9:266
* yathā yathā hi puruṣaḥ kalyāṇīṃ kurute matim | tathā tathāsya sidhyanti sarvārthā nātra saṃśayaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:267
* ityevaṃ bhāvaśaucaṃ tantre siddham  || bhāṣya 1.9:268
* tathātmaśaucaṃ tantre siddham  || bhāṣya 1.9:269
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.9:270
* yasmādavamānaparibhavaparivādādyairapahatapāpmā bhavati ityātmaśaucaṃ tantre siddham  || bhāṣya 1.9:271
* yasmādanyairapyuktam | kṛtsnāṃ mahīṃ paryaṭataḥ saśailavanakānanām | apamānātparaṃ nāsti sādhanaṃ manurabravīt || bhāṣya 1.9:272
* ityevaṃ śaucaṃ tantre siddham  || bhāṣya 1.9:273
* tathā āhāralāghavaṃ tantre siddham  || bhāṣya 1.9:274
* kasmāt | bhaikṣotsṛṣṭayathālabdhopadeśāt || bhāṣya 1.9:275
* svalpamapi anupāyato'rjitamalaghu prabhūtamapi upāyato'rjitaṃ laghveva draṣṭavyam  || bhāṣya 1.9:276
* uktaṃ hi | carenmādhukarīṃ vṛttiṃ valmīkanicayopamām | akruddhaścāprahṛṣṭaśca tapastaddhi sanātanam  || bhāṣya 1.9:277
* yaścaretsarvabhojyeṣu bhaikṣyaṃ ca vyavahārataḥ | bhuñjīta pratigṛhṇīyātpraśastānāṃ svakarmasu  || bhāṣya 1.9:278
* cāturvarṇyaṃ caredbhaikṣyaṃ patitāṃstu vivarjayet | payaścāpaśca bhaikṣyaṃ ca samametanna saṃśayaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:279
* bhaikṣyaśeṣaṃ tu yo bhikṣuryadi kiṃcitsamutsṛjet | grāse grāse tu kartavyāḥ prāṇāyāmāstrayastrayaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:280
* saṃnidhānaṃ na kurvīta sarvāvastho'pi yogavit | saṃnidhānakṛtairdoṣairyatiḥ saṃjāyate kṛmiḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:281
* mādhukaramasaṃkalpaṃ prākpravṛttamayācitam | tattatkālopapannaṃ ca bhaikṣyaṃ pañcavidhaṃ smṛtam  || bhāṣya 1.9:282
* gṛhādgṛhaṃ paryaṭaṃstu na gṛhaṃ parivarjayet | parasya vacanaṃ śrutvā duṣṭaveśma vivarjayet || bhāṣya 1.9:283
* aduṣṭāpatitaṃ sādhuṃ bhikṣuko yo vyatikramet | sa tasya sukṛtaṃ dattvā duṣkṛtaṃ pratipadyate  || bhāṣya 1.9:284
* tathaiva ca gṛhasthasya nirāśo bhikṣuko vrajet | sa tasyeṣṭaṃ ca pūrtaṃ ca bhikṣurādāya gacchati  || bhāṣya 1.9:285
* akṛte vaiśvadeve tu bhikṣuke gṛhamāgate | uddhṛtya vaiśvadevārthaṃ bhikṣukaṃ tu visarjayet || bhāṣya 1.9:286
* vaiśvadevakṛtān doṣān śakto bhikṣurvyapohitum | nahi bhikṣukṛtān doṣān vaiśvadevo vyapohati  || bhāṣya 1.9:287
* daśāhaṃ dvādaśāhaṃ vā yatra bhikṣā na labhyate | tadgṛhaṃ varjayedbhikṣurūṣarāṇīva karṣakaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:288
* caturakṣarasaṃyuktāṃ bhikṣāṃ tu samudāharet | eṣa pravrajināṃ dharmaḥ śeṣastu krayavikrayaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:289
* na hasenna cābhiprekṣetbhikṣāmicchaṃstu bhikṣukaḥ | godohamātraṃ saṃtiṣṭhennopatiṣṭhetkadācana  || bhāṣya 1.9:290
* jarāmaraṇagarbhebhyo bhītasya narakādapi | bhayātkṣapayate yasmāttasmādbhaikṣyamiti smṛtam  || bhāṣya 1.9:291
* dadhibhakṣāḥ payobhakṣā ye'nye yāvakabhakṣiṇaḥ | sarve te bhaikṣyabhakṣasya kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm  || bhāṣya 1.9:292
* taptakāñcanavarṇena gavāṃ mūtreṇa yāvakam | pibeddvādaśa varṣāṇi na tadbhaikṣyasamaṃ bhavet || bhāṣya 1.9:293
* māsi māsi kuśāgreṇa yaḥ pibetsomamagrajaḥ | bhaikṣyaṃ cāvyavahāreṇa tulyaṃ bhavati vā na vā  || bhāṣya 1.9:294
* bhaikṣyamannaṃ paraṃ śreyo bhaikṣyamannaṃ paraṃ śuci | bhaikṣyaṃ hi vratināṃ śreṣṭhaṃ bhaikṣyameva parā gatiḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:295
* yadyajjalaṃ nirdhamaneṣvapeyaṃ nadīgataṃ tatpunareva peyam | tathānnapānaṃ vidhipūrvamāgataṃ dvijātipātrāntaritaṃ na duṣyati  || bhāṣya 1.9:296
* lavaṇamalavaṇaṃ vā snigdhamasnehikaṃ vā saharasavirasaṃ vā śuṣkamannaṃ dravaṃ vā | yadi iha niravadyaṃ bhuñjate bhaikṣyamannaṃ sa khalu bhavati bhikṣurbhikṣudharmādaluptaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:297
* tathotsṛṣṭaṃ yathālabdhaṃ ca tatraivāvasaraprāptatvātpratitantrasiddhāntasiddhaṃ sūtrato'rthanirdeśaṃ kariṣyāmaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:298
* ityevamāhāralāghavaṃ tantre siddham  || bhāṣya 1.9:299
* tathā apramādastantre siddhaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:300
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.9:301
* apramādopadeśātjapyopadeśācca  || bhāṣya 1.9:302
* iha nityaṃ yameṣvapramattenopasthitasmṛtinā bhavitavyam  || bhāṣya 1.9:303
* uktaṃ hi | apramādo damastyāgo brāhmaṇasya hayāḥ smṛtāḥ | śīlaraśmisamāyuktairdheyātmā mānase rathe  || bhāṣya 1.9:304
* taṃ brahmarathamāruhya garbhajanmajarāyutān | chindanmṛtyubhayān pāśān brahmabhūto'vatiṣṭhate  || bhāṣya 1.9:305
* ityevamapramādastantre siddhaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:306
* evaṃ prasiddhā yamā ahiṃsādayaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:307
* āha aviśeṣadoṣānna prasiddhā yamāḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:308
* ihānyeṣāmapyahiṃsādīni dharmasādhanāni  || bhāṣya 1.9:309
* ihāpi ca śāstre tānyeva  || bhāṣya 1.9:310
* tasmātsādhyasādhananiṣṭhāsvapyaviśeṣaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:311
* ucyate na  || bhāṣya 1.9:312
* atiprasaṅgādanekāntācca  || bhāṣya 1.9:313
* yadi dharmasādhanāstitvamātrasādharmyādahiṃsādīnāṃ tyāgaḥ kriyate tasmātkāryakāraṇakṣetrajñadharmādharmasukhaduḥkhasaṃsārapadārthādayo'pi tyājyāḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:314
* atha naivamanekāntaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:315
* kiṃca tyāge kṛtihiṃsādīnāṃ dharmasādhanatvaprasaṅgaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:316
* kiṃca atidānātiyajanātitapo'tigatyanāvṛttyādibhiḥ yamaniyamagarbhatvādvidheḥ siddham  || bhāṣya 1.9:317
* niyamaviśeṣaṇācca nāviśeṣaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.9:318
* tasmādyuktamuktaṃ prasiddhā yamā ahiṃsādaya iti  || bhāṣya 1.9:319
* ata etaduktaṃmahādevasya dakṣiṇāmūrteḥ iti  || bhāṣya 1.9:320
* atredaṃ yamaprakaraṇaṃ samāptam  || bhāṣya 1.9:321
* āha tasminnāyatane prativasataḥ kā mātrā  || bhāṣya 1.9:322
* sā vācyā gṛhasthādivat || bhāṣya 1.9:323
* taducyate na  || bhāṣya 1.9:324
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 1.9:325

**********************************************

ekavāsāḥ  || 1.10 ||

* atra ekamiti saṃkhyā  || bhāṣya 1.10:1
* vāsa ityācchādane bhavati  || bhāṣya 1.10:2
* tasya vāsaḥ pañcavidhamaṇḍajaṃ voḍajaṃ vālajaṃ valkalajaṃ carmajaṃ vā  || bhāṣya 1.10:3
* yatkuśalenābhyupāyenopapadyate tadekapaṭalamanekapaṭalaṃ vā grāmyādibhyo niṣparigrahaṃ kaupīnapracchādanamātraṃ lajjāpratīkārārthaṃ caikaṃ vāso grāhyam  || bhāṣya 1.10:4
* asyaiva ca sūtrasya sāmarthyātsarvadravyaparityāge kṛte ekavāsomātraparigrahaḥ saṃskartavyaḥ śiṣyaḥ | āha lajjāvinivṛttirasya kadā bhavatīti | ucyate jñānākaluṣābhyām | atra yadā prāptajñānaḥ kṣīṇakaluṣaśca bhavati tadā tasya lajjānivṛttiḥ  || bhāṣya 1.10:5
* āha kiṃ vinivṛttāyāmapi lajjāyāṃ niyatamevaikaṃ vāso grāhyamāhosvidaniyatamiti | ucyate aniyataṃ yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 1.10:6

**********************************************

avāsā vā  || 1.11 ||

* atra akāro vāsaḥpratiṣedhe vartate | avāsasā nagnena yathā jātena niṣparigraheṇa bhavitavyam | āha avāsastve kiṃ te prayojanam | tadvācyamekavāsastvavat | taducyate niṣparigrahārthamamaṅgalakhyāpanārthaṃ ceti prayojanadvayaṃ draṣṭavyam | vāśabdaḥ śaktyaśaktyorvicāraṇe | yadyaśaktastadā anagnenaikavāsasā bhāvyam | yadi śaktastadā avāsasā nagnena yathājātena niṣparigraheṇa bhavitavyamityarthaḥ | na tu vā vikalpe | vikalpārthāsaṃbhavādityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.11:1
* āha tasminnāyatane prativasatā kimā dehapātādanirgacchataiva stheyaṃ dhyānaikaniṣṭhena śilāvadāhosviddṛṣṭo'syāyatanānnirgamaḥ bhasmabhaikṣyodakārjanādinimittaṃ grāmādipraveśo vā | ucyate dṛṣṭaḥ | yasmāt || bhāṣya 1.11:2

**********************************************

mūtrapurīṣaṃ nāvekṣet || 1.12 ||

* atra mūtraṃ ca purīṣaṃ ca mūtrapurīṣam | cārthe dvaṃdvasamāsaḥ atra mūtraṃ nāma yadetadudaraparyuṣitaṃ niḥsarati bahiḥ sravati tanmūtram  || bhāṣya 1.12:1
* mocanānmūtram  || bhāṣya 1.12:2
* mūtratvābhisambandhāddhi mūtraṃ lokādiprasiddhamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.12:3
* purīṣaṃ nāma yadetatpītakhāditāvalīḍhānāmāhāraviśeṣāṇāmādhyātmikena agninā paripakvamapānena skhalati tatpurīṣam  || bhāṣya 1.12:4
* purānnirgatatvātpurīṣatvābhisambandhādvā purīṣaṃ lokādiprasiddhamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.12:5
* nakāro darśanapratiṣedhe  || bhāṣya 1.12:6
* na draṣṭavyamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.12:7
* ava iti apavarjanaṃ nāma pratiṣedhe jātigrahaṇendriyāntarapratiṣedhe cetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.12:8
* īkṣa darśane  || bhāṣya 1.12:9
* yadetannijaṃ buddhīndriyaṃ cakṣuranena cakṣuṣā anayā buddhyā manuṣyādīnāṃ mūtrapurīṣaṃ na draṣṭavyam  || bhāṣya 1.12:10
* na tu gavādīnāmityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.12:11
* āha kiṃ mūtrapurīṣasaṃdarśanamātramevāsya pratiṣidhyate  || bhāṣya 1.12:12
* ucyate na  || bhāṣya 1.12:13
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 1.12:14

**********************************************

strīśūdraṃ nābhibhāṣet || 1.13 ||

* atra strī ca śūdraśca strīśūdram  || bhāṣya 1.13:1
* cārthe dvaṃdvasamāsaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.13:2
* atra strī nāma seyaṃ lokaprasiddhā stanajaghanakeśavatī hāvabhāvavilāsayuktā puruṣabhāvasvabhāvikā divyā mānuṣā atiratirasā viṣayamūrtiriti kṛtvā pratiṣidhyate  || bhāṣya 1.13:3
* anubhāṣaṇapūrvikā cāsyāḥ prāptirbhaviṣyatītyataḥ strī nābhibhāṣitavyetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.13:4
* śūdro nāmāyaṃ lokādiprasiddhastrivarṇaparicārakaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.13:5
* śocanāddrohaṇācca śūdraḥ  || bhāṣya 1.13:6
* sa khalvadayāluriti kṛtvā pratiṣidhyate  || bhāṣya 1.13:7
* kimartham  || bhāṣya 1.13:8
* tenākruṣṭaścābhihataśca vā kruddhastadvadhārthaṃ pravartate ato jātijñānatapaḥśrutahānirbhavati  || bhāṣya 1.13:9
* sūcite cāvamānādyabhāve'vamānādyabhāvātsūcivṛddhayorabhāvaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.13:10
* akaluṣasūtre cāsya doṣanirdeśaṃ kariṣyāmaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.13:11
* nakāro bhāṣaṇapratiṣedhe  || bhāṣya 1.13:12
* nābhibhāṣitavyamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.13:13
* abhiśabdaḥ prasaṅga iti  || bhāṣya 1.13:14
* pratiṣedhe jātigrahaṇe cetarapratiṣedhe cetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.13:15
* bhāṣa vyaktāyāṃ vāci  || bhāṣya 1.13:16
* yadetatkarmendriyaṃ vāganayā vāṇyā iti  || bhāṣya 1.13:17
* ataḥ strīśūdraṃ nābhibhāṣitavyamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.13:18
* āha nāvekṣennābhibhāṣedityukte'tha kimanena sādhakenāndhamūkavadavasthātavyamiti  || bhāṣya 1.13:19
* ucyate na  || bhāṣya 1.13:20
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 1.13:21

**********************************************

yadyavekṣedyadyabhibhāṣet || 1.14 ||

* atra yadi yadi ityāśaṅkāyām  || bhāṣya 1.14:1
* nābhibhāṣediti vacanānniṣiddhe'pyarthe gurvarthamātmārthaṃ vā bhasmabhaikṣyodakārjanādinimittaṃ grāmādīn praviṣṭasya viṇmūtrayoḥ strīśūdrayośca darśanamabhibhāṣaṇaṃ ca bhaviṣyatīti kṛtvā  || bhāṣya 1.14:2
* ata etaduktaṃ sarvajñena bhagavatā yadyavekṣedyadyabhibhāṣediti  || bhāṣya 1.14:3
* avaśyaṃ bhavedityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.14:4
* āha dṛṣṭe cābhibhāṣite copahatena nirghātanaṃ kiṃ kartavyam  || bhāṣya 1.14:5
* taducyate upasparśanam  || bhāṣya 1.14:6
* yasmāt || bhāṣya 1.14:7

**********************************************

upaspṛśya  || 1.15 ||

* atra upa ityabhyupagame  || bhāṣya 1.15:1
* abhyupagamanena kaluṣamatinetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.15:2
* spṛśya iti bhasmadravyagātrasaṃyojanameva  || bhāṣya 1.15:3
* upaspṛśyeti snānaparyāyaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.15:4
* sacailodakasparśanavat || bhāṣya 1.15:5
* sa ca bhasmanā kartavyaḥ nādbhiḥ  || bhāṣya 1.15:6
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.15:7
* pūrvottaravyāghātāt || bhāṣya 1.15:8
* snānasyāprasaṅgācca  || bhāṣya 1.15:9
* upaspṛśyeti niṣṭhā  || bhāṣya 1.15:10
* āha upaspṛśya yadi kaluṣaṃ na kṣīṇaṃ syāttato nirghātanaṃ kiṃ kartavyam  || bhāṣya 1.15:11
* taducyate sākāṅkṣatvānniṣṭhāśabdasya prāṇāyāmaḥ kartavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.15:12
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 1.15:13

**********************************************

prāṇāyāmaṃ kṛtvā  || 1.16 ||

* atra prāṇo nāma ya eṣa mukhanāsikābhyāṃ niḥsarati vāyureṣa prāṇaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.16:1
* tasya āyāmo nigraho nirodhaḥ sa prāṇāyāmaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.16:2
* sa ca puruṣavṛttirdraṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.16:3
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.16:4
* jñānecchāprayatnapūrvakatvātprāṇāyāmasya ca  || bhāṣya 1.16:5
* ekoddhāto dviruddhāto vā  || bhāṣya 1.16:6
* tathā viṃśatimātraścaturviṃśatimātrastriṃśanmātro vā  || bhāṣya 1.16:7
* mātrā tvakṣinimeṣakālaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.16:8
* sa yathāśakti yathābalaṃ kartavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.16:9
* tasmādupaspṛśya padmakasvastikopasthāñjalikārdhacandrapīṭhakadaṇḍāyatasarvatobhadrādīnāmanyatamenāsanabandhena prāṅmukha udaṅmukho vā upaviśyaitānyaṅgāni kṛtvā grīvāmunnāmya pūraṇapūrvako vā recakapūrvako vā tāvatkartavyo yāvannigṛhītā vāyavo dhyānībhūtaśca bhavati  || bhāṣya 1.16:10
* tatra dhyānībhūto nāma yadā dantivadantaḥśarīraṃ pūrṇaṃ bhavati  || bhāṣya 1.16:11
* nigṛhītānāṃ tu lakṣaṇaṃ yadā kūrmavadantaḥśarīre ucchvāsapratyucchvāsā vartante svacchendriyaśca bhavati tadā mantavyā nigṛhītā vāyava iti  || bhāṣya 1.16:12
* tataḥ śanaiḥ śanairmoktavyā nāsikayā yathotpalapattramapi nāsāpuṭasthaṃ na kampayati  || bhāṣya 1.16:13
* tadatra praśnākrāntau krameṇākramitavyaḥ antarbhāve'ntare vāyavo bhāvayitavyāḥ  || bhāṣya 1.16:14
* ana prāṇane  || bhāṣya 1.16:15
* āṅiti āsanabandhanibhṛtanigṛhītakaluṣakṣapaṇavisargādimaryādāmadhikurute  || bhāṣya 1.16:16
* yamu bandhane  || bhāṣya 1.16:17
* bandhayitavyāḥ  || bhāṣya 1.16:18
* ḍukṛñkaraṇe draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.16:19
* tvā iti karmaniṣṭhāyām  || bhāṣya 1.16:20
* vicchedavadityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.16:21
* āha atha kṛte prāṇāyāme yadi kaluṣaṃ na kṣīṇaṃ syāttato'nena kiṃ kartavyam  || bhāṣya 1.16:22
* taducyate japyam  || bhāṣya 1.16:23
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 1.16:24

**********************************************

raudrīṃ gāyatrīṃ bahurūpīṃ vā japet || 1.17 ||

* atra tvāśabdasāmarthyādgamyate prāṇasaṃyamena samaṃ japyaṃ kartavyam  || bhāṣya 1.17:1
* upasparśanavat || bhāṣya 1.17:2
* tasmādatra raudrī nāma tatpuruṣā  || bhāṣya 1.17:3
* raudrī ca kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.17:4
* rudrasyopasthāpakatvādraudrī  || bhāṣya 1.17:5
* rudro vāsyāṃ cintyate rudraprāpakatvādvā raudrī  || bhāṣya 1.17:6
* āha sadyojātādibahuprakārā tatra kā sā raudrī  || bhāṣya 1.17:7
* taducyate gāyatrī  || bhāṣya 1.17:8
* atra yā raudrī sā gāyatrī  || bhāṣya 1.17:9
* gāyatrī ca kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.17:10
* gītā gātāraṃ trāyata iti  || bhāṣya 1.17:11
* gāyatre vā chandasi vartata iti gāyatrī  || bhāṣya 1.17:12
* atra raudrīgrahaṇādvaidikyādigāyatrīpratiṣedhaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.17:13
* iha tu gāyatrīgrahaṇātsadyojātādīnāṃ pratiṣedhaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.17:14
* gāyatrīmiti karma  || bhāṣya 1.17:15
* bahurūpī nāmāghorā  || bhāṣya 1.17:16
* bahurūpī ca kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.17:17
* bahurūpasyoktaparigraheṣvākāreṣu vartata iti bahurūpī  || bhāṣya 1.17:18
* bahurūpo vā asyāṃ cintyata iti bahurūpaprāpakatvādbahurūpī  || bhāṣya 1.17:19
* bahurūpīmiti karma  || bhāṣya 1.17:20
* vā iti vikalpe  || bhāṣya 1.17:21
* ubhayorapi brahmatvamubhayorapi tulyārthasādhakatvamubhe api maheśvaraparigṛhīte ityata ekāmanekāṃ vā upaspṛśya japediti mānasī kriyetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.17:22
* āha upasparśanaprāṇāyāmajapyādhikṛtasya kā kāryaniṣpattiḥ  || bhāṣya 1.17:23
* taducyate akaluṣatvam  || bhāṣya 1.17:24
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 1.17:25

**********************************************

akaluṣamateḥ  || 1.18 ||

* iti  || bhāṣya 1.18:1
* atra akaluṣā yasya matiḥ so'yamakaluṣamatiḥ  || bhāṣya 1.18:2
* bahuvrīhisamāsaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.18:3
* atrākāraḥ kaluṣapratiṣedhe  || bhāṣya 1.18:4
* bhāvakāluṣyamevātra kāluṣyam  || bhāṣya 1.18:5
* kathaṃ gamyate  || bhāṣya 1.18:6
* prāksiddhatvāt || bhāṣya 1.18:7
* iha ca purastāduktam  || bhāṣya 1.18:8
* nāvekṣennābhibhāṣedityukte arthāpannaṃ dṛṣṭe cābhibhāṣite ca dveṣecchākrodhā utpadyante  || bhāṣya 1.18:9
* te cotpannā matāvabhivyajyante  || bhāṣya 1.18:10
* kālatilakādidarśanavat || bhāṣya 1.18:11
* abhivyakteścocyate kaluṣito'haṃ vyāhato'haṃ malinīkṛto'hamiti  || bhāṣya 1.18:12
* ato dveṣecchākrodhanimittatvānmūtrapurīṣastrīśūdrapratiṣedhaḥ kriyate  || bhāṣya 1.18:13
* yadā tvete dveṣādayo bhāvā bījakṣaye sati notpadyante tadā paraṃ bhāvaśaucaṃ pratyavagantavyam  || bhāṣya 1.18:14
* kaluṣamateriti  || bhāṣya 1.18:15
* naimittikaṃ ca kaluṣam  || bhāṣya 1.18:16
* na ca nimittānityatvānnaimittikaṃ nityaṃ bhavati  || bhāṣya 1.18:17
* bījakṣaye'ṅkuravat || bhāṣya 1.18:18
* na ca yatraiva kaluṣamutpadyate tatraivopasparśanādīni kartavyāni  || bhāṣya 1.18:19
* āyatane tu kartavyāni  || bhāṣya 1.18:20
* athāpi kaluṣamutpannaṃ pradhvastaṃ syāttathāpi tadarthaṃ na kartavyāni  || bhāṣya 1.18:21
* yadā tu tadavasthitaṃ sambhavati śirorogādivattadā kartavyāni  || bhāṣya 1.18:22
* atra matiriti buddhirityanarthāntaram  || bhāṣya 1.18:23
* atrāpi karaṇavyapadeśenātmaśaucaṃ vyākhyāyata ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.18:24
* āha akaluṣamatinā sādhakena kiṃ kartavyam  || bhāṣya 1.18:25
* taducyate caritavyam  || bhāṣya 1.18:26
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 1.18:27

**********************************************

carataḥ  || 1.19 ||

* atra carataḥ iti dharmārjanamadhikurute  || bhāṣya 1.19:1
* bhaikṣyacaraṇavattapaścaritavyaṃ vihartavyaṃ tapaso'rjanaṃ kartavyaṃ na stheyamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.19:2
* carata iti vartamānakālaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.19:3
* akaluṣamateścarato vā asya kā kāryaniṣpattiḥ  || bhāṣya 1.19:4
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 1.19:5

**********************************************

tato'sya yogaḥ pravartate  || 1.20 ||

* atra tataḥ iti caryāpadeśe  || bhāṣya 1.20:1
* tataḥ caryābhiniveśādanantaraṃ tajjanyadharmādityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.20:2
* asya iti sādhakāpadeśe  || bhāṣya 1.20:3
* yo'yamakaluṣamatiścarati tasyetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.20:4
* āha kiṃ bhavatīti  || bhāṣya 1.20:5
* taducyate yogaḥ pravartate  || bhāṣya 1.20:6
* adhyayanadhyānādilakṣaṇaḥ kriyāyogaścarataḥ pravartata ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.20:7
* atrātmeśvarasaṃyogo yogaḥ pratyetavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.20:8
* pra iti ādikarmaṇi ārambhe bhavati  || bhāṣya 1.20:9
* yadā akaluṣamatiścarati tadā pravartata ityarthāt || bhāṣya 1.20:10
* tatra yataḥ pravartate  || bhāṣya 1.20:11
* viṣayebhyaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.20:12
* pratyāhṛtacittasya yatpravartate tadyogaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.20:13
* yathā pravartate  || bhāṣya 1.20:14
* kramaśaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.20:15
* yena pravartate  || bhāṣya 1.20:16
* tapasā pravartate  || bhāṣya 1.20:17
* yasya pravartate  || bhāṣya 1.20:18
* ātmanaḥ sādhakasya  || bhāṣya 1.20:19
* yasmin pravartate  || bhāṣya 1.20:20
* yo'yamātmanyātmabhāvaḥ sa maheśvare pravartata ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.20:21
* evaṃ yasmāddravyāvasthānakāladeśakriyāprayogoccāvacaprayojanayamaniyamavṛttivasatyarthaprāṇāyāmapratyāhāranimittapratiṣedhasaṃśayanirghātanaśaucaniyogaphalopāyāśca vyākhyātāḥ ato'trāyatanaprakaraṇaṃ samāptam  || bhāṣya 1.20:22
* atrāha kiṃ prayojananiṣṭhaṃ tantram  || bhāṣya 1.20:23
* ucyate na  || bhāṣya 1.20:24
* yoganiṣṭham  || bhāṣya 1.20:25
* yasmādāha yuktottare satyapi padārthavailakṣaṇye raṅgapatākādivacchiṣyapralobhanārthamidamārabhyate  || bhāṣya 1.20:26

**********************************************

darśanaśravaṇamananavijñānāni cāsya pravartante  || 1.21 ||

* atra dūraṃ nāma yadetaddarśanādyaṃ vikaraṇāntaṃ māheśvaramaiśvaryamanena kadācitprāptapūrvakaṃ tasmiṃstatprāptau ca  || bhāṣya 1.21:1
* darśanādiṣvādhikāriko'tra dūraśabdo draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.21:2
* tatprāptiśca yogapravṛttitaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.21:3
* āha yadyevaṃ sūtrato'bhidhīyantāṃ darśanādayaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.21:4
* taducyante  || bhāṣya 1.21:5
* darśanamityatrāpi ca nastrikaṃ cintyate  || bhāṣya 1.21:6
* draṣṭā darśanaṃ dṛśyamiti  || bhāṣya 1.21:7
* atra draṣṭā siddhaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.21:8
* darśanamasya siddhiḥ jñānam  || bhāṣya 1.21:9
* draṣṭavyāni rūpāṇi  || bhāṣya 1.21:10
* tatkṛtsneṣu viṣayeṣu samāsavistaravibhāgaviśeṣataśca darśanaṃ pravartata ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.21:11
* tathā śravaṇamityatrāpi nastrikaṃ cintyante  || bhāṣya 1.21:12
* śrotā śravaṇaṃ śravyamiti  || bhāṣya 1.21:13
* tatra śrotā siddhaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.21:14
* śravaṇamasya siddhirjñānam  || bhāṣya 1.21:15
* śravyāḥ śabdāḥ  || bhāṣya 1.21:16
* tadasya siddhasya śrāvyeṣvartheṣu samāsavistaravibhāgaviśeṣataśca śravaṇaṃ pravartata ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.21:17
* tathā mananamityatrāpi ca nastrikaṃ cintyate  || bhāṣya 1.21:18
* mantā mananaṃ mantavyamiti  || bhāṣya 1.21:19
* atra mantā siddhaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.21:20
* mananamasya siddhirjñānam  || bhāṣya 1.21:21
* mantavyāni paracittāni  || bhāṣya 1.21:22
* devamanuṣyatiryagyonīnāṃ dharmārthakāmamokṣacittānāṃ mantā bhavatītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.21:23
* tathā vijñānamityatrāpi nastrikaṃ cintyante  || bhāṣya 1.21:24
* vijñātā vijñānaṃ vijñeyamiti  || bhāṣya 1.21:25
* tatra vijñātā siddhaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.21:26
* vijñānamasya siddhirjñānam  || bhāṣya 1.21:27
* vijñeyā vṛttayaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.21:28
* asya siddhasya pravartante svataḥ prādurbhavantītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.21:29
* asya jñānamasti neti  || bhāṣya 1.21:30
* ucyate asti  || bhāṣya 1.21:31
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 1.21:32

**********************************************

sarvajñatā  || 1.22 ||

* atrokteṣu dṛśyaśravyādiṣu ca aśeṣeṣu siddheśvarapaśvādiṣu nirviśeṣavācī sarvaśabdo draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.22:1
* jñatā ityatrāpi ca nastrikaṃ cintyate  || bhāṣya 1.22:2
* jñātā jñānaṃ jñeyamiti  || bhāṣya 1.22:3
* tatra jñātā siddhaḥ jñānamasya siddhirjñānam  || bhāṣya 1.22:4
* jñeyaṃ kāryaṃ kāraṇaṃ siddhāśceti  || bhāṣya 1.22:5
* tasmādekā jñānaśaktiraparimitena jñeyenānekenānekadhopacaryate  || bhāṣya 1.22:6
* sphaṭikādityavaccāsya sarvataḥ pravartata ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.22:7
* āha kimayaṃ siddho jñānamātrasaṃtuṣṭaḥ paṅguvaduta kriyāśaktirapyasti neti  || bhāṣya 1.22:8
* ucyate asti  || bhāṣya 1.22:9
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 1.22:10

**********************************************

manojavitvam  || 1.23 ||

* atrāgantukatvātsarvajñānaśaktiruktā  || bhāṣya 1.23:1
* na tu ṛṣitvavipratvavadityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.23:2
* yasmātatra manojavavadityevaṃ prāpte samānopamānatvānmanojavitvamityuktam  || bhāṣya 1.23:3
* manojavavat || bhāṣya 1.23:4
* āha  || bhāṣya 1.23:5
* ko'rthaḥ sūtrasya  || bhāṣya 1.23:6
* ucyate yādṛṅmanaso javitvamāśukāritvamīdṛśamasya siddhasya kartṛtve śīghratvam  || bhāṣya 1.23:7
* na cāsya prajāpativattaponimittatvādbhāvottarā pravṛttiḥ  || bhāṣya 1.23:8
* kiṃtu bhāvasya balīyastvātpravṛtterutpannasvabhāvaḥ karomīti kṛtameva bhavati  || bhāṣya 1.23:9
* vināśayāmīti vinaṣṭaṃ vā  || bhāṣya 1.23:10
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.23:11
* dṛkkriyayorapratīghātatvāt || bhāṣya 1.23:12
* tvamiti bhāvanirdeśādgamyate vittamasya śaktiḥ sāmarthyam  || bhāṣya 1.23:13
* aiśvaryamīdṛśamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.23:14
* āha kimasya siddhasya kartavyaṃ karaṇaṃ kuto vā karoti  || bhāṣya 1.23:15
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 1.23:16

**********************************************

kāmarūpitvam  || 1.24 ||

* kāmarūpī ityatrāpi ca nastrikaṃ cintyate  || bhāṣya 1.24:1
* kāmī kāmaḥ kāmyamiti  || bhāṣya 1.24:2
* tatra kāmī siddhaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.24:3
* kāmo'syecchā  || bhāṣya 1.24:4
* kāmyāni rūpāṇi  || bhāṣya 1.24:5
* katham  || bhāṣya 1.24:6
* kamu icchāyāṃ bhavati  || bhāṣya 1.24:7
* rūpāṇi yāvanti yādṛśāni cecchati tāvanti tādṛśāni ca karoti  || bhāṣya 1.24:8
* ātmāyattāni cāsya rūpakaraṇāni pṛthivyādīni  || bhāṣya 1.24:9
* vibhutvācca karaṇānāṃ yatra yatra rūpāṇyabhinirvartayati tatra tatra cāsya buddhyādīnāṃ karaṇānāṃ vṛttilābho bhavati  || bhāṣya 1.24:10
* cakṣurādivaddṛṣṭāntāt || bhāṣya 1.24:11
* nādhiṣṭhātā iti cet || bhāṣya 1.24:12
* tacca na  || bhāṣya 1.24:13
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.24:14
* yasmādāha rūpīti  || bhāṣya 1.24:15
* atra rūpāṇyadhitiṣṭhatīti rūpī  || bhāṣya 1.24:16
* daṇḍivat || bhāṣya 1.24:17
* rūpivacanācca sarveṣāmeva rūpāṇāṃ yugapadevādhiṣṭhātā bhavati  || bhāṣya 1.24:18
* vibhutvādabhinno maheśvarāt || bhāṣya 1.24:19
* idaṃ ca rudrasāyujyanirdeśādgamyate  || bhāṣya 1.24:20
* tvamiti bhāvanirdeśādgamyate vittamasya śaktiḥ sāmarthyam  || bhāṣya 1.24:21
* aiśvaryamīdṛśamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.24:22
* āha parimiteṣu kṛtyeṣu aśaktidarśanātsaṃdehaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.24:23
* atha kimayaṃ siddhasteṣāṃ svakṛtānāṃ rūpāṇāṃ saṃhāre śaktaḥ uta viśvāmitravadaśaktaḥ iti  || bhāṣya 1.24:24
* ucyate  || bhāṣya 1.24:25
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 1.24:26

**********************************************

vikaraṇaḥ  || 1.25 ||

* atra viḥ vināśe vinākaraṇe  || bhāṣya 1.25:1
* vikaraṇo bhavati  || bhāṣya 1.25:2
* viśikhavirathavat || bhāṣya 1.25:3
* karaṇapratiṣedhātkāryapratiṣedhaḥ kṛto bhavati  || bhāṣya 1.25:4
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.25:5
* viśiṣṭatvādgrāhakatvātsūkṣmatvācca karaṇānām  || bhāṣya 1.25:6
* tasmādvikaraṇa iti kaivalyam  || bhāṣya 1.25:7
* āha aviśeṣādiha sāṃkhyayogādīnāmapi sahaiśvaryeṇa kāryakaraṇatyāgaṃ kṛtvā kaivalyaniṣṭhā  || bhāṣya 1.25:8
* ihāpi ca śāstre  || bhāṣya 1.25:9
* kathaṃ tasmādaviśeṣaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.25:10
* atha matiḥ niratiśaye mokṣe nāsti vaiṣamyaṃ tathāpyatidānādibhiḥ sādhyasādhananiṣṭhāto'tha viśeṣaḥ ucyate  || bhāṣya 1.25:11
* nāviśeṣaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.25:12
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 1.25:13

**********************************************

dharmitvaṃ ca  || 1.26 ||

* atra guṇadharmeṇāyaṃ dharmī bhavati  || bhāṣya 1.26:1
* yadetaddarśanādyaṃ vikaraṇāntaṃ māheśvaramaiśvaryamasyeśaprasādātsvaguṇasaṃvṛttaṃ tenāyaṃ guṇadharmeṇa dharmī bhavati  || bhāṣya 1.26:2
* kutaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.26:3
* tvamiti bhāvanirdeśādgamyate  || bhāṣya 1.26:4
* ṛte'pi kāryakaraṇe jñātā kartā ca bhavati  || bhāṣya 1.26:5
* tataśca kaivalyādyāḥ sarvaniṣṭhā viśeṣitā bhavanti  || bhāṣya 1.26:6
* caśabdo'tra jñānakriyāśaktisamāropaṇārthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.26:7
* evamatrāsya siddhasya kāmarūpivikaraṇavacanātsvakṛteṣu rūpeṣu prabhutvaṃ vibhutvaṃ guṇadharmitvaṃ ca vyākhyātam  || bhāṣya 1.26:8
* etadyuktottare prasādādguṇāḥ pravartanta ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.26:9
* atredamādhikārikamaiśvaryaprakaraṇaṃ parisamāptamiti  || bhāṣya 1.26:10
* āha kiṃ parakṛteṣvapi devamanuṣyatiryagyonirūpeṣvasya siddhasya prabhutvaṃ vibhutvaṃ cāsti neti  || bhāṣya 1.26:11
* ucyate asti  || bhāṣya 1.26:12
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 1.26:13

**********************************************

sarve cāsya vaśyā bhavanti  || 1.27 ||

* atra sarve niravaśeṣāḥ paśudharmāṇa ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.27:1
* caśabdaḥ svakṛtaparakṛtarūpasamuccayārthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.27:2
* parakṛteṣvapi devādirūpeṣu prabhutvaṃ vibhutvaṃ cāstīti  || bhāṣya 1.27:3
* asya iti siddhasyetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.27:4
* vaśyāḥ vidheyāḥ  || bhāṣya 1.27:5
* vaśavartinaśca bhavantītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.27:6
* bhavanti iti bhūtārthavādo niḥsaṃśayam  || bhāṣya 1.27:7
* āha kimayaṃ siddhasteṣāṃ kadācidvaśyo bhavati neti  || bhāṣya 1.27:8
* ucyate na  || bhāṣya 1.27:9
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 1.27:10

**********************************************

sarveṣāṃ cāvaśyo bhavati  || 1.28 ||

* atrāpi sarvaśabdaḥ paśuṣveva  || bhāṣya 1.28:1
* sarveṣāmiti nyūnaparigrahe  || bhāṣya 1.28:2
* caśabdo'bhyadhikatve  || bhāṣya 1.28:3
* abhyadhika utkṛṣṭo vyatiriktaśca bhavatītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.28:4
* avaśya iti  || bhāṣya 1.28:5
* akāro bhūtapūrvaṃ vaśyatvaṃ pratiṣedhayati  || bhāṣya 1.28:6
* bhavati iti bhūtārthavādo niḥsaṃśayam  || bhāṣya 1.28:7
* yadā guṇairyuktaḥ prāptaiśvaryaḥ siddhastadā sarveṣāṃ śakteravaśyo bhavatītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.28:8
* āha kiṃ svaśaktyādhyākrāntā vaśyā bhavanti āhosviddharmamaryādāṃ rakṣanti guruśiṣyavat || bhāṣya 1.28:9
* guroḥ śaktaḥ śiṣyo nādhyākrāntaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.28:10
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 1.28:11

**********************************************

sarvāṃścāviśati  || 1.29 ||

* atrāpi sarvaśabdaḥ paśuṣveva niravaśeṣavācī draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.29:1
* caśabdaḥ pūrvoktasamuccaye  || bhāṣya 1.29:2
* na kevalamasya te vaśyāḥ kiṃ tvāveśyāśceti  || bhāṣya 1.29:3
* atra āṅiti āveśanamaryādāmadhikurute  || bhāṣya 1.29:4
* viśa praveśane  || bhāṣya 1.29:5
* sa tasya jñānakriyayorvibhutve'pi śaktisaṃyogādāviśya pratyayalopaṃ kartuṃ samartho bhavatītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.29:6
* āha kimayaṃ siddhasteṣāṃ kadācidāveśyo bhavati neti  || bhāṣya 1.29:7
* ucyate na  || bhāṣya 1.29:8
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 1.29:9

**********************************************

sarveṣāṃ cānāveśyo bhavati  || 1.30 ||

* atrāpi sarvaśabdaḥ paśuṣveva  || bhāṣya 1.30:1
* sarveṣāmiti nyūnaparigrahe  || bhāṣya 1.30:2
* caśabdo'bhyadhikatve  || bhāṣya 1.30:3
* abhyadhika utkṛṣṭo vyatiriktaśca bhavatītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.30:4
* anāveśya iti  || bhāṣya 1.30:5
* akārā bhūtapūrvamāveśyatvaṃ pratiṣedhayati  || bhāṣya 1.30:6
* anāveśyadharmā bhavati  || bhāṣya 1.30:7
* na vyādhiśeṣavadavasthānaṃ bhavati iti bhūtārthavādo niḥsaṃśayam  || bhāṣya 1.30:8
* yadā guṇairyuktaḥ prāptaiśvaryaḥ siddhastadā sarveṣāṃ cānāveśyo bhavatītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.30:9
* āha kimāveśanamātra eva śakto yakṣarakṣaḥpiśācādivaduta prāṇairapi viprayogaṃ yātanābhiśca saṃyogaṃ kartuṃ śakto bhavatīti  || bhāṣya 1.30:10
* ucyate śaktaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.30:11
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 1.30:12

**********************************************

sarve cāsya vadhyā bhavanti  || 1.31 ||

* atrāpi sarvaśabdaḥ paśuṣveva niravaśeṣavācī draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.31:1
* caśabdaḥ samuccaye  || bhāṣya 1.31:2
* na kevalamasya te vaśyāḥ āveśyāśca kiṃtu vadhyāśceti  || bhāṣya 1.31:3
* asya iti siddhāpadeśe  || bhāṣya 1.31:4
* vadhyā iti  || bhāṣya 1.31:5
* vadha prāṇaviprayoge yātanāyāṃ ca  || bhāṣya 1.31:6
* prāṇairapi viprayogaṃ yātanābhiśca saṃyogaṃ kartuṃ samartho bhavatītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.31:7
* bhavanti iti bhūtārthavādo niḥsaṃśaye  || bhāṣya 1.31:8
* yadā guṇairyuktaḥ prāptaiśvaryaḥ siddhastadā sarve cāsya vadhyā bhavantītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.31:9
* āha kimayaṃ siddhasteṣāṃ kadācidvadhyo bhavati neti  || bhāṣya 1.31:10
* ucyate na  || bhāṣya 1.31:11
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 1.31:12

**********************************************

sarveṣāṃ cāvadhyo bhavati  || 1.32 ||

* atrāpi sarvaśabdaḥ paśuṣveva  || bhāṣya 1.32:1
* sarveṣāmiti nyūnaparigrahe  || bhāṣya 1.32:2
* caśabdo'bhyadhikatve  || bhāṣya 1.32:3
* abhyadhikaḥ utkṛṣṭo vyatiriktaśca bhavatītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.32:4
* avadhya iti  || bhāṣya 1.32:5
* akāro bhūtapūrvaṃ vadhyatvaṃ pratiṣedhayati  || bhāṣya 1.32:6
* yadā guṇairyuktaḥ prāptaiśvaryaḥ siddhastadā sarveṣāṃ cāvadhyo bhavatītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.32:7
* evaṃ parakṛteṣvapi devādiśarīreṣu rūpeṣu prabhutvaṃ vibhutvaṃ ca vyākhyātam  || bhāṣya 1.32:8
* atredaṃ ṣaṭsūtrīprakaraṇaṃ parisamāptam  || bhāṣya 1.32:9
* āha kimasya siddhasyaitadaiśvaryaṃ nityamāhosvitpārthivāpyataijasavāyavyavyomamānasāhaṃkārikamahadātmakādivadanityamiti  || bhāṣya 1.32:10
* ucyate nityam  || bhāṣya 1.32:11
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 1.32:12

**********************************************

abhītaḥ  || 1.33 ||

* atra akṣayādivacanavirodhādadhītaścaratīti pāṭhānupapattiḥ  || bhāṣya 1.33:1
* tasmādatītānāgatavartamānakālabhayaṃ na vidyata ityato'bhītaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.33:2
* āha abhītānāmapi brahmādīnāṃ saṃhāre kṣayaḥ śrūyate  || bhāṣya 1.33:3
* tasmānnābhītatvātnityam  || bhāṣya 1.33:4
* abhītasya vā kiṃ lakṣaṇam  || bhāṣya 1.33:5
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 1.33:6

**********************************************

akṣayaḥ  || 1.34 ||

* atra akāraḥ kṣayapratiṣedhe  || bhāṣya 1.34:1
* atra kṣayo nāma sati puruṣanityatve pūrvamasya brāhmaṇasya taistairaiśvaryairapakarṣaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.34:2
* āhaṃkārikamahadātmakādibhiranityo yogaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.34:3
* ayaṃ tu anena nityena māheśvareṇaiśvaryeṇa yogātpuruṣaḥ akṣayaḥ ityupacaryate  || bhāṣya 1.34:4
* rājakośavatkuṭumbidravyavat || bhāṣya 1.34:5
* āha īśvarāṇāmapi yayātiprabhṛtīnāṃ jarābhibhavanādatha kimayaṃ jīryate neti  || bhāṣya 1.34:6
* akṣayasya vā kiṃ lakṣaṇam  || bhāṣya 1.34:7
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 1.34:8

**********************************************

ajaraḥ  || 1.35 ||

* atra akāro jarāṃ pratiṣedhati  || bhāṣya 1.35:1
* atra jarā nāma palitaskhalityādilakṣaṇā kāryasya dṛkkriyāśaktihāniśca karaṇānām  || bhāṣya 1.35:2
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.35:3
* tatphalabhoktṛtvādayaṃ jīryata ityupacaryate  || bhāṣya 1.35:4
* idānīṃ tu kāmitvādvikaraṇadharmitvācca nāstītyataḥ ajara ityucyate  || bhāṣya 1.35:5
* āha ajarāṇāmapi devādīnāṃ saṃhārādarvāṅmṛtyurdṛśyate  || bhāṣya 1.35:6
* atha kimasya mṛtyurvidyate neti  || bhāṣya 1.35:7
* ajarasya vā kiṃ lakṣaṇam  || bhāṣya 1.35:8
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 1.35:9

**********************************************

amaraḥ  || 1.36 ||

* atra akāro mṛtyupratiṣedhe  || bhāṣya 1.36:1
* mṛṅprāṇatyāge  || bhāṣya 1.36:2
* atra prāṇādivṛttinirodho mṛtyurityucyate  || bhāṣya 1.36:3
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.36:4
* tatphalabhoktṛtvāt || bhāṣya 1.36:5
* so'sya kāmitvādvikaraṇadharmitvācca nāstītyataḥ  || bhāṣya 1.36:6
* amara ityucyate  || bhāṣya 1.36:7
* tasmādabhītākṣayādivacanānnityamaiśvaryamiti siddham  || bhāṣya 1.36:8
* āha  || bhāṣya 1.36:9

**********************************************

sarvatra cāpratihatagatirbhavati  || 1.37 ||

* sarvatra abhipretārtheṣu pravartamānasya maheśvareṇāpi apratibandhadharmitvamapratīghātaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.37:1

**********************************************

ityetairguṇairyukto bhagavato mahādevasya mahāgaṇapatirbhavati  || 1.38 ||

* ityetaiḥ pūrvoktaiḥ avaśyatvānāveśyatvāvadhyatvābhītatvākṣayatvājaratvāmaratvāpratīghātatvākhyaiḥ aṣṭabhirguṇaiḥ siddhilakṣaṇairyukto bhagavato mahādevasya mahāgaṇapatirbhavati  || bhāṣya 1.38:1
* sarvapaśubhyo'bhyadhikatvamaiśvaryātiśayānmahattvam  || bhāṣya 1.38:2
* gaṇāḥ nandimahākālādayaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.38:3
* sarvapaśvādikāryasvāmitvaṃ patitvam  || bhāṣya 1.38:4
* evamadhyāyaparisamāptiṃ kṛtvā yuktaṃ vaktum  || bhāṣya 1.38:5

**********************************************

atredaṃ brahma japet || 1.39 ||

* bṛhacca tebhyaḥ sarvabrahmabhyaḥ svayaṃ bhavati  || bhāṣya 1.39:1
* japediti ca mānasakriyā  || bhāṣya 1.39:2
* japyaṃ pratyavagantavyam  || bhāṣya 1.39:3
* uktamāha japayajñastu yajñānāṃ viśiṣṭo daśabhirguṇaiḥ  || bhāṣya 1.39:4
* upāṃśu syācchataguṇaḥ sāhasro mānasaḥ smṛtaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.39:5
* ato manasaiva japtavyam  || bhāṣya 1.39:6
* kimarthamiti cet || bhāṣya 1.39:7
* taducyate adharmavyucchittyarthaṃ dharmasya cābhivṛddhyarthaṃ tasya cākuśalebhyo vyāvartanārthaṃ brahmaṇyanavaratapadapaṅktyāmupanibandhanārthaṃ cetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.39:8
* āha kiṃ punastadbrahmeti  || bhāṣya 1.39:9
* atrocyate sadyojātādyam  || bhāṣya 1.39:10
* athavātra brahmādhyāyayordūrasthaḥ sambandhaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.39:11
* katham  || bhāṣya 1.39:12
* paśupaterityukte saṃdehaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.39:13
* kiṃnarapatisurapatiprajāpatiprabhṛtivadasyaiśvaryaṃ kṛtamanityamāgantukaṃ vā  || bhāṣya 1.39:14
* kiṃ cāsya janma mṛtyurvā vidyate neti  || bhāṣya 1.39:15
* ucyate na  || bhāṣya 1.39:16
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 1.39:17

**********************************************

sadyo'jātaṃ prapadyāmi  || 1.40 ||

* atra sadyaḥ ityasmin pade'rthadvayaṃ cintyate  || bhāṣya 1.40:1
* saṃśca ādyaśca  || bhāṣya 1.40:2
* āptipālanavat || bhāṣya 1.40:3
* atra saditi nityatve  || bhāṣya 1.40:4
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.40:5
* vināśahetvabhāvāt || bhāṣya 1.40:6
* nityaṃ dhruvamavināśi patyuḥ patitvaṃ nānyeṣāmityato'bhidhīyate saditi  || bhāṣya 1.40:7
* āha kimayamādimattve sati nityo mokṣavat || bhāṣya 1.40:8
* ucyate na  || bhāṣya 1.40:9
* yasmādāha ādyaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.40:10
* tadvyatiriktasya  || bhāṣya 1.40:11
* hetorasaṃbhavādādyamanāgantukaṃ patyuḥ patitvaṃ nānyeṣāmityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.40:12
* āha kiṃ nityānāditve sati puruṣavajjāyate  || bhāṣya 1.40:13
* ucyate na  || bhāṣya 1.40:14
* yasmādāha ajātaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.40:15
* atra akāro janmamṛtyupratiṣedhe  || bhāṣya 1.40:16
* janmamṛtyurahito bhagavānnirañjanaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.40:17
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.40:18
* sāñjanavṛttyalābhāt || bhāṣya 1.40:19
* nirabhimānitvaṃ nānyeṣāmityataḥ  || bhāṣya 1.40:20
* ajātamiti karma  || bhāṣya 1.40:21
* āha athaitatsattvamādyatvamajātatvaṃ ca guṇaṃ kāraṇe jñātvā sādhakena kiṃ kartavyam  || bhāṣya 1.40:22
* taducyate prapattavyam  || bhāṣya 1.40:23
* yasmādāha prapadyāmi  || bhāṣya 1.40:24
* iti sādhakāpadeśaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.40:25
* yathā agne vratapate vrataṃ cariṣyāmi iti  || bhāṣya 1.40:26
* atra praśabdaḥ kāraṇāntareṣu sattvādyatvājātatvapratiṣedhārtho bhṛśārthaśca  || bhāṣya 1.40:27
* tasmātsarvabhāvānabhiṣvaṅgeṇa tadeva kāraṇaṃ prapattavyam  || bhāṣya 1.40:28
* śaraṇamabhyupagantavyamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.40:29
* āha atra prapannaḥ kiṃ kariṣyati  || bhāṣya 1.40:30
* kiṃ vā dāsyati  || bhāṣya 1.40:31
* taducyate pūjāṃ kariṣyatyātmānaṃ ca dāsyati  || bhāṣya 1.40:32
* yathāha  || bhāṣya 1.40:33

**********************************************

sadyo'jātāya vai namaḥ  || 1.41 ||

* āha sattvamādyatvamajātatvaṃ ca pūrvoktam  || bhāṣya 1.41:1
* sadyo'jātāya iti caturthī  || bhāṣya 1.41:2
* vaiśabdaḥ sambhāvane  || bhāṣya 1.41:3
* sattvamādyatvamajātatvaṃ ca dharmān saṃbhāvya bravīti sadyo'jātāya vai namaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.41:4
* nama ityātmapradāne pūjāyāṃ ca  || bhāṣya 1.41:5
* namaskāreṇātmānaṃ prayacchati pūjāṃ ca prayuṅkta ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.41:6
* āha kiṃ prayojanamātmānaṃ maheśvarāya prayacchati  || bhāṣya 1.41:7
* kimasya duḥkhaṃ vā  || bhāṣya 1.41:8
* kiṃ vā maheśvarānmṛgayate  || bhāṣya 1.41:9
* kiṃ vā svayamutpāditānugṛhītatirobhāvitānāṃ paśūnāṃ patiḥ uta parairiti  || bhāṣya 1.41:10
* ucyate svayam  || bhāṣya 1.41:11
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 1.41:12

**********************************************

bhave bhave nātibhave  || 1.42 ||

* atra bhave bhave iti vīpsā  || bhāṣya 1.42:1
* bhava iti vidyākalāpaśūnāṃ samastānāṃ grahaṇam  || bhāṣya 1.42:2
* bhavaḥ kasmāt || bhāṣya 1.42:3
* bhavanabhāvanakṛtatvāt || bhāṣya 1.42:4
* yasmāddevamanuṣyatiryaktvena bhāvayati ca tānīśvaraḥ  || bhāṣya 1.42:5
* dharmajñānavairāgyaiśvaryādharmājñānāvairāgyānaiśvaryāṇāṃ bhavanabhāvanatvādbhavaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.42:6
* tasya bhūyobhūya utpattyanugrahatirobhāvaṃ ca dṛṣṭvā  || bhāṣya 1.42:7
* vīpsāyāḥ utpattāvutpattāvanugrahe'nugrahe tirobhāve tirobhāve cetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.42:8
* nātibhave iti  || bhāṣya 1.42:9
* nakāraḥ kāryatvaṃ pratiṣedhayati  || bhāṣya 1.42:10
* atiśayitabhaveṣu mā bhavāmītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.42:11
* āha kiṃ bhavādviyogamātramevaikaṃ mṛgayate  || bhāṣya 1.42:12
* taducyate na  || bhāṣya 1.42:13
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 1.42:14

**********************************************

bhajasva mām  || 1.43 ||

* atra bhaja ityanugrahe  || bhāṣya 1.43:1
* sva iti kāraṇāpadeśe  || bhāṣya 1.43:2
* māmityātmāpadeśe  || bhāṣya 1.43:3
* bhajasva māṃ trāyasva māmanugṛhṇīṣva māmityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 1.43:4
* āha kamāmantrayate  || bhāṣya 1.43:5
* kaṃ prapadyate  || bhāṣya 1.43:6
* kasmai namaskāraṃ karoti  || bhāṣya 1.43:7
* kastaṃ puruṣaṃ bhavānmokṣayati  || bhāṣya 1.43:8
* kaṃ vā bravīti bhajasva māmiti  || bhāṣya 1.43:9
* taducyate yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 1.43:10

**********************************************

bhavodbhavaḥ  || 1.44 ||

* atra bhava iti vidyākalāpaśūnāmeva grahaṇam  || bhāṣya 1.44:1
* tasyotpattikartā bhagavānityato bhavodbhava iti  || bhāṣya 1.44:2
* atrotpādakānugrāhakatirobhāvakadharmi kāraṇamutpādyānugrāhyatirobhāvyadharmi kāryamityetatkāryakāraṇayorlakṣaṇam  || bhāṣya 1.44:3
* etasmin kāraṇe prapattyādi kramopayogi draṣṭavyam  || bhāṣya 1.44:4
* evamatra bhagavatkauṇḍinyakṛte pañcārthabhāṣye prathamo'dhyāyaḥ saha brahmaṇā granthato'rthataśca parisamāpta iti  || bhāṣya 1.44:5

**********************************************


ড়āśupatasūtra, 2

vāmaḥ  || 2.1 ||

* nāmabhiḥ śreṣṭha ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.1:1
* uktaṃ hi | puruṣadhvajaśṛṅgeṣu havirbhūṣaṇalakṣmasu | vāmaḥ śreṣṭheṣvavakreṣu navasvartheṣu kīrtitaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.1:2
* āha kiṃnimittāsyotpādakādipravṛttiḥ kiṃprayojanā vā  || bhāṣya 2.1:3
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 2.1:4

**********************************************

devasya  || 2.2 ||

* iti  || bhāṣya 2.2:1
* atra deva iti divu krīḍāyām  || bhāṣya 2.2:2
* krīḍādharmitvātkrīḍānimittā  || bhāṣya 2.2:3
* krīḍāvāneva sa bhagavān vidyākalāpaśusaṃjñakaṃ trividhamapi kāryamutpādayananugṛhṇāti tirobhāvayati cetyato devaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.2:4
* pravṛttiścotpattyādiphalā draṣṭavyā  || bhāṣya 2.2:5
* devasya iti taddharmitve ṣaṣṭhī  || bhāṣya 2.2:6
* āha kiṃ nāmadvayamevātra kāraṇe vitanyate arthadvayameva vā āhosvidanyadapyasti neti  || bhāṣya 2.2:7
* ucyate'sti  || bhāṣya 2.2:8
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 2.2:9

**********************************************

jyeṣṭhasya  || 2.3 ||

* iti  || bhāṣya 2.3:1
* atra paratvājjyeṣṭhaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.3:2
* keṣāṃ kena vā paraḥ  || bhāṣya 2.3:3
* taducyate siddhasādhakapaśūnām  || bhāṣya 2.3:4
* tadāyattatvātsiddhasādhakabhāvasya sarvapaśūnāṃ ca pravṛttinivṛttisthityādiphalānāmityato jyeṣṭhaḥ parataraḥ  || bhāṣya 2.3:5
* paratamaśceti  || bhāṣya 2.3:6
* akṛtakaṃ cāsyaiśvaryam  || bhāṣya 2.3:7
* uktaṃ hi | dṛkkriyālakṣaṇā śaktistattvadharmo'sya nityatā | śreṣṭho'taḥ sarvabhūteṣu tasmādeṣa paraḥ smṛtaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.3:8
* ityevaṃ paratvājjyeṣṭhaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.3:9
* atrāpi jyeṣṭhasyeti taddharmitve ṣaṣṭhī  || bhāṣya 2.3:10
