* āha kiṃ nāmatrayamevātra kāraṇe cintyate arthatrayameva vā āhosvidanyadapyasti neti  || bhāṣya 2.3:11
* ucyate'sti  || bhāṣya 2.3:12

**********************************************

rudrasya  || 2.4 ||

* iti  || bhāṣya 2.4:1
* atra rutasya bhayasya drāvaṇātsaṃyojanādrudraḥ tatra rutamabhilāpa ityanarthāntaram  || bhāṣya 2.4:2
* drāvaṇaṃ nāma  || bhāṣya 2.4:3
* bhayaṃ vividham  || bhāṣya 2.4:4
* uktaṃ hi | nānāvidhaiḥ kṛtairyasmādbhayaiśca vividhaistathā | saṃyojayati bhūtāni tasmādrudra iti smṛtaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.4:5
* atrāpi taddharmitve ṣaṣṭhī  || bhāṣya 2.4:6
* āha kiṃ tat || bhāṣya 2.4:7
* kīdṛśaṃ vā tadasyeti  || bhāṣya 2.4:8
* ucyate  || bhāṣya 2.4:9

**********************************************

kalitāsanam  || 2.5 ||

* atra trividhena kāryeṇa vidyākalāpaśusaṃjñakena tatraiva sthityutpattipralayān prāpnuvatā kalitaṃ śobhitaśabditaṃ nabhastārābhirivetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.5:1
* āha yadetatpatyuḥ patitvaṃ śaktiḥ sāmarthyamaiśvaryaṃ svaguṇaḥ sadbhāvaḥ satattvaṃ tattvadharmaḥ tadāsanam  || bhāṣya 2.5:2
* natu padmāsanavadupaveśanalakṣaṇamityarthaḥ āsanaṃ kasmāt || bhāṣya 2.5:3
* āste'smināsanam  || bhāṣya 2.5:4
* kāryamanena vā adhyāsta ityāsanamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.5:5
* ata āsanam  || bhāṣya 2.5:6
* ato'vyayo'mṛto bhagavān kāmataḥ svaśaktisthaṃ kāryaṃ svaśaktyā adhyāste  || bhāṣya 2.5:7
* tasmādāsanasthaṃ kāryaṃ kāraṇaṃ ceti  || bhāṣya 2.5:8
* āha kāryakāraṇayorvṛttisaṃkaradoṣo gojāvimahiṣīkṣīravat || bhāṣya 2.5:9
* taduta na  || bhāṣya 2.5:10
* aṅgulyagrarūpādivadityasaṃkaraḥ  || bhāṣya 2.5:11
* dīpādityaprakāśanayanaraśmivaccāsaṃkaraḥ  || bhāṣya 2.5:12
* āha saṃkare aparicchedadoṣaḥ īśvarapuruṣavidyākalānāṃ mākṣikakoṭadravyavat || bhāṣya 2.5:13
* taducyate ekottarotkarṣeṇa vyāpyavyāpakabhāvenāvasthitānāṃ tattvādīnāṃ nāparicchedadoṣaḥ sūtratvādvyāpakaṃ maheśvaratattvaṃ vyāpyaṃ puruṣādipañcaviṃśakam  || bhāṣya 2.5:14
* tathā ātmatvādvyāpakaṃ puruṣatattvaṃ vyāpyaṃ pradhānādicaturviṃśakam  || bhāṣya 2.5:15
* tathā vyāpakaṃ pradhānaṃ vyāpyaṃ buddhyāditrayoviṃśakam  || bhāṣya 2.5:16
* vyāpikā bhavati buddhiḥ vyāpyamahaṃkārādidvāviṃśakam  || bhāṣya 2.5:17
* tathā vyāpako bhavatyahaṃkāraḥ vyāpyānyekādaśendriyāṇi daśavidhaṃ ca kāryam  || bhāṣya 2.5:18
* vyāpakānyekādaśendriyāṇi vyāpyāni pañcabhūtasūkṣmāṇi śabdādīni  || bhāṣya 2.5:19
* tathā vyāpakāni pañcabhūtasūkṣmāṇi śabdādīni vyāpyāni ākāśādīni pañcamahābhūtāni  || bhāṣya 2.5:20
* tathā vyāpakamākāśaṃ vyāpyaṃ vāyvādibhūtacatuṣkam  || bhāṣya 2.5:21
* tathā vyāpako bhavati vāyuḥ vyāpyaṃ tejaḥprabhṛti bhūtatrayam  || bhāṣya 2.5:22
* tathā vyāpakaṃ bhavati tejaḥ vyāpyamabādidvayam  || bhāṣya 2.5:23
* tathā vyāpikā bhavantyāpaḥ vyāpyā pṛthivī  || bhāṣya 2.5:24
* vyāpikā pṛthivī vyāpyāni bhūmyudakarasalakṣaṇāni kāryāṇi  || bhāṣya 2.5:25
* tathā vyāpakāni bhūmyudakarasalakṣaṇāni kāraṇāni vyāpyaṃ devamanuṣyatiryagyoni tṛṇauṣadhivṛkṣagulmalatāvanaspatyādikāryamanekavidhamato nāparicchedadoṣaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.5:26
* āha vṛttyasaṃkaragrahaṇe dṛṣṭāntābhāvādayuktaṃ taducyate haridrodakavadvyāpyaṃ vyāpakaṃ ca tadyathā haridrodake snigdhatvaśaityādidharmairapāṃ grahaṇaṃ gandhavarṇaghanakṣāratvādibhirharidrāyāḥ  || bhāṣya 2.5:27
* tathārṇavadevādisthānaśarīrendriyaviṣayādisaṃniveśena sukhaduḥkhasaṃnipātena ceśvarasya  || bhāṣya 2.5:28
* evaṃ puruṣasyāpi  || bhāṣya 2.5:29
* īśvarasyāpi apariṇāmidharmitvātsukhaduḥkhadātṛtvācca pradhānadharmādharmādīnāṃ grahaṇam  || bhāṣya 2.5:30
* tathā pradhānasya mānasasaṃkalpālocanagamanādibhiḥ karaṇagrāmasya viṣayāṇāṃ ca grahaṇam  || bhāṣya 2.5:31
* tathā dhṛtisaṃgrahapaktivyūhāvakāśadānādibhirdharmaiḥ pṛthivyādīnāṃ grahaṇamiti pradhānavādino manyante  || bhāṣya 2.5:32
* āha atheha tantre kathaṃ kāryakāraṇāvasthānam  || bhāṣya 2.5:33
* tadvācyam  || bhāṣya 2.5:34
* taducyate iha yasmādāha śarvasarvebhyaḥ iti vacanādyathāsambhavam  || bhāṣya 2.5:35
* sati vibhutve svavṛttyā kāryakāraṇayoḥ sarvagatatve'pi svavṛttyasaṃkaraḥ tasmādāsanasthaṃ kāryaṃ kāraṇaṃ ceti  || bhāṣya 2.5:36
* āha kiṃ tadāsanasthaṃ kāryamāsane nityamāhosvidanityamiti  || bhāṣya 2.5:37
* ucyate nityaṃ kāryam  || bhāṣya 2.5:38
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 2.5:39
* patyurbhavati  || bhāṣya 2.5:40
* kāraṇeśvaranityatvātpatinityatvam  || bhāṣya 2.5:41
* iha sadyojātādivacanātpālako nityaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.5:42
* pālakanityatvācca pālyamapi nityam  || bhāṣya 2.5:43
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 2.5:44
* na hyasati pālye pālaka ityeva  || bhāṣya 2.5:45
* sati nityatve tānyeva paśvādīni saṃyojayati  || bhāṣya 2.5:46
* mṛllohamayaprākārādivaddṛṣṭāntāt || bhāṣya 2.5:47
* vṛttilābhaścotpattirityucyate  || bhāṣya 2.5:48
* sthitisthānaśarīrendriyaviṣayādyāyatanānāṃ parasparopakārāccānugrahaḥ īśvaracodanānugrahaḥ viyojanaṃ vṛttilopaśca  || bhāṣya 2.5:49
* lopo'bhāvaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.5:50
* tasmāttriṣvapi kāleṣu āsanasthaṃ kāryaṃ draṣṭavyam  || bhāṣya 2.5:51
* pṛthivyāṃ bījavadityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.5:52
* āha kiṃ tatkāryaṃ bhagavān yugapadutpādayati kramaśo vā  || bhāṣya 2.5:53
* kiṃ vā karmāpekṣaḥ anapekṣo vā  || bhāṣya 2.5:54
* taducyate yatheṣṭam  || bhāṣya 2.5:55
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 2.5:56

**********************************************

sārvakāmika ityācakṣate  || 2.6 ||

* atra sarvaśabdo vidyādikāryaniravaśeṣavācī draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.6:1
* kāmika ityatrāpi nastrikaṃ cintyate  || bhāṣya 2.6:2
* kāmī kāmaḥ kāmyamiti ca tatra kāmī īśvaraḥ  || bhāṣya 2.6:3
* kāmo'syecchā  || bhāṣya 2.6:4
* kāmyaṃ vidyādikāryam  || bhāṣya 2.6:5
* tadakrameṇa kramaśo vā yatheṣṭamutpādayati  || bhāṣya 2.6:6
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 2.6:7
* kāmitvāt || bhāṣya 2.6:8
* akarmāpekṣitvaṃ cāsyāta eva siddham  || bhāṣya 2.6:9
* karmakāminaśca maheśvaramapekṣante na tu bhagavānīśvaraḥ karma puruṣaṃ vāpekṣate  || bhāṣya 2.6:10
* ato na karmāpekṣa īśvaraḥ  || bhāṣya 2.6:11
* itiśabdo'rthānāṃ nirvacanatvātprakaraṇaparisamāptyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.6:12
* āha kāmitvaṃ tattvavaidharmyaṃ kuryāt || bhāṣya 2.6:13
* kāryatvena vā pariṇāmitvamātmano bandhamokṣaviparyayaṃ vā kuryāt || bhāṣya 2.6:14
* taducyate yasmādāha āṅiti  || bhāṣya 2.6:15
* āṅiti kāryakāraṇatvamātmano muktānāṃ ca maryādā  || bhāṣya 2.6:16
* taducyate utpādyānugrāhyatirobhāvyakalpakatvābhāvakatvenāpariṇāmitvamātmano muktānāṃ ca punarduḥkhairasaṃyojanamityeṣā kāraṇamaryādā  || bhāṣya 2.6:17
* sthāpyā ca kāryamaryādā  || bhāṣya 2.6:18
* taducyate utpādyānugrāhyatirobhāvyakālpyavikāryamaryādā  || bhāṣya 2.6:19
* tadanyacodyādhiṣṭheyatvaṃ ca  || bhāṣya 2.6:20
* cakṣiṅkathane  || bhāṣya 2.6:21
* bhagavatā kāryebhyo bhavatā rudreṇācakṣitam  || bhāṣya 2.6:22
* bhagavato maheśvarasya śaktiḥ sanātanī  || bhāṣya 2.6:23
* tadvidyādikāryaṃ kalitamityeṣo'rthaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.6:24
* tathā coktaṃ varṇitamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.6:25
* atredamādhikārikaṃ kāryakāraṇaprakaraṇaṃ parisamāptamiti  || bhāṣya 2.6:26
* kiṃ nāma kāmitvaṃ rudrakāmitvaṃ ca  || bhāṣya 2.6:27
* etadeva kāraṇe mahābhāgyamāhosvidanyadasti  || bhāṣya 2.6:28
* ucyate asti  || bhāṣya 2.6:29
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 2.6:30

**********************************************

amaṅgalaṃ cātra maṅgalaṃ bhavati  || 2.7 ||

* amaṅgalamiti atra sādhanajātamadhikurute taduddeśena tu maṅgalavacananirdeśaṃ karoti  || bhāṣya 2.7:1
* caśabdaḥ kāraṇaguṇavacanopakṣepe draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.7:2
* upakṣipyottaratra maṅgalaṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ ca vakṣyāmaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.7:3
* tasmādubhayaṃ rudre devāśca pitaraśca ityupariṣṭādvakṣyāmaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.7:4
* atraśabde mūrtyadhiṣṭhātari manaḥsaṃjñe kāraṇe sakale upatiṣṭhatā apasavyasambandho draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.7:5
* maṅgalamityatrāpi cāmaṅgale vaktavye maṅgalameva tadvakṣyāmaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.7:6
* taducyate atra amaṅgalaṃ nāma nagnatvāpasavyatvasambandho hasitādyaḥ sādhanavargaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.7:7
* sa tkiha kāraṇamūrtisāmarthyānmaṅgalaṃ bhavati  || bhāṣya 2.7:8
* śailodomāvanavat || bhāṣya 2.7:9
* maṅgalaṃ sādhanaṃ dharmaniṣpādakam  || bhāṣya 2.7:10
* bhavati iti bhūtārthavādo niḥsaṃśayam  || bhāṣya 2.7:11
* kāraṇamūrtau kriyamāṇamamaṅgalaṃ maṅgalaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.7:12
* āha kiṃ nagnatvamapasavyatvaṃ vā sādhanadvayamevocyate  || bhāṣya 2.7:13
* na  || bhāṣya 2.7:14
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 2.7:15

**********************************************

apasavyaṃ ca pradakṣiṇam  || 2.8 ||

* nagnatvaṃ ca sādhanamevetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.8:1
* atrāmaṅgalanirdeśārthatvātpratyāhāravadupahārasāmānyamātrakhyāpanācca pṛthagapasavyārambhaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.8:2
* tasmādatra apasavyaṃ nāma yatsavyādviparītam  || bhāṣya 2.8:3
* caśabdaḥ samuccaye  || bhāṣya 2.8:4
* na kevalaṃ kāraṇamūrtisāmarthyādamaṅgalaṃ maṅgalamāpadyate apasavyaṃ ca pradakṣiṇamāpadyata ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.8:5
* pradakṣiṇaṃ nāma yadanyeṣāmapasavyaṃ tadiha pradakṣiṇaṃ dharmaniṣpādakaṃ bhavati  || bhāṣya 2.8:6
* nanu yatteṣāṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ kasmāt || bhāṣya 2.8:7
* utsūtrādidoṣādapsnānādivadityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.8:8
* āha samastānāṃ kāraṇaguṇānāṃ tu vacanaṃ kimasti neti  || bhāṣya 2.8:9
* ucyate asti  || bhāṣya 2.8:10
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 2.8:11

**********************************************

tasmādubhayathā yaṣṭavyaḥ  || 2.9 ||

* iti  || bhāṣya 2.9:1
* atra tasmācchabdaḥ kāraṇaguṇavacane  || bhāṣya 2.9:2
* ke kāraṇaguṇā iti  || bhāṣya 2.9:3
* taducyate patitvasattvādyatvājātatvotpādakānugrāhakatirobhāvakatvatapāvāvede vāmadevajyeṣṭharudrakāmitvaṃ ca maṅgalāvāptiḥ pradakṣiṇāvāptiśca  || bhāṣya 2.9:4
* etān kāraṇaguṇān jñātvā  || bhāṣya 2.9:5
* ato bravīti tasmāditi  || bhāṣya 2.9:6
* athaitatkāraṇaguṇavacanaṃ jñātvā sādhakena kiṃ kartavyam  || bhāṣya 2.9:7
* taducyate ubhayathā yaṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.9:8
* atrobhayathā dvidhetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.9:9
* yaṣṭavya ityatrāpi nastrikaṃ cintyate  || bhāṣya 2.9:10
* yaṣṭā yajanaṃ yaṣṭavyamiti  || bhāṣya 2.9:11
* atra yaṣṭā sādhakaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.9:12
* yaṣṭavyo bhagavānmaheśvaraḥ  || bhāṣya 2.9:13
* yajanaṃ bhāvanā  || bhāṣya 2.9:14
* yajanamadhyayanadhyāpanasmaraṇādyam  || bhāṣya 2.9:15
* tatphaladevanityatā sāyujyamityuttaratra vakṣyāmaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.9:16
* āha anirdeśātsaṃdehaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.9:17
* kathamubhayathā  || bhāṣya 2.9:18
* dvidhā nirdeśo vācyaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.9:19
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 2.9:20

**********************************************

devavatpitṛvacca  || 2.10 ||

* kathamiti  || bhāṣya 2.10:1
* ucyate pūrvamasya brāhmaṇasya devayajane pitṛyajane cādhikāro'dhigataḥ  || bhāṣya 2.10:2
* tasmāttebhyo devapitṛbhyo bhaktivyāvartanaṃ kṛtvā ubhayathāpi maheśvare bhāvamavasthāpya yajanaṃ kartavyaṃ nānyasya  || bhāṣya 2.10:3
* caśabdaḥ pratiṣedhe  || bhāṣya 2.10:4
* yattatpūrvaṃ devapitṛṣu kārakatvaṃ saṃbhāvitaṃ tatteṣu na vidyate  || bhāṣya 2.10:5
* atasteṣāṃ yajanaṃ na kartavyamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.10:6
* āha yadyevaṃ tasmāducyatāṃ devapitṇāṃ ko doṣaḥ yasmātte na yaṣṭavyāḥ  || bhāṣya 2.10:7
* rudre vā ko guṇaḥ yasmātsa eva yaṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.10:8
* iti nirdeśo vācyaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.10:9
* apsnānādivat || bhāṣya 2.10:10
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 2.10:11

**********************************************

ubhayaṃ tu rudre devāḥ pitaraśca  || 2.11 ||

* tatrobhayaṃ dvayaṃ samastamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.11:1
* tuśabdo devapitṛṣu vaiśeṣikaṃ kāraṇatvaṃ vyāvartayati rudre iti kāraṇāpadeśam  || bhāṣya 2.11:2
* rudrasya rudratvaṃ pūrvoktam  || bhāṣya 2.11:3
* rudra iti vaiṣayikaṃ saṃnidhānam  || bhāṣya 2.11:4
* atra śaktirviṣaya ityanarthāntaram  || bhāṣya 2.11:5
* te devapitaro rudraśaktyāṃ hāryadhāryakāryatvena vartante ādhīyante viṣaye vartanta ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.11:6
* devāḥ pitaraśca  || bhāṣya 2.11:7
* tatra devā iti ṛbhuṣu brahmādipiśācānteṣu manuṣyatiryagyonivarjaṃ sāmānyasaṃjñā  || bhāṣya 2.11:8
* vidhivat || bhāṣya 2.11:9
* pitara iti viśeṣasaṃjñā bhasmasnānādivadityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.11:10
* brāhmaṇadevadattādivacca  || bhāṣya 2.11:11
* atrāha sāmānyaviśeṣasaṃjñābhidhāne kiṃ prayojanamiti cet || bhāṣya 2.11:12
* taducyate trividhasyāpi kāryasya rudre hāryadhāryakāryajñāpanārthaṃ kiṃca kālakriyāsvāhāsvadhāmantrānyatvadarśanāddevapitṛyajanāpahṛtacittavyāvartanārthatvācca  || bhāṣya 2.11:13
* tasmādanyānanyatvenāpi vyākhyānamaduṣṭam  || bhāṣya 2.11:14
* caśabdo'bhyadhikatve  || bhāṣya 2.11:15
* devapitṛvatsattvasya trividhasyāpi kāryasyeśvare pratiṣṭhāprasavasaṃyogaviyogasukhamohabandhamokṣadātṛtvena ca sa eva paraṃ kāraṇaṃ samastatvenāpyate ityevaṃ caśabdo'bhyadhikatve draṣṭavyaḥ tasmādduḥkhāntārthinā te devapitaro na yaṣṭavyāḥ  || bhāṣya 2.11:16
* tadarthe bhagavānmaheśvaro yaṣṭavya ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.11:17
* atredamānuṣaṅgikaṃ kāryakāraṇaprakaraṇaṃ parisamāptam  || bhāṣya 2.11:18
* ata uttarasya vidhiprakaraṇasya satyapi padārthavailakṣaṇye sādhyasādhanabhāvādyajanena saha sambandhaṃ kariṣyāmaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.11:19
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 2.11:20
* iha purastāduktamubhayathā yaṣṭavyaḥ devavatpitṛvacca  || bhāṣya 2.11:21
* yatra pūrvaṃ devapitṛbhyo vyāvartitayā bhaktyā maheśvaraṃ yajato'navagamātsvātmeśvarasaṃyogaṃ yogaṃ prāpsyasi tatphalaṃ vakṣyāmaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.11:22
* yena ca balenopabṛṃhitasya tatraiva yajane udyogo'bhiniveśaśca bhaviṣyati tadbalaṃ vakṣyāmaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.11:23
* tadbalaprāptau copāyaṃ vakṣyāmaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.11:24
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 2.11:25

**********************************************

harṣāpramādī  || 2.12 ||

* iti  || bhāṣya 2.12:1
* athavānyo dūrasthaḥ sambandhaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.12:2
* yasmāducyate yasya yenārthasambandho dūrasthasyāpi tena tadarthotpattiḥ samānā  || bhāṣya 2.12:3
* ānantarye'pi asaṃbandho grahaṇakṣayādivanna tvatra amaṅgalādibhiḥ  || bhāṣya 2.12:4
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 2.12:5
* śeṣābhāvāt || bhāṣya 2.12:6
* evamihāpi dūrasthaḥ sambandhaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.12:7
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 2.12:8
* iha purastāduktamakaluṣamateḥ carataḥ tato'sya yogaḥ pravartate  || bhāṣya 2.12:9
* atra kaluṣavyatirekeṇa ye'nye yogavyāghātakarā hetavaḥ tān vakṣyāmaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.12:10
* yathā caikā caritavyā yogaprāpikā iti vyākhyāsyāmaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.12:11
* tathā caryāntareṇa tapasā yogaprāptiryathā bhavati tadbalaṃ vakṣyāmaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.12:12
* tadbalaprāptau copāyaṃ vakṣyāmaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.12:13
* harṣāpramādī iti  || bhāṣya 2.12:14
* atra harṣo nāma divyeṣu viṣayeṣu vidhānajadharmaprakāśiteṣu prītituṣṭipramodāḥ  || bhāṣya 2.12:15
* te tviha prāptyādiva viśvāmitrādisvarūpā draṣṭavyāḥ  || bhāṣya 2.12:16
* kathaṃlakṣaṇā iti cet || bhāṣya 2.12:17
* taducyate kāryakaraṇaviśuddhilakṣaṇāḥ tatra kāryaviśuddhistāvadyadaitaddevaśarīraṃ jvalantaṃ bhāsā dīpyantaṃ divi bhuvyantarikṣe ca rukmadaṇḍavaducchritamātmānaṃ paśyati tadā divi aṇimā laghimā mahimā iti trayaḥ kāryaguṇā bhavanti  || bhāṣya 2.12:18
* antarikṣe ca yasmāddharmaviśeṣāt || bhāṣya 2.12:19
* tathā karaṇaviśuddhirapi garimādibhiḥ bāhyairantaḥ karaṇena ca dūraviṣayagrāhakatvālocanasaṃkalpādhyavasāyābhimānādayo bhavanti  || bhāṣya 2.12:20
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 2.12:21
* dharmādivacanāt || bhāṣya 2.12:22
* apica prāptiḥ prākāmyamīśitvaṃ vaśitvaṃ ca yatra kāmāvasāyitvamiti pañca karaṇaguṇā bhavanti  || bhāṣya 2.12:23
* ityevaṃ yadanyeṣāmaṇimādyaṣṭaguṇaṃ catuḥṣaṣṭivikalpaṃ dharmakāryamaiśvaryaṃ tadiha śāstre harṣa iti saṃjñitam  || bhāṣya 2.12:24
* teṣu madamakurvan harṣāpramādī bhavati  || bhāṣya 2.12:25
* dharmavidyābalenetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.12:26
* āha kutrasthasya te harṣā abhivyajyante kīdṛśasya vā  || bhāṣya 2.12:27
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 2.12:28

**********************************************

caryāyāṃ caryāyām  || 2.13 ||

* atra caryāyāṃ caryāyāmiti vīpsā  || bhāṣya 2.13:1
* āha anirdeśādiha vīpsā ekavacanadvivacanabahuvacaneṣu bhavati  || bhāṣya 2.13:2
* tatraikavacane tāvadbhavati  || bhāṣya 2.13:3
* yasmāduktam | adya te rudhiraṃ tīvraṃ pibāmi puruṣādhama | vadedānīṃ susaṃrabdhaḥ punargauriti gauriti  || bhāṣya 2.13:4
* iti  || bhāṣya 2.13:5
* tathā dvivacane'pi bhavati | ubhau dhvajau vātamalau śuśubhāte rathe rathe | saṃrabdhau paramakruddhau yudhi ghnantau parasparam  || bhāṣya 2.13:6
* tathā bahuvacane'pi bhavati  || bhāṣya 2.13:7
* yasmāduktam | puruṣe puruṣe buddhiḥ sā sā bhavati niścitā | tuṣyanti ca pṛthaksarve te manuṣyāstathā tathā  || bhāṣya 2.13:8
* ityataḥ saṃśayaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.13:9
* ataḥ kimekā cariḥ uta caridvayamuta caribahutvamiti  || bhāṣya 2.13:10
* ucyate ekā cariḥ kriyābahutve'pi bhavati  || bhāṣya 2.13:11
* yathāyatane loke ca  || bhāṣya 2.13:12
* tatrāyatane snānahasitādyāḥ loke ca krāthanaspandanādyāḥ vidhikriyāḥ ityevaṃ carikriyātattvaṃ dṛṣṭvā vīpsārthenābhihitaṃ caryāyāṃ caryāyāṃ kriyāyāmityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.13:13
* tasmātsakṛduccāritā vīpsā bahuvacane'pi bhavati  || bhāṣya 2.13:14
* vṛkṣabalivat || bhāṣya 2.13:15
* kiṃca māhātmyamiti caryottarasambandhātkathyate ekā cariḥ kriyābahutve bhavati  || bhāṣya 2.13:16
* kāryakaraṇaviśuddhilakṣaṇā harṣā ityuktam  || bhāṣya 2.13:17
* na ca vibhā kāryakaraṇaiśvaryābhiniveśaḥ śakyate kartumityato'vagamyate kāryakaraṇavataścarato mahimāno'bhivyajyanta ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.13:18
* āha apramattasyācarataḥ kā kāryaniṣpattiḥ  || bhāṣya 2.13:19
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 2.13:20

**********************************************

māhātmyamavāpnoti  || 2.14 ||

* māhātmyaṃ nāma aryamaprakāśavat || bhāṣya 2.14:1
* taduta yasya sāṃnidhyādayaṃ brāhmaṇaḥ paridṛṣṭārtho'pi bhūtvā duṣṭāśvatararathasthānīyairdehendriyādibhirapanīyate apahriyate tadamāhātmyam  || bhāṣya 2.14:2
* vidhiyogābhiniveśāsāmarthyamadharmabalam  || bhāṣya 2.14:3
* uktaṃ hi | hriyate buddhirmano'pi narasya dehendriyaiḥ | amūḍhasaṃjño durdāntairduṣṭāśvairiva sārathiḥ  || bhāṣya 2.14:4
* māhātmyamato viparītam  || bhāṣya 2.14:5
* yasya sāṃnidhyādayaṃ brāhmaṇaḥ snānaśayanānusnānādikrāthanaspandanādhyayanadhyānasmaraṇakaraṇasamartho bhavati parayā śraddhayā yuktastanmāhātmyam  || bhāṣya 2.14:6
* āha kiṃ taditi  || bhāṣya 2.14:7
* ucyate yadetatprabhraṣṭasya tapaso vīryaṃ tapobalaṃ tapaḥśaktistanmāhātmyam  || bhāṣya 2.14:8
* kathaṃ gamyate  || bhāṣya 2.14:9
* caryānantaroktatvāt || bhāṣya 2.14:10
* yadanantaraṃ yadavāpnoti tata eva tadāsādayatītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.14:11
* āha prāpnotīti astu pāṭhaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.14:12
* labdhurbhaviṣyatītyucyate  || bhāṣya 2.14:13
* avaramya kṣaṇagatiprītiprāptyarthatvāttena vidhicaraṇena rakṣate harṣaviśeṣāṇāmabhiprītiviśeṣaṇamatigatisaṃstavanāvāptiśca  || bhāṣya 2.14:14
* tasmānmantrabalavaddharmabalamevāsya māhātmyamavāpnotītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.14:15
* āha kaḥ so'bhyupāyaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.14:16
* kāni vā tāni dharmasādhanāni yairharṣotpattirmāhātmyalābhaśca bhavati  || bhāṣya 2.14:17
* carikriyāṇāṃ vā katividho vibhāgaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.14:18
* dānādīnāṃ vā pūrvoktānāṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ kimasti neti  || bhāṣya 2.14:19
* ucyate'sti  || bhāṣya 2.14:20
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 2.14:21

**********************************************

atidattamatīṣṭam  || 2.15 ||

* atra kudānāni gobhūhiraṇyasuvarṇādīni  || bhāṣya 2.15:1
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 2.15:2
* anaikāntikānātyantikasātiśayaphalatvātkupathādhvapravādācca  || bhāṣya 2.15:3
* tasmādatra atiśabdo viśeṣaṇam  || bhāṣya 2.15:4
* atidānaṃ cātmapradānam  || bhāṣya 2.15:5
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 2.15:6
* ātmanaḥ dātṛtvādbhūyo dānaprayojanābhāvāt || bhāṣya 2.15:7
* sthānaśarīrendriyaviṣayādyaprāpakatvāt || bhāṣya 2.15:8
* aikāntikātyantikarudrasamīpaprāpterekāntenaivānāvṛttiphalatvādasādhāraṇaphalatvāccātmapradānamatidānam  || bhāṣya 2.15:9
* tathā kuyajanānyagniṣṭomādīni  || bhāṣya 2.15:10
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 2.15:11
* saṃgrahapratigrahahiṃsādiyuktena śraveṇābhinirvṛttidarśanātpattrīrātrijadevatādisādhāraṇaphalatvādanityasātiśayasaṃkīrṇaphalatvācca kuyajanānyagniṣṭomādīni  || bhāṣya 2.15:12
* tasmādatrātiśabdo viśeṣaṇam  || bhāṣya 2.15:13
* atiyajanaṃ nāma yadāyatane loke vā  || bhāṣya 2.15:14
* tatrāyatane snānahasitādyā loke ca krāthanaspandanādyā vidhikriyā  || bhāṣya 2.15:15
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 2.15:16
* saṃgrahapratigrahahiṃsādirahitena krameṇa svaśarīrasamutthābhiḥ kāyikavācikamānasikābhirijyate yasmāt || bhāṣya 2.15:17
* ataścetyucyate atiyajanam  || bhāṣya 2.15:18
* āha kiṃ dānaṃ yajanaṃ ca sādhanadvayamevātrātiśabdena viśiṣṭaṃ kartavyamiti  || bhāṣya 2.15:19
* ucyate na  || bhāṣya 2.15:20
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 2.15:21

**********************************************

atitaptaṃ tapastathā  || 2.16 ||

* atiśabdo viśeṣaṇe  || bhāṣya 2.16:1
* nāyāntyādhyātmikādhibhautikādhidaivikāsteṣāṃ svaśāstroktena krameṇa manasi saṃmatānāṃ matānāmanupāyataḥ pratīkāramakurvatāṃ tapo niṣpadyate  || bhāṣya 2.16:2
* yatra tṛtīyāyāmātmasaṃyogānniṣpadyate tattapa ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.16:3
* tadā śayanārthe katham  || bhāṣya 2.16:4
* tathātitāpebhyo'titapo niṣpadyate tathā dānayajanābhyāmapīti  || bhāṣya 2.16:5
* tathāśabdaḥ sādhanatrayopasaṃhārārthaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.16:6
* yadā dadāti tadā yajati tapyati ca  || bhāṣya 2.16:7
* yadāpi yajati tadā dadāti tapyati ca  || bhāṣya 2.16:8
* yadā tapyati tadā dadāti yajati ca  || bhāṣya 2.16:9
* evamādidīkṣāprabhṛtirasya brāhmaṇasya  || bhāṣya 2.16:10
* ebhistribhirupāyairgaṅgāsrotovaddharmasyāyo'dharmasya vyayo bhavati tadātidānādiniṣpannena prakṛṣṭena tapasā asya brāhmaṇasya harṣotpattirmāhātmyalābhaśca sambhavatītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.16:11
* āha atidānādiniṣpannena prakṛṣṭena tapasāsya brāhmaṇasya kā gatirbhavatīti  || bhāṣya 2.16:12
* ucyate abhyudayakaivalyavyatirekeṇa  || bhāṣya 2.16:13

**********************************************

atyāgatiṃ gamayate  || 2.17 ||

* atra atiśabdo viśeṣaṇe  || bhāṣya 2.17:1
* āṅiti maryādāyāṃ bhavati  || bhāṣya 2.17:2
* kathamadhyayanadhyānādirahitamapi sādhakaṃ tapo'tigatiṃ gamayati tadabhyāso haratyenamiti vacanāt || bhāṣya 2.17:3
* atigatiriti yogaparyāyaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.17:4
* kathaṃ gamyate  || bhāṣya 2.17:5
* tapaḥkāryatvādānantyabrahmasāyujyavat || bhāṣya 2.17:6
* sāṃkhyayogābhyudayādigativiśeṣaviśeṣitatvādupariṣṭādvartata ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.17:7
* tasmāttapasaḥ phalaṃ viśeṣārthamabhidhīyate yogo'tigatimiti  || bhāṣya 2.17:8
* atigatimiti karma  || bhāṣya 2.17:9
* gamayate  || bhāṣya 2.17:10
* na tāvadgataḥ gamiṣyati kiṃtu gamayatītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.17:11
* āha atidānādyathāvattapaso guṇavacanaṃ kimasti neti  || bhāṣya 2.17:12
* ucyate asti  || bhāṣya 2.17:13
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 2.17:14

**********************************************

tasmāt || 2.18 ||

* iti  || bhāṣya 2.18:1
* atra tasmācchabdastapaso guṇavacane  || bhāṣya 2.18:2
* tasmādatidānādiniṣpanno dharmo'tyāgatiṃ gamayate niratiśayāṃ prāpayati  || bhāṣya 2.18:3
* na sthānaśarīrendriyaviṣayādiprāptau  || bhāṣya 2.18:4
* aikāntikātyantikarudrasamīpaprāpterekāntenaivānāvṛttiphalatvaṃ ca dṛṣṭvā  || bhāṣya 2.18:5
* ato bravīti tasmāditi  || bhāṣya 2.18:6
* āha atyantatapaso guṇavacanaṃ jñātvā kāraṇaṃ ca sādhakena kiṃ kartavyam  || bhāṣya 2.18:7
* taducyate tadarthameva  || bhāṣya 2.18:8

**********************************************

bhūyastapaścaret || 2.19 ||

* atra bhūyaḥ punaḥsaṃdhāne draṣṭavyaḥ iṣṭāpūrtavat || bhāṣya 2.19:1
* katham  || bhāṣya 2.19:2
* harṣeṣvabhisaktasya muhūrtamardhamuhūrtaṃ vā sādhanebhyo vyucchedaṃ dṛṣṭvā saṃdhāne bhūyaḥśabdo'bhihitaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.19:3
* tasmādatra tapastadeva  || bhāṣya 2.19:4
* niruktamasya pūrvoktam  || bhāṣya 2.19:5
* caredityarjanamadhikurute  || bhāṣya 2.19:6
* dharmānekasaṃśayānyatvācca apunarukto'yaṃ caraśabdo draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.19:7
* āha yadyevaṃ tasmāducyatāṃ harṣāṇāṃ ko doṣo'bhivyajyate  || bhāṣya 2.19:8
* māhātmyasya vā ko guṇaḥ yasmāttadgrāhyamiti  || bhāṣya 2.19:9
* taducyate kurute māhātmyam  || bhāṣya 2.19:10
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 2.19:11

**********************************************

nānyabhaktistu śaṃkare  || 2.20 ||

* ayaṃ nakāro'nyabhaktipratiṣedhe  || bhāṣya 2.20:1
* bhaktirbhāvanetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.20:2
* tuśabdo viśeṣaṇe  || bhāṣya 2.20:3
* katham  || bhāṣya 2.20:4
* ye harṣeṣvabhisaktāḥ dūṣyataḥ taskaratvamāpannāḥ te viśeṣeṇa tu śaṃkarāddūrasthā bhavanti  || bhāṣya 2.20:5
* śaṃkaraḥ kasmāt || bhāṣya 2.20:6
* samastasukhanirvāṇakaratvācchaṃkaraḥ  || bhāṣya 2.20:7
* śaṃkare ityaupaśleṣikaṃ saṃnidhānam  || bhāṣya 2.20:8
* śaṃkare bhāvanā kartavyā nānyatretyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.20:9
* uktaṃ hi | karmaṇā manasā vācā yadaślakṣṇaṃ niṣevate | tadabhyāso haratyenaṃ tasmātkalyāṇamācaret || bhāṣya 2.20:10
* evamete mahātmānaḥ prāhuradhyātmacintakāḥ | yaccittastanmayo bhāvo guhyametatsanātanam  || bhāṣya 2.20:11
* gacchaṃstiṣṭhan śayāno vā jāgraccaiva svapaṃstathā | śaṃkare bhāvanāṃ kuryādyadīcchedyogamātmanaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.20:12
* yasmātkṣayānte trailokyaṃ śaṃkare yāti saṃkṣayam | tasmātsaṃvartako dhātā śaṃkarastvabhidhīyate  || bhāṣya 2.20:13
* evaṃ śaṃkare bhāva upaśleṣitavyo nānyatretyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.20:14
* evaṃ parisamāptiṃ kṛtvā yuktaṃ vaktum  || bhāṣya 2.20:15

**********************************************

atredaṃ brahma japet || 2.21 ||

* iti  || bhāṣya 2.21:1
* asya pūrvokto'rthaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.21:2
* āha dharmapariṇāmakatvātśaṃkaratvātsukhada īśvaro'bhihitaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.21:3
* athāntarasṛṣṭyāṃ sukhaduḥkhakāraṇaṃ kiṃ bhavati dharmādharmasattvarajovaduta neti  || bhāṣya 2.21:4
* ucyate atra ya upāyaḥ sukhadaḥ tathā vakṣyāmaḥ yathāvān yatra vyavasthite saṃsāragate kārye sa eva kāraṇaṃ param  || bhāṣya 2.21:5
* nanu koryakalavanniradhikārastathā vakṣyāmo vistaraśaścāsmin brahmaṇi kāraṇaśaktiṃ vakṣyāmaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.21:6
* śaktiṃ ca jñātvā yathā sādhako'ṣṭabhirnamaskārairātmānaṃ dadāti tathā vakṣyāmaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.21:7
* ata idamārabhyate  || bhāṣya 2.21:8

**********************************************

vāmadevāya namo jyeṣṭhāya namo rudrāya namaḥ  || 2.22 ||

* iti  || bhāṣya 2.22:1
* prayogānyatvātprayojanānyatvāccāpunaruktā vāmadevādiśabdā draṣṭavyāḥ  || bhāṣya 2.22:2
* atra vāmatvaṃ devatvaṃ jyeṣṭhatvaṃ rudratvaṃ ca pūrvoktam  || bhāṣya 2.22:3
* vāmadevajyeṣṭharudrāyeti caturthī  || bhāṣya 2.22:4
* nama ityātmapradāne pūjāyāṃ ca  || bhāṣya 2.22:5
* sambhāvanānyatvāccāpunaruktāḥ sarvanamaskārā draṣṭavyāḥ  || bhāṣya 2.22:6
* āha kiṃ catuṣkamevātra kāraṇe cintyate  || bhāṣya 2.22:7
* nāmacatuṣkāpadeśena vā nāmni namaskāro draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.22:8
* ucyate na  || bhāṣya 2.22:9
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 2.22:10

**********************************************

kālāya namaḥ  || 2.23 ||

* atra kāla ityeṣa maheśvaraparyāyaḥ kasmātpūrvottarasūtrasāmarthyāt || bhāṣya 2.23:1
* kālayate yasmātkṣetrasādhiniṣṭhāni sthānāni kalādiśarīrendriyaviṣayādibhyo viyogeneti tataḥ kālaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.23:2
* uktaṃ hi  || bhāṣya 2.23:3
* brahmādibhūrjaparyantaṃ jagadetaccarācaram | yataḥ kalayate rudraḥ kālarūpī tataḥ smṛtaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.23:4
* kālyān kalayate yasmātkalābhyaḥ kālaparyayāt | kalanātkālanāccāpi kāla ityabhidhīyate  || bhāṣya 2.23:5
* evaṃ kālo hi bhagavān  || bhāṣya 2.23:6
* kālyāḥ kṣetrajñāḥ  || bhāṣya 2.23:7
* sthānāni tu brahmendradevapitrādivacanādbrāhmaṃ prājāpatyaṃ saumyamaindraṃ gāndharvaṃ yākṣaṃ rākṣasaṃ paiśācamiti  || bhāṣya 2.23:8
* tathā brāhmaṇaśūdragomṛgasarvabhūtakṛtānnādivacanāttathā mānuṣapaśumṛgapakṣisarīsṛpasthāvarādīnāṃ grahaṇam  || bhāṣya 2.23:9
* tathā yogeśvarā deveṣvantarbhūtāḥ  || bhāṣya 2.23:10
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 2.23:11
* dharmabāhulyāt || bhāṣya 2.23:12
* tathā nārakāstiryakṣvantarbhūtāḥ  || bhāṣya 2.23:13
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 2.23:14
* adharmabāhulyāt || bhāṣya 2.23:15
* evaṃ sthānataścaturdaśakaḥ saṃsāra ityupacaryate  || bhāṣya 2.23:16
* eteṣu kalādivacanānmaheśvaro nimittam  || bhāṣya 2.23:17
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 2.23:18
* pūrvottaraśarīreṣu bhogalopābhivyaktimātratvāt || bhāṣya 2.23:19
* ādimān saṃsāro draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.23:20
* tatphalabhoktṛtvātkāryakaraṇayoranāditvādanādirakṛtābhyāgamādityetadbhagavatyabhyadhikatvaṃ śeṣeṣu ca puruṣeṣu nyūnatvaṃ jñātvā yuktaṃ vaktuṃ kālāya namaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.23:21
* atrāpi kālāya iti caturthī  || bhāṣya 2.23:22
* nama ityātmapradāne pūjāyāṃ ca  || bhāṣya 2.23:23
* sambhāvanānyatvāccāpunarukto'yaṃ namaskāro draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.23:24
* āha atha sthānaśarīrendriyaviṣayādīnāṃ kimeṣa bhagavān prabhuḥ kartā bhavati neti  || bhāṣya 2.23:25
* ucyate prabhuḥ kartaiva  || bhāṣya 2.23:26
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 2.23:27

**********************************************

kalavikaraṇāya namaḥ  || 2.24 ||

* atra kalā nāma kāryakaraṇākhyāḥ kalāḥ  || bhāṣya 2.24:1
* tatra kāryākhyāḥ pṛthivyāpastejo vāyurākāśaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.24:2
* ākāśaḥ śabdaguṇaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.24:3
* śabdasparśaguṇo vāyustau ca rūpaṃ ca tejasi | te rasaśca jale jñeyāste ca gandhaḥ kṣitāvapi  || bhāṣya 2.24:4
* śabdasparśarūparasagandhāḥ  || bhāṣya 2.24:5
* tathā karaṇākhyāḥ śrotraṃ tvakcakṣuḥ jihvā ghrāṇaṃ pādaḥ pāyuḥ upasthaḥ hastaḥ vākmanaḥ ahaṃkāraḥ buddhiriti  || bhāṣya 2.24:6
* tāsāṃ vikaraṇo bhagavānīśvaraḥ  || bhāṣya 2.24:7
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 2.24:8
* dṛkkriyāśaktyorapratīghātāt || bhāṣya 2.24:9
* vikaraṇatvaṃ nāma sthānaśarīrendriyaviṣayādisaṃniveśena vistaravibhāgaviśeṣataśca kāryakaraṇākhyābhiḥ kalābhirdharmajñānavairāgyaiśvaryādharmājñānāvairāgyānaiśvaryādibhiśca kṣetrajñasaṃyojanamityetadbhagavatyabhyadhikatvaṃ śeṣeṣu ca puruṣeṣu nyūnatvaṃ jñātvā yuktaṃ vaktuṃ kalavikaraṇāya namaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.24:10
* atrāpi kalavikaraṇāya iti caturthī | nama ityātmapradāne pūjāyāṃ ca  || bhāṣya 2.24:11
* sambhāvanānyatvāccāpunarukto'yaṃ namaḥśadaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.24:12
* āha kālanavikaraṇatvādavāntarasṛṣṭyāṃ karmakṣaye vṛttilābhe cāpekṣate neti  || bhāṣya 2.24:13
* dharmajñānavairāgyaiśvaryādīnāṃ vā kimeṣa bhagavān prabhurbhavati neti  || bhāṣya 2.24:14
* ucyate prabhuḥ  || bhāṣya 2.24:15
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 2.24:16

**********************************************

balapramathanāya namaḥ  || 2.25 ||

* balaṃ nāma dharmajñānavairāgyaiśvaryādharmājñānāvairāgyānaiśvaryāṇi icchādveṣaprayatnādīni vidyāvargaḥ rūpāṇi  || bhāṣya 2.25:1
* pra iti bhṛśārthe kāmitvākarṣaṇe ca  || bhāṣya 2.25:2
* mathanatvaṃ nāma balavṛttinirodhanamudadhimathanavat || bhāṣya 2.25:3
* na vāgnijñānādīni durbalāni  || bhāṣya 2.25:4
* yasmāduktam | na hyatejasvināśāya taijasāḥ prabhavanti vai | balānyatibalānyasya na bhave'tibalāni vai  || bhāṣya 2.25:5
* ityevaṃ bhagavatyabhyadhikatvaṃ śeṣeṣu puruṣeṣu nyūnatvaṃ jñātvā yuktaṃ vaktuṃ balapramathanāya namaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.25:6
* atrāpi balapramathanāya iti caturthī  || bhāṣya 2.25:7
* nama ityātmapradāne pūjāyāṃ ca  || bhāṣya 2.25:8
* sambhāvanānyatvāccāpunarukto'yaṃ namaskāro draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.25:9
* āha keṣāṃ kālanavikaraṇamathanāni karoti  || bhāṣya 2.25:10
* taducyate bhūtānām  || bhāṣya 2.25:11
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 2.25:12

**********************************************

sarvabhūtadamanāya namaḥ  || 2.26 ||

* atra kalāvacane punaruktidoṣānna pṛthivyādiṣu sarvaśabdaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.26:1
* kiṃ tu siddheśvaravarjaṃ cetaneṣveva sarvabhūtaśabdaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.26:2
* āha bhūtatvānupapatterna cetaneṣu sarvabhūtaśabdaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.26:3
* taducyate damanāya  || bhāṣya 2.26:4
* śamu damu upaśame  || bhāṣya 2.26:5
* devamanuṣyādīnāṃ sthānaśarīrendriyaviṣayādiṣu yā ratiḥ rañjanādhivāsanā tatsarvamantaradṛṣṭyā sarvamīśvarakṛtameva draṣṭavyamityetadbhagavatyabhyadhikatvaṃ śeṣeṣu ca puruṣeṣu nyūnatvaṃ jñātvā yuktaṃ vaktuṃ sarvabhūtadamanāya namaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.26:6
* atrāpi sarvabhūtadamanāya iti caturthī  || bhāṣya 2.26:7
* nama ityātmapradāne pūjāyāṃ ca  || bhāṣya 2.26:8
* sambhāvanānyatvāccāpunarukto'yaṃ namaskāro draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.26:9
* āha kīdṛśe maheśvare kālanādiśaktirucyate kiṃ sakale niṣkale uta ubhayorapi  || bhāṣya 2.26:10
* ucyate ubhayorapi  || bhāṣya 2.26:11
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 2.26:12

**********************************************

mano'manāya namaḥ  || 2.27 ||

* atra manaḥśabdenāntaḥkaraṇaṃ tattantratvādudāharaṇārthatvācca manograhaṇasya ubhayātmakatvācca manasaḥ sarvakaraṇagrahaṇānugrahaṇācca kāryagrahaṇamityataḥ kāryakaraṇādhiṣṭhātṛtvācca sakala ityupacaryate  || bhāṣya 2.27:1
* tathā caitādṛśamanasaḥ pratiṣedhādatra kāryakaraṇarahito niṣkalo bhagavānamana ityucyate  || bhāṣya 2.27:2
* tasmātsakaletarānugrāhakānādiśaktirvidyate  || bhāṣya 2.27:3
* uktaṃ hi | apāṇipādodarapārśvajihvaḥ atīndriyo vyāpisvabhāvasiddhaḥ | paśyatyacakṣuḥ sa śṛṇotyakarṇo na cāstyabuddhaṃ na ca buddhirasti | sa vetti sarvaṃ na ca tasyāsti vettā tamāhuragryaṃ puruṣaṃ mahāntam  || bhāṣya 2.27:4
* ityetadbhagavatyabhyadhikatvaṃ śeṣeṣu ca nyūnatvaṃ ca jñātvā yuktaṃ vaktuṃ mano'manāya namaḥ  || bhāṣya 2.27:5
* mano'manāya iti caturthī  || bhāṣya 2.27:6
* namu ityātmapradāne pūjāyāṃ ca  || bhāṣya 2.27:7
* sambhāvanānyatvāccāpunaruktāḥ sarve namaḥśabdā draṣṭavyāḥ  || bhāṣya 2.27:8

**********************************************


ড়āśupatasūtra, 3

avyaktaliṅgī  || 3.1 ||

* iti  || bhāṣya 3.1:1
* atra akāro liṅgavyaktatvaṃ pratiṣedhati  || bhāṣya 3.1:2
* avasitaprayojanaḥ pūrvoktairliṅgopakaraṇairanusnānanirmālyaikavāsādyaiḥ prayojanairvinivṛttairlokatastriṣu snānaṃ kurvannapi  || bhāṣya 3.1:3
* ṣaḍāśramaliṅgānupalabdhāvanavadhṛtoktaliṅgavadavyaktāḥ kriyāḥ kāryāḥ  || bhāṣya 3.1:4
* āha kayā vā maryādayā kasmin vā kāle sā kriyā kartavyā  || bhāṣya 3.1:5
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 3.1:6

**********************************************

vyaktācāraḥ  || 3.2 ||

* atra vyaktaśabdo divasamadhikurute  || bhāṣya 3.2:1
* vyaktāḥ sphuṭāḥ prakāśāḥ  || bhāṣya 3.2:2
* ahanītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.2:3
* āṅityavamānādiniṣpattimaryādāmadhikurute  || bhāṣya 3.2:4
* cāra iti krāthanādīnāmuddeśaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.2:5
* tān krāthanādīn sādhako naṭadavasthito raṅgavallaukikānadhijanya nāṭakavadācārānācarati karoti prayuṅkta ityato'yaṃ vyaktācāraḥ  || bhāṣya 3.2:6
* āha avyaktaliṅgino vyaktācārasya kā kāryaniṣpattiḥ  || bhāṣya 3.2:7
* taducyate avamānaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.2:8
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 3.2:9

**********************************************

avamataḥ  || 3.3 ||

* atra ava varjane  || bhāṣya 3.3:1
* mānena teṣāṃ liṅgācārajñānavidhiviparītapravṛttiṃ dṛṣṭvā sarvadoṣaduṣṭo'yamiti mānasādhenāvamāne yo janaḥ parivarjayatānyato'yamabahumatatvaṃ prāpnoti  || bhāṣya 3.3:2
* uktaṃ ca  || bhāṣya 3.3:3
* amṛtasyeva lipseta naiva mānaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ | viṣasyeva jugupseta sanmānasya sadā dvijaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.3:4
* sukhaṃ hyavamataḥ śete sarvasaṅgavivarjitaḥ | doṣān parasya na dhyāyettasya pāpaṃ sadā muniḥ  || bhāṣya 3.3:5
* āha keṣvavyaktaliṅginā vyaktācāreṇāvamatena bhavitavyamiti  || bhāṣya 3.3:6
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 3.3:7

**********************************************

sarvabhūteṣu  || 3.4 ||

* atra sarvabhūtaśabdo varṇāśramiṣu draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.4:1
* kasmāducyate varṇāśramiṣviti  || bhāṣya 3.4:2
* bhūteṣu ityuktaṃ natu devatiryagyonimlecchādiṣu  || bhāṣya 3.4:3
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 3.4:4
* vyaktācārāvamānadānādānavirodhāt || bhāṣya 3.4:5
* uktaṃ hi | dhanyo deśo yatra gāvaḥ prabhūtāḥ medhyaṃ cānnaṃ pārthivā dharmaśīlāḥ | puṇyā nadyaḥ sarvalokopabhogyāstāṃstān deśānsiddhikāmo vrajeta  || bhāṣya 3.4:6
* bhūteṣu iti sāmīpikaṃ saṃnidhānam  || bhāṣya 3.4:7
* bhūtasamīpe bhūtābhyāśe bhūtānāmadhyakṣa ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.4:8
* sāmīpikavyākhyānenāvamānadeśādispaṣṭataratvādasya vidhyācaraṇam  || bhāṣya 3.4:9
* asureṣu ityācaraṇajñāpakācca  || bhāṣya 3.4:10
* āha avamatena sarvabhūteṣu kiṃ kartavyam  || bhāṣya 3.4:11
* taducyate caritavyam  || bhāṣya 3.4:12
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 3.4:13

**********************************************

paribhūyamānaścaret || 3.5 ||

* atra pari sarvatobhāve  || bhāṣya 3.5:1
* pagakṣyāḥ parīkṣyā ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.5:2
* bhūya iti bahudhā  || bhāṣya 3.5:3
* yaṣṭimuṣṭyādibhiḥ saṃyojanaṃ paribhavaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.5:4
* kāyika ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.5:5
* māna iti sādhakakālakarmābhidhāne  || bhāṣya 3.5:6
* paribhūyamānenaiva  || bhāṣya 3.5:7
* sa paribhavo daridrapuruṣarājābhiṣeka iva draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.5:8
* kanakapāṣāṇavadindrakīlakavacca bhavitavyam  || bhāṣya 3.5:9
* caredityarjanamadhikurute  || bhāṣya 3.5:10
* dharmārjane niyoge ca nindita iti garhitābhyākhyāne caritavyamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.5:11
* āha avamatasya paribhūyamānasyācarataḥ kiṃ tāpaśāntireva uta śuddhirapyasti  || bhāṣya 3.5:12
* ucyate asti  || bhāṣya 3.5:13
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 3.5:14

**********************************************

apahatapāpmā  || 3.6 ||

* bhavatīti vākyaśeṣo vacanādhikārādgamyate  || bhāṣya 3.6:1
* atra apa varjane āghāte nāśe ca  || bhāṣya 3.6:2
* apahatā anyatanaṣṭā ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.6:3
* pāpmāno'tra dvividhāḥ sulakṣaṇāḥ duḥkhalakṣaṇāśca  || bhāṣya 3.6:4
* tatra sukhalakṣaṇāḥ unmādaḥ madaḥ mohaḥ nidrā ālasyaṃ koṇatā aliṅgaḥ nityamasadvāditvaṃ bahubhojanamityevamādyāḥ  || bhāṣya 3.6:5
* tathā duḥkhalakṣaṇāḥśirorogadantarogākṣirogādyāḥ  || bhāṣya 3.6:6
* evamete pāpmāna ātmagatāḥ kāyakaraṇeṣvādarśapratirūpakavadabhivyaktāḥ  || bhāṣya 3.6:7
* kṛtsnāḥ apahatāḥ pāpmānaḥ so'yamapahatapāpmā bhavatītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.6:8
* āha kimavamānaḥ paribhavaśca kāyikaṃ mānasaṃ sādhanadvayamevāsya pāpakṣayaśuddhihetuḥ āhosvidvācikamapyasti neti  || bhāṣya 3.6:9
* ucyate yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 3.6:10

**********************************************

pareṣāṃ parivādāt || 3.7 ||

* atra parā nāma svaparasamayādhikṛtā ye avamānādibhiḥ saṃyojayanti teṣām  || bhāṣya 3.7:1
* pareṣāmiti ṣaṣṭhībahuvacanam  || bhāṣya 3.7:2
* āha svaparavākyāvamānādibhiḥ śuddhirevāsya na tu vṛddhiḥ  || bhāṣya 3.7:3
* sā ca sādhakasya phalābhidhānādatidānādiṣvityucyate na pūrvakṛtasukṛtadānavivakṣayā  || bhāṣya 3.7:4
* pari iti ca sarvatobhāve  || bhāṣya 3.7:5
* vada vādaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.7:6
* avyakto'yaṃ preto'yamunmatto'yaṃ mūḍho'yaṃ mūrkho'yaṃ nidrāviṣṭo vāyuruddho'yaṃ duṣkāmyasamyakkārī asamyagvādī ityevamugrairvacobhirabhighnantīti vādāḥ  || bhāṣya 3.7:7
* vādāditi nimittapañcamī draṣṭavyā  || bhāṣya 3.7:8
* tasmādavamānādibhiḥ parān saṃyojayatā svayamevātmā saṃyoktavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.7:9
* anyathā pāśamātraḥ syāditi  || bhāṣya 3.7:10
* pāpmanāṃ vā jānapadeśātsaṃdehaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.7:11
* kasya kāryaṃ pāpmanaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.7:12
* kathaṃ vā teṣāṃ kāryakaraṇeṣvabhivyaktānāṃ parasamutthairavamānādibhirnirghātanaṃ bhavati  || bhāṣya 3.7:13
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 3.7:14
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 3.7:15

**********************************************

pāpaṃ ca tebhyo dadāti  || 3.8 ||

* atra pāpamityadharmaparyāyaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.8:1
* tadyathā | āgo'parāgo musalaṃ duritaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ tarūn | pāpaṃ pāpmānaṃ vṛjinaṃ steyam  || bhāṣya 3.8:2
* ityekārthavācakāḥ śabdāḥ  || bhāṣya 3.8:3
* iha coktaṃ pāpamiti  || bhāṣya 3.8:4
* pāpaṃ ca kasmāt || bhāṣya 3.8:5
* pāvakapāsakatvātpāpam  || bhāṣya 3.8:6
* pāvayati yasmātśirorogadantarogākṣirogādibhiḥ pātayati narakādiṣu pāsayati vāniṣṭābhiḥ kāryakaraṇākhyābhiḥ kalābhiriti  || bhāṣya 3.8:7
* ataḥ pāvakapātakapāsakatvātpāpam  || bhāṣya 3.8:8
* evaṃca bījāṅkuravatpāpapāpmanāṃ hetuhetumattvopanayo draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.8:9
* bījapāpaprasavāḥ pāpmāna ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.8:10
* tebhya iti caturthī sampradānārthā  || bhāṣya 3.8:11
* atha evamavamānādibhiḥ saṃyojayanti tebhyo dadāti prayacchati saṃkrāmayatītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.8:12
* āha kimavamānādibhiḥ śuddhirevāsya na tu vṛddhiriti  || bhāṣya 3.8:13
* taducyate vṛddhirapyasti  || bhāṣya 3.8:14
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 3.8:15

**********************************************

sukṛtaṃ ca teṣāmādatte  || 3.9 ||

* atra su praśaṃsāyām  || bhāṣya 3.9:1
* kṛtamiti dharmaparyāyaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.9:2
* caśabdaḥ śuddhisamuccayārthaḥ pūrvadharmaniyogārthaśca  || bhāṣya 3.9:3
* teṣāmiti  || bhāṣya 3.9:4
* ye avamānādibhiḥ saṃyojayanti teṣāmityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.9:5
* teṣāmiti ṣaṣṭhīgrahaṇamanabhivyaktasya kṛtsnasyādānajñāpanārtham  || bhāṣya 3.9:6
* ādatte  || bhāṣya 3.9:7
* ā grahaṇe  || bhāṣya 3.9:8
* svātmani karoti viṣamaṃ vā ihāturavadityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.9:9
* āha atidānādyatitapovadavamānādisādhanaṃ guṇavacanaṃ kimasti neti  || bhāṣya 3.9:10
* ucyate asti  || bhāṣya 3.9:11
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 3.9:12

**********************************************

tasmāt || 3.10 ||

* iti | atra tasmācchabdaḥ avamānādisādhanaguṇavacane  || bhāṣya 3.10:1
* katham  || bhāṣya 3.10:2
* yasmādavamānādibhiḥ pāpapāpmanāṃ kṣaye śuddhiḥ sukṛtādāne ca vṛddhirbhavati  || bhāṣya 3.10:3
* yasmācca tanniṣṭhālaukikaśarīrendriyaviṣayādiprāpakaḥ aikāntikātyantikarudrasamīpaprāptirekāntenātyantikī bhavati  || bhāṣya 3.10:4
* ato bravīti tasmāditi  || bhāṣya 3.10:5
* āha athaitadavamānādisādhanameva kartavyam  || bhāṣya 3.10:6
* kiṃ vā avyaktāvasthānaiva caritavyam  || bhāṣya 3.10:7
* taducyate na  || bhāṣya 3.10:8
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 3.10:9

**********************************************

pretavaccaret || 3.11 ||

* atra puruṣākhyaḥ pretaḥ na mṛtākhyaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.11:1
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 3.11:2
* ācaraṇopadeśāt || bhāṣya 3.11:3
* vaditi kiṃcidupamā  || bhāṣya 3.11:4
* unmattasadṛśadaridrapuruṣasnātamaladigdhāṅgena rūḍhaśmaśrunakharomadhāriṇā sarvasaṃskāravarjitena bhavitavyam  || bhāṣya 3.11:5
* ato varṇāśramavyucchedo vairāgyotsāhaśca jāyate  || bhāṣya 3.11:6
* prayojananiṣpattiśca bhavati avamānādi  || bhāṣya 3.11:7
* caredityājñāmadhikurute  || bhāṣya 3.11:8
* dharmārjane niyoge ca  || bhāṣya 3.11:9
* saṃśayānyatvāccāpunarukto'yaṃ caraśabdo draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.11:10
* āha carato'sya ke kriyāviśeṣāḥ  || bhāṣya 3.11:11
* kā vārthaniṣpattiḥ  || bhāṣya 3.11:12
* ācārāṇāṃ vā ko vistāraḥ  || bhāṣya 3.11:13
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 3.11:14

**********************************************

krātheta vā  || 3.12 ||

* atra yadā prāptajñānaḥ kṣīṇakaluṣaśca kṛtābhyanujñaḥ tadā ācāryasakāśānniṣkramyāgatya pratyagāraṃ nagaraṃ vā praviśya yatra laukikānāṃ samūhastatra teṣāṃ nātidūre nātisaṃnikarṣe yatra ca teṣāṃ noparodho dṛṣṭinipātaśca bhavati tatra hastyaśvarathapadātīnāṃ panthānaṃ varjayitvopaviśya nidrāliṅgaśiraścalitajṛmbhikādīni prayoktavyāni  || bhāṣya 3.12:1
* tatraivānenāsuptena supta iva bhavitavyam  || bhāṣya 3.12:2
* tataḥ prāṇarecanasya vāyoḥ kaṇṭhadeśe purupuruśabdaḥ kartavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.12:3
* tataste manasā vā vācā vā nidrāviṣṭo'yamiti laukikāḥ prapadyante paribhavanti ca  || bhāṣya 3.12:4
* anenānṛtābhiyogenāsya yatteṣāṃ sukṛtaṃ tadāgacchati  || bhāṣya 3.12:5
* asyāpi ca yatpāpaṃ tattān prati  || bhāṣya 3.12:6
* evaṃ krāthanamiti kriyā  || bhāṣya 3.12:7
* ita ityabhiyajana ājñāyāṃ niyoge ca  || bhāṣya 3.12:8
* vāśabdaḥ krāthanaspandanādivibhāge draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.12:9
* āha avibhaktābhidhānādeva krāthanaspandanādīnāṃ vibhāgasiddhiḥ hasitādivat || bhāṣya 3.12:10
* kasmādbhāvivacanaṃ bhavati  || bhāṣya 3.12:11
* ucyate pṛthagabhidhāne satyapi hasitagītanṛtyavatsaṃdehaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.12:12
* pṛthagabhidhāne satyapi hasitagītayoḥ pṛthakpṛthakprayogaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.12:13
* atra vibhaktayostu gītanṛtyayoḥ  || bhāṣya 3.12:14
* adhyayanayantraṇayośca  || bhāṣya 3.12:15
* tasmātpṛthagabhidhānamanurato vā vibhāge bhavatānyadoṣaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.12:16
* tataḥ krāthanakriyayāvamānādiṣu niṣpanneṣu krāthanādi saṃsṛṣṭaṃ samutsṛjya śīghramutthātavyam  || bhāṣya 3.12:17
* yathā laukikānāṃ sampratyayo bhavati kimapyanena svapnāntare bhayaṃ dṛṣṭamiti  || bhāṣya 3.12:18
* ata utthāya śiraḥpāṇyādīnāmanyatamaṃ spanditavyam  || bhāṣya 3.12:19
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 3.12:20

**********************************************

spandeta vā  || 3.13 ||

* atra spandanamiti jñānecchāmadhikurute  || bhāṣya 3.13:1
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 3.13:2
* jñānecchāprayatnapūrvakaṃ śarīrāvayavāḥ spandayitavyāḥ  || bhāṣya 3.13:3
* draṣṭāro hi vāyusaṃsṛṣṭo'yamiti laukikāḥ pratipadyante paribhavanti ca  || bhāṣya 3.13:4
* anenānṛtābhiyogenāsya tatpuṇyamāgacchati asyāpi ca yatpāpaṃ tān gacchati  || bhāṣya 3.13:5
* evaṃ spandanamiti kriyā  || bhāṣya 3.13:6
* ita ityabhiyajana ājñāyāṃ niyoge ca  || bhāṣya 3.13:7
* evamādisādhane sati vā vikalpe raudrībahurūpīvat || bhāṣya 3.13:8
* avasthānakrāthanotthānaspandanādau vāśabdo draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.13:9
* āha abhiprasthitasya dharmasādhanaṃ kimasti neti  || bhāṣya 3.13:10
* ucyate asti  || bhāṣya 3.13:11
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 3.13:12

**********************************************

maṇṭeta vā  || 3.14 ||

* veti pādavaikalyamadhikurute  || bhāṣya 3.14:1
* maṇṭane ca prayukte vaktāro vadantyupahatamasya pādendriyam  || bhāṣya 3.14:2
* kṛtsnasyāśubhasya ca vṛttirasmiñcharīre upalabhyate  || bhāṣya 3.14:3
* uktaṃ hi | dāridryaṃ vyādhibhūyiṣṭhatā mūrkhatvaṃ cārūpatā bhraṃśatāpi | dehotpattirvarṇahīne kule vā pratyādeśaḥ karmaṇāṃ duṣkṛtānām  || bhāṣya 3.14:4
* draṣṭāro hi upahatapāda iti prapadyante paribhavanti ca  || bhāṣya 3.14:5
* anenānṛtābhiyogena yatteṣāṃ sukṛtaṃ tadasyāgacchati  || bhāṣya 3.14:6
* asyāpi ca yatpāpaṃ tān gacchati  || bhāṣya 3.14:7
* evaṃ maṇṭanamiti kriyā  || bhāṣya 3.14:8
* ita ityabhiyajane ājñāyāṃ niyoge ca  || bhāṣya 3.14:9
* vāśabdaḥ krāthanaspandanamaṇṭanādivibhāge draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.14:10
* āha strīṣvadhikārikarmasādhanaṃ kimasti neti  || bhāṣya 3.14:11
* ucyate asti  || bhāṣya 3.14:12
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 3.14:13

**********************************************

śṛṅgāreta vā  || 3.15 ||

* atra śṛṅgāraṇamiti bhāvaprasādamadhikurute  || bhāṣya 3.15:1
* katham  || bhāṣya 3.15:2
* strījanasamūhasyānuparodhe nātidūre nātisaṃnikarṣe adhidṛṣṭinipāte sthitvaikāṃ rūpayauvanasampannāṃ striyamadhikṛtyālocanasaṃkalpādhyavasāyābhimānādayaḥ prayoktavyāḥ  || bhāṣya 3.15:3
* ayuktā cecchāvaloke hi sati keśasaṃyamanādīni kāmaliṅgāni prayoktavyāni  || bhāṣya 3.15:4
* tataḥ strīpuṃnapuṃsakādayo vaktāro vadantyabrahmacārī kāmyayamiti  || bhāṣya 3.15:5
* anenānṛtābhiyogenāsya yatteṣāṃ sukṛtaṃ tadāgacchati asyāpi yatpāpaṃ tatteṣāṃ gacchatyeva  || bhāṣya 3.15:6
* śṛṅgāraṇamiti kriyā  || bhāṣya 3.15:7
* ita ityabhiyajane ājñāyāṃ niyoge ca vāśabdaḥ krāthanaspandanamaṇṭanaśṛṅgāraṇādikriyāntarāṇāṃ vikalpe  || bhāṣya 3.15:8
* kriyānyatvāccāpunarukto'yaṃ vāśabdo draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.15:9
* āha krāthanādikriyācatuṣkaṃ yasya nāsti tasya sāmānyatvāt || bhāṣya 3.15:10
* uktaṃ hi | ye hi vai dīkṣitaṃ yajamānaṃ pṛṣṭhato'pavadanti te tasya pāpmānamabhivrajanti  || bhāṣya 3.15:11
* pṛṣṭhataḥ padamagrataḥ pārśvataśca yojyam  || bhāṣya 3.15:12
* tasmātkrātheta vā spandeta vā maṇṭeta vā śṛṅgāreta vā  || bhāṣya 3.15:13
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 3.15:14
* atiyajanādiviśeṣitatvāt || bhāṣya 3.15:15
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 3.15:16
* sarvajñavacanādarthāvisaṃvāditvācca lokāparigrahābhāvaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.15:17
* āha kriyācatuṣkamevātra kartavyamiti  || bhāṣya 3.15:18
* ucyate na  || bhāṣya 3.15:19
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 3.15:20

**********************************************

apitatkuryāt || 3.16 ||

* atra apiśabdaḥ krāthanādisarvakriyāsamuccayavacane  || bhāṣya 3.16:1
* taditi anaikānte  || bhāṣya 3.16:2
* kuryāditi kuśalāṃ hāsavṛttimadhikurute  || bhāṣya 3.16:3
* yamānāmavirodhināṃ śuvirūpakāṇāṃ dravyāṇāṃ kāṣṭhaloṣṭādīnāṃ grahaṇadhāraṇasaṃsparśanādīni kartavyāni  || bhāṣya 3.16:4
* tataste vaktāro vadanti asamyakkārī śucyaśucyoḥ kāryākāryayoravibhāgajña iti  || bhāṣya 3.16:5
* anenānṛtābhiyogenāsya dharmādharmayośca hānādānaśuddhirbhavati  || bhāṣya 3.16:6
* āha kiṃ kriyāpañcakamevātra kartavyam  || bhāṣya 3.16:7
* taducyate na  || bhāṣya 3.16:8
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 3.16:9

**********************************************

apitadbhāṣet || 3.17 ||

* atra apiśabdaḥ sarvendriyavṛttyapādānasambhāvane  || bhāṣya 3.17:1
* taditi anaikānte  || bhāṣya 3.17:2
* bhāṣediti vākyavṛttimadhikurute  || bhāṣya 3.17:3
* itpadamapārthakaṃ punaruktaṃ vyāhataṃ bhāṣitavyamiti  || bhāṣya 3.17:4
* tataste vaktāro vadanti asamyagvādī vācyāvācyayoravibhāgajña iti  || bhāṣya 3.17:5
* anenānṛtābhiyogenāsya dharmādharmayostyāgādānaśuddhirbhavati  || bhāṣya 3.17:6
* āha kiṃ hasitādivadyathāpāṭhakrameṇaiva krāthanādayaḥ prayoktavyāḥ  || bhāṣya 3.17:7
* kiṃ vā prayojanaṃ kartavyam  || bhāṣya 3.17:8
* taducyate paribhavādiniṣpattyartham  || bhāṣya 3.17:9
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 3.17:10

**********************************************

yena paribhavaṃ gacchet || 3.18 ||

* atra yacchabdaḥ atikrāntāpekṣaṇe vīpsāyāṃ ca  || bhāṣya 3.18:1
* paribhavaḥ pūrvoktaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.18:2
* gacchedityavamānaparibhavaparivādāḥ prāptavyā ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.18:3
* evamatra vyaktācārasamāsoktānāṃ krāthanādīnāmācārāṇāṃ vistaravibhāgaviśeṣopasaṃhārādayaśca vyākhyātā ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.18:4
* āha kiyantaṃ kālaṃ paribhavādayaḥ prāptavyāḥ  || bhāṣya 3.18:5
* kīdṛśena vā  || bhāṣya 3.18:6
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 3.18:7

**********************************************

paribhūyamāno hi vidvān kṛtsnatapā bhavati  || 3.19 ||

* atra pari sarvatobhāve  || bhāṣya 3.19:1
* bhūya ityanekaśo'vamānādayaḥ prāptavyāḥ  || bhāṣya 3.19:2
* māna ityasya pūrvokto'rthaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.19:3
* hiśabdaḥ kṛtsnatapautkarṣe  || bhāṣya 3.19:4
* utkarṣāpekṣo draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.19:5
* vidyā nāma yā granthārthavartipadārthānāmabhivyañjikā vipratvalakṣaṇā  || bhāṣya 3.19:6
* nyāyātpadārthānāmadhigatapratyayo lābhamalopāyābhijñaḥ vidvānityucyate  || bhāṣya 3.19:7
* kṛtsnamiti prayogaprāptau paryāptimadhikurute na tu harṣādiprāptāvityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.19:8
* kṛtsnatapāḥ paryāptatapāḥ sādhaka ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.19:9
* bhavati iti bhūtārthavādo niḥsaṃśayam  || bhāṣya 3.19:10
* yadā yamaniyameṣu dṛḍho bhūtvā krāthanādīn prayuṅkte tadā kṛtsnatapā bhavati  || bhāṣya 3.19:11
* kṛtsnasya tapaso lakṣaṇamātmapratyakṣaṃ veditavyam  || bhāṣya 3.19:12
* evamadhyāyaparisamāptiṃ kṛtvā yuktaṃ vaktum  || bhāṣya 3.19:13

**********************************************

atredaṃ brahma japet || 3.20 ||

* iti  || bhāṣya 3.20:1
* asya pūrvokto'rthaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.20:2
* kiṃpunastadbrahmeti  || bhāṣya 3.20:3
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 3.20:4

**********************************************

aghorebhyaḥ  || 3.21 ||

* athavā brahmaṇā saha brahmasambandho bhavati  || bhāṣya 3.21:1
* katham  || bhāṣya 3.21:2
* manograhaṇādrūpādivihīnā arthāḥ  || bhāṣya 3.21:3
* kiṃ tāni surūpāṇi salakṣaṇāni vilakṣaṇāni uta salakṣaṇavilakṣaṇānīti  || bhāṣya 3.21:4
* kiṃ parimitāni uta aparimitāni uta parimitāparimitāni  || bhāṣya 3.21:5
* ucyate kāraṇatvabahutvenoktasya bhagavato rūpanānātvaṃ vailakṣaṇyāvailakṣaṇyaṃ parimitāparimitatvaṃ cocyate aghorebhyaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.21:6
* akāro rūpāṇāṃ ghoratvaṃ pratiṣedhati  || bhāṣya 3.21:7
* aghorāṇyatiśāntāni anugrahakarāṇītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.21:8
* ebhya ityaparimitāparisaṃkhyātebhya ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.21:9
* āha kimetānyeva ebhya eva vā  || bhāṣya 3.21:10
* taducyate na  || bhāṣya 3.21:11
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 3.21:12

**********************************************

atha ghorebhyaḥ  || 3.22 ||

* atra athaśabdo ghorarūpopādhiko draṣṭavyaḥ ghorāṇi aśivāni aśāntāni ananugrahakārīṇītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.22:1
* ebhya ityaparimitāparisaṃkhyātebhya ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.22:2
* āha kimetānyeva dvisaṃsthānasaṃsthebhya eva vā  || bhāṣya 3.22:3
* ucyate yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 3.22:4

**********************************************

ghoraghoratarebhyaśca  || 3.23 ||

* atra ghora ityetadbhagavato nāmadheyam  || bhāṣya 3.23:1
* dvitīyo ghoraśabdo rūpeṣu draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.23:2
* tara viśeṣaṇe  || bhāṣya 3.23:3
* śānatarādivat || bhāṣya 3.23:4
* tebhyo ghorebhyo'ghorebhyaśca yānyanyāni paśūnāṃ sammohakarāṇi tāni ghoratarāṇītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.23:5
* ebhya ityaparimitāsaṃkhyātebhya ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.23:6
* caśabdo ghorāghorarūpopasaṃhāre draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.23:7
* etānyeva trisaṃkhyāni rūpāṇi nānyānītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.23:8
* āha kutastāni rūpāṇi karoti  || bhāṣya 3.23:9
* kutrasthāni vā  || bhāṣya 3.23:10
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 3.23:11

**********************************************

sarvebhyaḥ  || 3.24 ||

* atra yāni rūpakāraṇe  || bhāṣya 3.24:1
* sarvatvaṃ kasmāt || bhāṣya 3.24:2
* sarvatrānavakāśadoṣātsūcyagre dvinābhīyabadaravat || bhāṣya 3.24:3
* kiṃtu kāraṇaśakteravyāhatatvācca  || bhāṣya 3.24:4
* sarvatra tānītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.24:5
* āha rūpakaraṇe karaṇeṣvasyāpratighāta iti kva siddham  || bhāṣya 3.24:6
* taducyate iha  || bhāṣya 3.24:7
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 3.24:8

**********************************************

śarvasarvebhyaḥ  || 3.25 ||
* atra śarva ityetadbhagavato nāmadheyam  || bhāṣya 3.25:1
* śarvaḥ kasmāt || bhāṣya 3.25:2
* vidyādikāryasya śaraṇāccharva ityucyate  || bhāṣya 3.25:3
* sarvaṃ vidyādikāryaṃ rudrastham  || bhāṣya 3.25:4
* sarvasmiṃśca bhagavāṃścodakaḥ kāraṇatvena sarvatra  || bhāṣya 3.25:5
* sarvaśabdaḥ trisaṃkhyeṣvapi eṣu niravaśeṣavācī draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.25:6
* ebhya ityaparimitāsaṃkhyebhya ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.25:7
* āha athaitāṃ rūpavibhūtiṃ jñātvā sādhakena kiṃ kartavyam  || bhāṣya 3.25:8
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 3.25:9

**********************************************

namaste astu rudrarūpebhyaḥ  || 3.26 ||

* atra nama ityātmaprayukta ityarthaḥ te iti kāraṇāpadeśe  || bhāṣya 3.26:1
* namastubhyaṃ namaste  || bhāṣya 3.26:2
* athavā namaskāreṇātmānaṃ pradāya dharmapracayaparigrahamicchanti  || bhāṣya 3.26:3
* atha katamo'yaṃ parigrahaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.26:4
* taducyate viśiṣṭe parigrahāt || bhāṣya 3.26:5
* taducyate'tra rudra iti kāraṇāpadeśe  || bhāṣya 3.26:6
* rudrasya rudratvaṃ pūrvoktam  || bhāṣya 3.26:7
* rūpāṇi yāni śarīrāṇyutpādayati tebhyo rūpebhya ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.26:8
* atra rūpavyapadeśena rūpiṇi namaskāro draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.26:9
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 3.26:10
* tadabhisaṃdhiprayogāt || bhāṣya 3.26:11
* śivapuri upasthānavat || bhāṣya 3.26:12
* ebhya iti  || bhāṣya 3.26:13
* aparimitāsaṃkhyātebhya ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 3.26:14
* rūpanirdeśārthānyatvācca punaruktāpunaruktābhyāṃ śabdā draṣṭavyāḥ  || bhāṣya 3.26:15

**********************************************


ড়āśupatasūtra, 4

gūḍhavidyā tapaānantyāya prakāśate  || 4.1 ||

* guhū rakṣaṇe  || bhāṣya 4.1:1
* rakṣitavyā na prakāśayitavyetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.1:2
* gopanaṃ nāmāprakāśanam  || bhāṣya 4.1:3
* vidyā pūrvoktā svaparānyaprakāśikā pradīpavat || bhāṣya 4.1:4
* liṅgairgopyā rūḍhavidyā sādhake ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.1:5
* āha gūḍhavidye sādhake kā kāryaniṣpattiḥ  || bhāṣya 4.1:6
* taducyate tapaānantyāya prakāśate ityeṣa pāṭhaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.1:7
* athavā kuravonmahitavattapo'nantyāya prakāśata ityeṣa vā pāṭhaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.1:8
* tasmādatra tapastadeva  || bhāṣya 4.1:9
* niruktamasya pūrvoktam  || bhāṣya 4.1:10
* anityapi niyogaparyāyaḥ gamyate  || bhāṣya 4.1:11
* tapaḥkāryatvādatigatisāyujyavat || bhāṣya 4.1:12
* āha kiṃ parimiteṣvartheṣvānantyaśabdaḥ utāparimiteṣu kiṃ vā parimitāparimiteṣviti  || bhāṣya 4.1:13
* ucyate parimitāparimiteṣvartheṣu ānantyaśabdaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.1:14
* tatra tāvadīśvarasyaikaikaśaḥ parimiteṣu teṣveva vibhutvādaparimiteṣu tathā parimitāparimiteṣvartheṣu abhivyaktāsya śaktiḥ  || bhāṣya 4.1:15
* tasya kuśalākuśaleṣu bhāveṣvānantyaśabdaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.1:16
* yasmāduktaṃ na caitāstanavaḥ kevalaṃ mama iti  || bhāṣya 4.1:17
* tathā rudraḥ samudro hi ananto bhāskaro nabhaḥ | ātmā brahma ca vākcaiva na śakyaṃ bhedadarśanam  || bhāṣya 4.1:18
* anātmavidbhiradhyastāṃ paṅktiṃ yojanamāyatām | vedavitpunate pārtha niyuktaḥ paṅktimūrdhani  || bhāṣya 4.1:19
* tathā ca vedavitpaṅktimātmavitpunate dvijaḥ | ānantyaṃ punate vidvānnābhātvaṃ yo na paśyati  || bhāṣya 4.1:20
* ānantyāya iti caturthī tasmāttapa etatna tu vidyā kāryā  || bhāṣya 4.1:21
* prakāśo nāma bhāvaprakāśyaṃ na tu pradīpavat || bhāṣya 4.1:22
* katham  || bhāṣya 4.1:23
* yogādhikṛtasya pradīpasthānītayamānaijasthānīyamāvārakamabhibhūya prakāśate  || bhāṣya 4.1:24
* cakṣuḥsthānīyayā vidyayā kuśalavivekādikāryaṃ māhātmyātigatiprakāśapravṛttismṛtisāyujyasthityādiprakāśanaṃ tapaḥkāryamityarthaḥ evaṃ ca gupte brāhmaṇe tapa ānantyāya prakāśata ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.1:25
* āha svabhāvaguptatvādatīndriyātmagatī vidyā gopyeti  || bhāṣya 4.1:26
* taducyate avyaktapretādyavasthānairliṅgairgopyā ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.1:27
* āha kāni punastāni vidyāliṅgāni yairguptairvidyā guptā bhavati  || bhāṣya 4.1:28
* taducyate vratādīni  || bhāṣya 4.1:29
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 4.1:30

**********************************************

gūḍhavrataḥ  || 4.2 ||

* atra gūḍhaṃ pracchannamaprakāśamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.2:1
* vrataṃ nāma yadāyatane snānahasitādyaḥ sādhanavargastadvratam  || bhāṣya 4.2:2
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 4.2:3
* sākṛtatvādyasmādayaṃ brāhmaṇastathā prayuṅkte yathā laukikānāṃ dharmasādhanabhāvo na vidyata iti ato gūḍhavrata iti  || bhāṣya 4.2:4
* āha avyaktapretatvādeva gūḍhatvaprāpteḥ punaruktamiti  || bhāṣya 4.2:5
* ucyate'rthānyatvādapunaruktam  || bhāṣya 4.2:6
* tatrāvasthānamātramevāvyaktam  || bhāṣya 4.2:7
* iha tu snānahasitādigopanam  || bhāṣya 4.2:8
* api ca tatra niṣpannaṃ liṅgamavyaktam  || bhāṣya 4.2:9
* iha tu niṣpattikāle ca gopanopadeśaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.2:10
* na vāvyaktapretatvaṃ vā vidyāliṅgam  || bhāṣya 4.2:11
* ataścāpunaruktam  || bhāṣya 4.2:12
* tasmādgūḍhavratopadeśāya sthānāpadeśāpavādāya sthāne vastavyam  || bhāṣya 4.2:13
* snānahasitādayaśca gūḍhāḥ kartavyāḥ  || bhāṣya 4.2:14
* evaṃ vidyā guptā bhavatītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.2:15
* āha kiṃ vratamevaikaṃ vidyāliṅgaṃ gopyamāhosvidanyadapyasti neti  || bhāṣya 4.2:16
* ucyate'sti  || bhāṣya 4.2:17
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 4.2:18

**********************************************

gūḍhapavitravāṇiḥ  || 4.3 ||

* atra gūḍhā guptā pracchannā aprakāśetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.3:1
* pavitrā nāma satyā saṃskṛtā arghyahetuḥ sampannā na tu viparītetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.3:2
* sā gopyā  || bhāṣya 4.3:3
* kimarthamiti cet || bhāṣya 4.3:4
* taducyate jātijñānatapaḥstavasūcanārtham  || bhāṣya 4.3:5
* yathā śaradaṃ kuraraḥ sūcayati  || bhāṣya 4.3:6
* uktaṃ hi | śaradaṃ kuraraḥ prāha vasantaṃ prāha kokilaḥ | prāha varṣā mayūraśca vākpavitrāha brāhmaṇam  || bhāṣya 4.3:7
* tathā | vāgeva hi manuṣyasya śrutamākhyāti bhāṣitā | dīpayantī yathā sarvaṃ prabhā bhānumivāmalā  || bhāṣya 4.3:8
* ato jātijñānatapaḥstavā bhavanti  || bhāṣya 4.3:9
* stavite cāvasānādimattvayoḥ puṇyapāpakṣayavṛddhyorabhāvaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.3:10
* ata etaduktaṃ gūḍhapavitravāṇiriti  || bhāṣya 4.3:11
* āha kiṃ vrataṃ vāṇī ca dvayamevātra gopyamāhosvidanyadapyasti neti  || bhāṣya 4.3:12
* ucyate asti  || bhāṣya 4.3:13
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 4.3:14

**********************************************

sarvāṇi dvārāṇi pidhāya  || 4.4 ||

* iti  || bhāṣya 4.4:1
* atra sarvaśabdo dvāraprakṛterniravaśeṣavācī draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.4:2
* dvārāṇi krāthanādīni  || bhāṣya 4.4:3
* dvārāṇi ca kasmāt || bhāṣya 4.4:4
* dharmādharmayorāyavyayahetutvāddvārāṇi  || bhāṣya 4.4:5
* dvārāṇīti bahuvacanaṃ vijñānayantrendriyavat || bhāṣya 4.4:6
* pidhāya iti prāksādhanaprayogamadhikurute  || bhāṣya 4.4:7
* katham  || bhāṣya 4.4:8
* asthānakāladeśakriyāprayogaprayojanāntarāṇi vidhivadvivecya yadā samyaṅmāyayā saṃnādyabhedakrameṇa prayuktāni tadā pihitāni bhavantītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.4:9
* āha kena tāni pidheyāni  || bhāṣya 4.4:10
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 4.4:11

**********************************************

buddhyā  || 4.5 ||

* yasmādasya śrotrendriyavatpidhāyeti samyagjñānaprayoge sarvajñena bhagavatā vidyānugṛhītayā buddhyā pidhānamuktaṃ tasmādatra karaṇākhyā buddhyeti na jñānākhyā  || bhāṣya 4.5:1
* kathaṃ gamyate  || bhāṣya 4.5:2
* buddhyeti tṛtīyāprayogāt || bhāṣya 4.5:3
* bhasmanā snānavat || bhāṣya 4.5:4
* na jñānākhyā  || bhāṣya 4.5:5
* yasya vijñānākhyā buddhirna jñānākhyā kathaṃ gamyate  || bhāṣya 4.5:6
* tasya prāgjñānotpatteracetanapuruṣastasya hyajñānādasaṃbodhyaḥ syāt || bhāṣya 4.5:7
* tasmādatra trikaṃ cintyate pidhātā pidhānaṃ pidheyamiti tatra pidhātā sādhakaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.5:8
* pidhānamasya vidyānugṛhītā buddhiḥ  || bhāṣya 4.5:9
* pidheyaṃ vrataṃ vāṇī dvārāṇi ceti  || bhāṣya 4.5:10
* kramavṛttitvācca buddhereva prayuṅkte śeṣāṇyakartṛtvenaivāprayuktāni  || bhāṣya 4.5:11
* tadā pihitāni bhavantītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.5:12
* atredaṃ vidyājñānaprakaraṇaṃ parisamāptamiti  || bhāṣya 4.5:13
* āha kimavyaktapreta ityavasthānadvayamevātra kartavyam  || bhāṣya 4.5:14
* vāgādīni vā gopāyitvā sādhakena kiṃ kartavyam  || bhāṣya 4.5:15
* ucyate  || bhāṣya 4.5:16

**********************************************

unmattavadeko vicareta loke  || 4.6 ||

* atra buddhyā antaḥkaraṇānāṃ śrotrādīnāṃ ca bāhyānāṃ vṛttivibhramaḥ kartavyo'sati viṣaye viṣayagrahaṇam  || bhāṣya 4.6:1
* atra | pañconmādāḥ samākhyātā vātapittakaphātmakāḥ | caturthaḥ saṃnipātastu abhighātastu pañcamaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.6:2
* evaṃ grahabahutve sati yo'yaṃ vātapittaśleṣmaṇāṃ samūhaḥ sāṃnipātiko'yaṃ mahāgrahaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.6:3
* evaṃ sādhanaprayogaḥ kartavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.6:4
* tatra yadi kaścidjñānajijñāsanārthaṃ dayārthamanugrahārthaṃ vā pṛcchati taṃ nivartayitvā brūyātsamayataḥ praviśasveti  || bhāṣya 4.6:5
* tato dvāreṇa praviśya viparītamaviparītaṃ vā yadi kaścidbrūyātko bhavāniti tato vaktavyaṃ māheśvaro'haṃ kaumāro'hamiti duratyayaṃ kṛtaṃ ca mamāneneti  || bhāṣya 4.6:6
* tato jighāṃsanārthaṃ mayā spṛṣṭo na tu viṣayakrīḍārthaṃ vā  || bhāṣya 4.6:7
* tataḥ parivarjayati ityevaṃ laukikaparīkṣakāṇāṃ sammohanārthamuktamunmattavaditi  || bhāṣya 4.6:8
* kiṃcidunmattapretavattasyāntaḥkaraṇādivṛttivibhramamātraṃ parigṛhyate  || bhāṣya 4.6:9
* eka ityasaṃvahatā cintyate  || bhāṣya 4.6:10
* ekenetarebhyo vicchinnenāsahāyenetyarthaḥ āha ekena kiṃ kartavyamiti  || bhāṣya 4.6:11
* ucyate vihartavyam  || bhāṣya 4.6:12
* yasmādāha vicareta  || bhāṣya 4.6:13
* atra virvistare  || bhāṣya 4.6:14
* caretyārjanamadhikurute dharmārjane  || bhāṣya 4.6:15
* īta ityājñāyāṃ niyoge ca  || bhāṣya 4.6:16
* vistaraniyoga viśeṣataśca vihartavyamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.6:17
* āha kva vihartavyamiti  || bhāṣya 4.6:18
* ucyate loke  || bhāṣya 4.6:19
* trivarṇāśramiṣu lokasaṃjñā na tu brahmalokādiṣu  || bhāṣya 4.6:20
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 4.6:21
* utkṛṣṭeṣvasaṃbhavāt || bhāṣya 4.6:22
* loke iti sāmīpikaṃ saṃnidhānam  || bhāṣya 4.6:23
* paravarṇā lokāsteṣu tadadhyakṣeṣu vihartavyamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.6:24
* āha kāṃ vṛttimāsthāya loke vihartavyaṃ sarvabhakṣameva  || bhāṣya 4.6:25
* ucyate na  || bhāṣya 4.6:26
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 4.6:27

**********************************************

kṛtānnamutsṛṣṭamupādadīta  || 4.7 ||

* atra kṛtagrahaṇādakṛtānāṃ bījakāṇḍaphalādīnāṃ pratiṣedhaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.7:1
* kṛtaṃ bhinnodbhinnādyaṃ tadbhaikṣamutsṛṣṭaṃ yathālabdhaṃ vidhinā prāptamupayojyam  || bhāṣya 4.7:2
* atra kṛtagrahaṇādakṛtapratiṣedhaḥ akṛtapratiṣedhācca kṛtsnā hiṃsā tantre pratiṣiddhā draṣṭavyā  || bhāṣya 4.7:3
* āha kiṃ tatkṛtaṃ nāma buddhighaṭādyam  || bhāṣya 4.7:4
* taducyate na  || bhāṣya 4.7:5
* yasmādāha annam  || bhāṣya 4.7:6
* tatrānnavacanādanannapratiṣedhaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.7:7
* tacca dviyoni indrābhiṣiktamindriyābhiṣiktaṃ ca tatrendrābhiṣiktaṃ vrīhiyavādyam  || bhāṣya 4.7:8
* indriyābhiṣiktaṃ tu māṃsam  || bhāṣya 4.7:9
* tatpañcavidhaṃ bhakṣyaṃ bhojyaṃ lehyaṃ peyaṃ coṣyamiti  || bhāṣya 4.7:10
* tathā ṣaḍrasaṃ madhurāmlalavaṇatiktakaṭukaṣāyamiti  || bhāṣya 4.7:11
* āha tasya kṛtānnasyārjanaṃ kutaḥ kartavyam  || bhāṣya 4.7:12
* taducyate utsṛṣṭam  || bhāṣya 4.7:13
* atrotsṛṣṭagrahaṇādbhaikṣayathālabdhapratiṣedhaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.7:14
* kiṃ kāraṇam  || bhāṣya 4.7:15
* sūnādidoṣaparihārārthatvānnasteyapratigrahādidoṣāt || bhāṣya 4.7:16
* tacca trividhamutsṛṣṭam  || bhāṣya 4.7:17
* tadyathā nisṛṣṭaṃ visṛṣṭamatisṛṣṭamiti  || bhāṣya 4.7:18
* tatra sanimittaṃ parityaktamannaṃ pānaṃ vā tannisṛṣṭam  || bhāṣya 4.7:19
* gobrāhmaṇādinimittaṃ tyaktaṃ visṛṣṭam  || bhāṣya 4.7:20
* atisṛṣṭamanyataḥ parityaktam  || bhāṣya 4.7:21
* dayārthamānṛśaṃsārthaṃ vā yadi kaściddadyāttadapi grāhyameva  || bhāṣya 4.7:22
* āha anena sādhakena kiṃ kartavyamiti  || bhāṣya 4.7:23
* ucyate upayoktavyam  || bhāṣya 4.7:24
* yasmādāha upādadīta atropetyabhyupagame  || bhāṣya 4.7:25
* atyantāsanmānasayantrasthenetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.7:26
* ādadīta ityupayoge grahaṇe ca vivakṣitasūtragrahaṇe tvāvadbhavati  || bhāṣya 4.7:27
* taduktam | saṃcitvā naramevainaṃ kāmānāmavitṛptikam | vyāghraḥ paśumivādāya mṛtyurādāya gacchati  || bhāṣya 4.7:28
* iti  || bhāṣya 4.7:29
* upayoge'pi nāthakaṇādavatsam  || bhāṣya 4.7:30
* tasmādupayoktavyamiti  || bhāṣya 4.7:31
* īta ityājñāyāṃ niyoge ca  || bhāṣya 4.7:32
* tadutsṛṣṭaṃ vidhiprāptamupayoktavyam  || bhāṣya 4.7:33
* anyathā hi vidhivyapetena krameṇa vṛttyarjanaṃ na kartavyamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.7:34
* āha vratādīni gopayitvā samyaksādhanaprayoge utsṛṣṭopayoge ca vartataḥ ke vārthā niṣpadyante  || bhāṣya 4.7:35
* asanmānaprakaraṇasya vā parisamāptiḥ kimasti neti  || bhāṣya 4.7:36
* ucyate asti  || bhāṣya 4.7:37
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 4.7:38

**********************************************

unmatto mūḍha ityevaṃ manyante itare janāḥ  || 4.8 ||

* atra unmattaḥ sa eva  || bhāṣya 4.8:1
* niruktamasya pūrvoktaṃ mūḍha iti  || bhāṣya 4.8:2
* muhu aparijñāne  || bhāṣya 4.8:3
* avyakto'yaṃ preto'yamunmatto'yaṃ mūḍho'yaṃ mūrkho'yamiti vaktāro vadantītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.8:4
* itiśabdo'rthānāṃ nirvacanatvātprakaraṇaparisamāptyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.8:5
* evaṃ yasmādavasthānakāladeśakriyāprayogaprayojanagopanavasatyarthakṛtsnatapāṃsi ca vyākhyātāni  || bhāṣya 4.8:6
* evamityatikrāntāpekṣaṇe  || bhāṣya 4.8:7
* manyate ityavadhāraṇe  || bhāṣya 4.8:8
* itare iti gṛhasthabrahmacārivānaprasthabhikṣupāṣaṇḍināṃ brahmacaryādhikṛtānāṃ grahaṇam  || bhāṣya 4.8:9
* janā iti  || bhāṣya 4.8:10
* janī prādurbhāve  || bhāṣya 4.8:11
* janā iti varṇāśramiṇāṃ janānāmadhikṛtānāṃ grahaṇamuktaṃ hi | janena hi jano jātaḥ janaṃ janayase jana | janaṃ śocasi nātmānaṃ mātmānaṃ śoca mā janam  || bhāṣya 4.8:12
* ityevaṃ vaktāro vadantītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.8:13
* atredamādhikārikamasanmānacariprakaraṇaṃ samāptam  || bhāṣya 4.8:14
* āha vratādīni gopayitvā samyaksādhanaprayoge utsṛṣṭopayoge ca tataḥ ko guṇaḥ yaṃ guṇaṃ jñātvā avyaktapretonmattādyā vādā niṣpādyā iti  || bhāṣya 4.8:15
* taducyate taṃ guṇaṃ jñātvā vakṣyāmaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.8:16
* api ca avyaktapretonmattādyaṃ brāhmaṇakarmaviruddhaṃ kramaṃ dṛṣṭvā yāvadayaṃ śiṣyaḥ enamarthaṃ na bravīti tattasya hṛdisthamaśaṅkitamupalabhyottaraṃ brūma iti kṛtvā bhagavānidaṃ sūtramuvāca  || bhāṣya 4.8:17

**********************************************

asanmāno hi yantrāṇāṃ sarveṣāmuttamaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 4.9 ||

* atra akāro mānapratiṣedhe  || bhāṣya 4.9:1
* māno'tra dvividhaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.9:2
* jātyabhimāno gṛhasthābhimānaśca  || bhāṣya 4.9:3
* tatra jātyabhimāno nāma brāhmaṇo'hamiti  || bhāṣya 4.9:4
* pūjyatvādūrdhvagamanādīnāṃ kāryāṇāmucchritatvāttrayāṇāmapi varṇānāmupadeśena gurutvādyajñakartṛtvāttrailokyasthitihetoḥ brāhmaṇo'hamiti prathamo māno jātyutkarṣāt || bhāṣya 4.9:5
* tathā brāhmaṇānāmapi gṛhasthādīnāṃ pūjyatvāttatkṛtamānaśca  || bhāṣya 4.9:6
* etacca mānadvayamavyaktaliṅgavacanātpratiṣiddham  || bhāṣya 4.9:7
* tathā vittaṃ bandhuryaśaḥ karma vidyā bhavati pañcamī | etāni mānyasthānāni garīyo yadyaduttaram  || bhāṣya 4.9:8
* etāni caikavāsaḥpretācaraṇagūḍhavratopadeśinā sūtrataḥ pratiṣiddhānītyato māno na kartavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.9:9
* saniti praśaṃsāyāmastitve ca  || bhāṣya 4.9:10
* agre tadasanmānacariprakaraṇaviśiṣṭaṃ ca  || bhāṣya 4.9:11
* hiśabdo'yamuttamotkarṣāpekṣo draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.9:12
* yantrāṇi agniṣṭomādīni māsopavāsādīni ca gṛhasthādīnāṃ śuddhivṛddhikarāṇi  || bhāṣya 4.9:13
* yantrāṇi ca kasmāt || bhāṣya 4.9:14
* yantraṃ karmādayaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.9:15
* yasmādayantrā laukikā amaryādāvasthā bhavantītyato yantrāṇām  || bhāṣya 4.9:16
* yantrāṇāmiti ṣaṣṭhībahuvacanam  || bhāṣya 4.9:17
* āha bahuvacanaprayogātsaṃdehaḥ atha kiyatāṃ yantrāṇām  || bhāṣya 4.9:18
* taducyate sarveṣām  || bhāṣya 4.9:19
* atra sarveṣāmityaśeṣāṇāmityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.9:20
* sarveṣāmiti ṣaṣṭhībahuvacanam  || bhāṣya 4.9:21
* āha ṣaṣṭhyāḥ sākāṅkṣatvātsaṃdehaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.9:22
* teṣāṃ kāraṇātmāno vartante  || bhāṣya 4.9:23
* taducyate uttamaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.9:24
* atrottama iti śreṣṭhatve paramaviśuddhityāgādānabhāvādiṣu  || bhāṣya 4.9:25
* uktaṃ hi | vareṇyaḥ sattamo mukhyo variṣṭhaḥ śobhano'thavā | uttamaścāparārdhaśca svarthaḥ śreṣṭhārthavācakāḥ  || bhāṣya 4.9:26
* śreṣṭhaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.9:27
* iha coktamuttama iti  || bhāṣya 4.9:28
* āha asanmānaḥ sarvayantrāṇāmuttama iti kva siddham  || bhāṣya 4.9:29
* ucyate iha  || bhāṣya 4.9:30
* yasmādāha smṛtaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.9:31
* atra smṛta ityuktaparyāyaḥ maheśvareṇoktaṃ proktaṃ kathitaṃ varṇitamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.9:32
* viśiṣṭaḥ kasmāt || bhāṣya 4.9:33
* sarvajñavacanādavisaṃvāditvācca  || bhāṣya 4.9:34
* nahi pratyakṣadarśināṃ vacanāni visaṃvadantītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.9:35
* āha asmin krame uttamatvena vyākhyāyamānaṃ ka ādyaḥ śodhakaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.9:36
* kena vā idaṃ vidhānaṃ cīrṇam  || bhāṣya 4.9:37
* ācaratā vā kiṃ phalaṃ prāptam  || bhāṣya 4.9:38
* so'smatpratyayārthaṃ vācyaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.9:39
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 4.9:40

**********************************************

indro vā agre asureṣu pāśupatamacarat || 4.10 ||

* atra devatānāṃ rājā indraḥ  || bhāṣya 4.10:1
* kathaṃ gamyate asureṣvācaraṇavacanāt || bhāṣya 4.10:2
* brāhmaṇaścāyamindraḥ śreṣṭhaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.10:3
* sūtre brāhmaṇagrahaṇātśūdrapratiṣedhācca  || bhāṣya 4.10:4
* idi paramaiśvarye dhātuḥ tasyendraḥ  || bhāṣya 4.10:5
* indra utkṛṣṭaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ devagandharvayakṣarākṣasapitṛpiśācādīnāṃ śreṣṭho na tu brahmādīnām  || bhāṣya 4.10:6
* kiṃ tu svargiṇāṃ madhye aiśvaryeṇa vidyayā ājñayā cetyataḥ śreṣṭhatvādindraḥ  || bhāṣya 4.10:7
* vāśabdaḥ sambhāvane  || bhāṣya 4.10:8
* anyairapi devaśreṣṭhairidaṃ vidhānamācīrṇam  || bhāṣya 4.10:9
* kutastarhi yuṣmadādibhirmanuṣyamātraiḥ  || bhāṣya 4.10:10
* tasmātsaṃbhāvyo'yamarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.10:11
* āha kadā cīrṇamiti  || bhāṣya 4.10:12
* ucyate'gre  || bhāṣya 4.10:13
* atrāgra iti pūrvakālamadhikurute  || bhāṣya 4.10:14
* kuśikeśānasambandhātprāk || bhāṣya 4.10:15
* prathamamamaraiścīrṇam  || bhāṣya 4.10:16
* kṛtatretādvāparādiṣu yugeṣvityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.10:17
* āha keṣvācīrṇamiti  || bhāṣya 4.10:18
* ucyate asureṣu  || bhāṣya 4.10:19
* atrāsurā nāma suretarāḥ steyayuktāḥ  || bhāṣya 4.10:20
* prāṇāpaharaṇādvā asurāḥ prajāpatiputrā vijñeyāḥ  || bhāṣya 4.10:21
* asureṣviti sāmīpikaṃ saṃnidhānam  || bhāṣya 4.10:22
* asurasamīpe asurābhyāśe asurāṇāmadhyakṣa ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.10:23
* āha kiṃ taditi  || bhāṣya 4.10:24
* ucyate pāśupatam  || bhāṣya 4.10:25
* atra paśupatinoktaparigrahādhikāreṣu vartata iti pāśupatam  || bhāṣya 4.10:26
* paśupatirvāsmin cintyata iti pāśupatam  || bhāṣya 4.10:27
* paśupatiprāpakatvādvā pāśupatam  || bhāṣya 4.10:28
* pāśupatamiti samastasya sampūrṇasya vidhānasyaitadgrahaṇam  || bhāṣya 4.10:29
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 4.10:30
* vyaktaliṅgapūrvakatvādavyaktādikramasya  || bhāṣya 4.10:31
* tasmātkṛtsnamidameva vidhānamācīrṇamindreṇa duḥkhāntārthinā śuddhivṛddhyartham  || bhāṣya 4.10:32
* dharmabāhulyātsurāṇāṃ bhuvyācīrṇam  || bhāṣya 4.10:33
* acaradityatītaḥ kālaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.10:34
* atīte kāle cīrṇavānityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.10:35
* āha indreṇāsureṣvācaratā kiṃ phalaṃ prāptam  || bhāṣya 4.10:36
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 4.10:37

**********************************************

sa teṣāmiṣṭāpūrtamādatta  || 4.11 ||

* sa itīndragrahaṇaṃ teṣāmityasuranirdeśaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.11:1
* iṣṭāpūrtamiti dvaṃdvasamāsaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.11:2
* iṣṭaṃ ca pūrtaṃ ceṣṭāpūrtaṃ tatra yanmantrapūrvakeṇa vidhinā dattaṃ hutaṃ stutyādiniṣpannaṃ sukṛtaṃ tadiṣṭam  || bhāṣya 4.11:3
* yadamantrapūrvakeṇaiva tatpūrtam  || bhāṣya 4.11:4
* indreṇāsurebhyaḥ kenopāyena dattamiti  || bhāṣya 4.11:5
* ucyate  || bhāṣya 4.11:6

**********************************************

māyayā sukṛtayā samavindata  || 4.12 ||

* krāthanaspandanādiprayogaiḥ dhikkṛtasya nidrāviṣṭo vāyusaṃspṛṣṭo mandakārī asamyakkārī asamyagvādīti yo'yaṃ duṣṭaśabdo'bhiyogaśabdaśca niṣpadyate tasminnanṛte māyāsaṃjñā  || bhāṣya 4.12:1
* mānasakāyikābhiyoge ca  || bhāṣya 4.12:2
* māyayā iti tṛtīyā  || bhāṣya 4.12:3
* sukṛtayā iti  || bhāṣya 4.12:4
* su praśaṃsāyāṃ tayā sukṛtayā samyakprayuktayeti sādhakasādhanaprādhānyam  || bhāṣya 4.12:5
* avindata iti prāptau prādhānye ca  || bhāṣya 4.12:6
* sa teṣāmiṣṭāpūrtamādatteti  || bhāṣya 4.12:7
* uktaṃ hi | ākrośamāno nākrośenmanyureva titikṣati | sa teṣāṃ duṣkṛtaṃ dattvā sukṛtaṃ cāsya vindati  || bhāṣya 4.12:8
* āha uttama indraḥ  || bhāṣya 4.12:9
* sa teṣāmiṣṭāpūrtamityukte parāpadeśenāsya vṛttirnirguṇīkṛtā  || bhāṣya 4.12:10
* athātmāpadeśo'tra kimasti neti  || bhāṣya 4.12:11
* ucyate asti  || bhāṣya 4.12:12
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 4.12:13

**********************************************

nindā hyeṣānindā tasmāt || 4.13 ||

* atrāvamānaparibhavādyā nindā  || bhāṣya 4.13:1
* kutsā garhā ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.13:2
* śabdo nindottamotkarṣopakṣepe draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.13:3
* uttamādhikārādgamyate  || bhāṣya 4.13:4
* eṣā ityatikrāntāpekṣaṇe  || bhāṣya 4.13:5
* avamānaparibhavaparivādādyā nindetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.13:6
* anindā ityakāro ninditatvaṃ pratiṣedhati  || bhāṣya 4.13:7
* anindā akutsā agarhā ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.13:8
* atra tasmācchabdaḥ pūrvottaraṃ cāpekṣate  || bhāṣya 4.13:9
* tatra pūrvākāṅkṣāyāṃ tāvatkṛtsnā nindāprakaraṇaguṇavacane  || bhāṣya 4.13:10
* yasmādindrasyāpi śuddhivṛddhikāriṇī ātmāpadeśena parāpadeśena ca bhagavatā asanmānacarirguṇīkṛtā tasmādityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.13:11
* āha nirākāṅkṣānirdeśātsaṃdeho yathā yathā varṇitaṃ tathā tathā ca vyākhyātam  || bhāṣya 4.13:12
* nindāyā aninditatvaṃ guṇaṃ jñātvā sādhakena kiṃ kartavyam  || bhāṣya 4.13:13
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 4.13:14

**********************************************

nindyamānaścaret || 4.14 ||

* atra nindā pūrvoktā  || bhāṣya 4.14:1
* nindyamānenaiva nindāyāḥ vartamānakāla ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.14:2
* caredityārjanamadhikurute  || bhāṣya 4.14:3
* dharmārjane niyoge ca  || bhāṣya 4.14:4
* saṃśayānyatvāccāpunaruktaścariśabdo draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.14:5
* atredamānuṣaṅgikamasanmārgacariprakaraṇaṃ parisamāptam  || bhāṣya 4.14:6
* āha nindyamānaścaredityuktvā ādyaṃ vidhānamācarataḥ ko'rtho niṣpadyate  || bhāṣya 4.14:7
* niṣpannena vā kathamabhilapyate  || bhāṣya 4.14:8
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 4.14:9

**********************************************

aninditakarmā  || 4.15 ||

* atra caryottarasambandhādgamyate yadetadaninditaṃ karma dharmaḥ sa eva nindyamānasyācarato niṣpadyate  || bhāṣya 4.15:1
* ataḥ aninditakarmā bhavatītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.15:2
* āha nindyamānasyācarato'ninditaṃ karma bhavatīti kva siddham  || bhāṣya 4.15:3
* taducyate iha yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 4.15:4

**********************************************

sarvaviśiṣṭo'yaṃ panthāḥ  || 4.16 ||

* atra ayamiti pratyakṣe  || bhāṣya 4.16:1
* yathāyaṃ puruṣaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.16:2
* yathāvidhiścaririti yāḥ kriyāḥ atrādhikṛtasyāninditaṃ karma bhavatītyāha bhagavān  || bhāṣya 4.16:3

**********************************************

satpathaḥ  || 4.17 ||

* kasmāt | rudrasamīpaprāpaṇasāmarthyāt || bhāṣya 4.17:1
* anāvṛttiprāpaṇasāmarthyāccāvikalaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.17:2
* tasmātsarvajñavacanāvisaṃvāditvāccāyaṃ satpatha ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.17:3
* āha kimanyatra panthāno na santi iti  || bhāṣya 4.17:4
* ucyate santi  || bhāṣya 4.17:5
* kiṃtu  || bhāṣya 4.17:6

**********************************************

kupathāstvanye  || 4.18 ||

* atra ku kutsāyāṃ bhavati  || bhāṣya 4.18:1
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 4.18:2
* kuśabdaprayogādgamyate  || bhāṣya 4.18:3
* kupuruṣavat || bhāṣya 4.18:4
* panthāno vidhaya upāyā ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.18:5
* tuśabdo'nāvṛttyutkarṣe  || bhāṣya 4.18:6
* anye iti  || bhāṣya 4.18:7
* gṛhasthabrahmacārivānaprasthabhikṣupāṣāṇḍināṃ panthānaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.18:8
* te kupathāḥ  || bhāṣya 4.18:9
* na  || bhāṣya 4.18:10
* āha ayameva satpathaḥ śeṣāḥ kupathā iti kva siddham  || bhāṣya 4.18:11
* kiṃ vāsya satpathatvam  || bhāṣya 4.18:12
* śeṣāṇāṃ vā kupathatvaṃ kimiti  || bhāṣya 4.18:13
* ucyate iha  || bhāṣya 4.18:14
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 4.18:15

**********************************************

anena vidhinā rudrasamīpaṃ gatvā  || 4.19 ||

* atra anena ityanapekṣaṇe | vidhinā bhasmasnānakrāthanādinopāyenetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.19:1
* vidhineti tṛtīyā  || bhāṣya 4.19:2
* rudra iti kālopadeśe  || bhāṣya 4.19:3
* rudrasya rudratvaṃ pūrvoktam  || bhāṣya 4.19:4
* samīpamiti yogaparyāyaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.19:5
* kathaṃ gamyate  || bhāṣya 4.19:6
* vidhyanantaroktatvāt || bhāṣya 4.19:7
* sati vidhiviṣayatve puruṣeśvarayorviṣayādhikārakṛtaṃ viyogaṃ dṛṣṭvā jñānaparidṛṣṭena vidhinādhyayanadhyānādhikṛto viśuddhabhāvaḥ samīpastha ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.19:8
* gatiḥ prāptirbhāvasyetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.19:9
* tvā iti vidhikarmaṇorniṣṭhā  || bhāṣya 4.19:10
* āha atraivaṃ vidhyācaraṇaṃ samīpagamanaṃ ca kasyopadiśyate  || bhāṣya 4.19:11
* ucyate na tīrthayātrādidharmavatsarveṣām  || bhāṣya 4.19:12
* kiṃtu saṃskāravadbrāhmaṇasyaiva  || bhāṣya 4.19:13
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 4.19:14

**********************************************

na kaścidbrāhmaṇaḥ punarāvartate  || 4.20 ||

* atra nakāropadeśo'nyācaraṇapratipattipratiṣedhārthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.20:1
* kaściditi gṛhasthādyaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.20:2
* sthānamātravailakṣaṇyadarśanādbrāhmaṇeṣveva kaścicchabdaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.20:3
* gṛhastho brahmacārī vānaprastho bhikṣurekavedo dvivedastrivedaścaturvedo gāyatrīmātrasāro vānena vidhinā rudrasamīpaṃ prāptaḥ sanna kaścidbrāhmaṇaḥ punarāvartata ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.20:4
* brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ brāhmaṇyāvadhāraṇārthaṃ brāhmaṇa eva nānya ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.20:5
* kṣetrajñe ca brāhmaṇasaṃjñā  || bhāṣya 4.20:6
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 4.20:7
* upacayajanmayogātsaṃskārayogātśrutayogācca brāhmaṇaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.20:8
* punaḥśabdaḥ punarāvṛttipratiṣedhe  || bhāṣya 4.20:9
* yathā pūrvasaṃjñānādibhirgatvā āvartate punaḥ punaḥ tathānena vidhinā rudrasamīpaṃ gatvā na sakṛdāvartate  || bhāṣya 4.20:10
* punaḥ punaḥ sarvathāpi nāvartata ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.20:11
* āṅiti svaśāstroktamaryādāmadhikurute abhividhyarthaṃ ca  || bhāṣya 4.20:12
* ye cānena vidhinākṣapitājñānakaluṣapāpamāyādayaḥ kṣīṇāḥ te punaḥ punarāvartante  || bhāṣya 4.20:13
* na taiḥ saha saṃyogo bhavati  || bhāṣya 4.20:14
* na cāparaṃ janma pratipadyata ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.20:15
* evamadhyāyaparisamāptiṃ kṛtvā yuktaṃ vaktum  || bhāṣya 4.20:16

**********************************************

atredaṃ brahma japet || 4.21 ||
* asya pūrvokto'rthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.21:1
* āha kiṃ punastadbrahma  || bhāṣya 4.21:2
* taducyate kāraṇādibhāvenoktasya bhagavata ekatvaṃ sādhako jñātvā tatsādhanamārabhate  || bhāṣya 4.21:3

**********************************************

tatpuruṣāya vidmahe  || 4.22 ||

* atra pūrvaṃ kāraṇatvabahutvanānātvenopadiṣṭasya parāmarśaḥ taditi  || bhāṣya 4.22:1
* puruṣa iti pauruṣyānupūraṇācca puruṣaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.22:2
* pauruṣyamasyānekeṣu rūpeṣvavasthānāt || bhāṣya 4.22:3
* tatsaṃsthāni rūpāṇi aghorādīni  || bhāṣya 4.22:4
* tatpuruṣāyeti caturthī  || bhāṣya 4.22:5
* yathā grāmāya tattvaṃ jñātumicchati tathā puruṣāya tattvaṃ jñātumicchati  || bhāṣya 4.22:6
* vidmaha iti  || bhāṣya 4.22:7
* vida jñāne  || bhāṣya 4.22:8
* vidmahe jānīmahe upalabhāmaha ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.22:9
* āha puruṣabahutvātsaṃdehaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.22:10
* atha katamasmai puruṣāya  || bhāṣya 4.22:11
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 4.22:12

**********************************************

mahādevāya dhīmahi  || 4.23 ||

* atra mahādevatvaṃ ca pūrvoktaṃ mahādevāyeti caturthī  || bhāṣya 4.23:1
* dhīmahi iti  || bhāṣya 4.23:2
* dhīṅsaṃśleṣaṇe  || bhāṣya 4.23:3
* dhyāyemahi līyāmahe jñānakriyāśaktibhyāṃ saṃyujyāmaha ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.23:4
* atra dhī iti jñānaśaktiparyāyaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.23:5
* yayā sarvapadārthānāṃ tattvamadhigacchati sā jñānaśaktiḥ  || bhāṣya 4.23:6
* mahi iti kriyāśaktiparyāyaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.23:7
* yayā vidhiyogācaraṇasamartho bhavati sā kriyāśaktirityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.23:8
* āha athaite dṛkkriyāśaktī mahādevātsādhakaḥ kiṃ svaśaktita āsādayati āhosvitparaśaktitaḥ utobhayaśaktitaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.23:9
* taducyate paraśaktitaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.23:10
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 4.23:11

**********************************************

tanno rudraḥ pracodayāt || 4.24 ||

* taditi dṛkkriyāśaktyorgrahaṇam  || bhāṣya 4.24:1
* na ityātmāpadeśe  || bhāṣya 4.24:2
* asmākamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.24:3
* rudra iti kāraṇāpadeśe  || bhāṣya 4.24:4
* rudrasya rudratvaṃ pūrvoktam  || bhāṣya 4.24:5
* pra ityādikarmaṇi  || bhāṣya 4.24:6
* cuda preraṇe  || bhāṣya 4.24:7
* codanaṃ nāma jñānakriyāśaktisaṃyogaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.24:8
* yāditi lipsā  || bhāṣya 4.24:9
* saṃyojayasva māmityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 4.24:10
* uktaṃ hi rudrasyecchāpūrvako yo yogo jñānakriyāśaktibhyāṃ paśvādiṣu sambhavaḥ taccodanamāhurācāryāḥ  || bhāṣya 4.24:11

**********************************************


ড়āśupatasūtra, 5

asaṅgaḥ  || 5.1 ||

* atra akāraḥ saṅgapratiṣedhe  || bhāṣya 5.1:1
* atra saṅgo nāma yadetatpuruṣe viṣayitvam  || bhāṣya 5.1:2
* tena viṣayitvena yogādadharmeṇa cāyaṃ puruṣo yadā adhyayanadhyānādibhyaścyavati  || bhāṣya 5.1:3
* dṛṣṭāntaśravaṇaprekṣaṇalakṣaṇo vanagajavattraikālyamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.1:4
* asaṅgitvamapyatītānāgatavartamānānāṃ viṣayāṇāmanucintanaṃ bhikṣuvat || bhāṣya 5.1:5
* evaṃ maheśvare bhāvasthitistadasaṅgitvamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.1:6
* āha kimasaṅgitvamevaikamuktaṃ nānyallakṣaṇam  || bhāṣya 5.1:7
* ucyate yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 5.1:8

**********************************************

yogī  || 5.2 ||

* iti  || bhāṣya 5.2:1
* atra yogo nāmātmeśvarasaṃyogo yogaḥ pratyetavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.2:2
* uktaṃ hi | śaṅkhadundubhinirghoṣairvividhairgativāditaiḥ | kriyamāṇairna budhyeta etadyuktasya lakṣaṇam  || bhāṣya 5.2:3
* iti  || bhāṣya 5.2:4
* āha kiṃ lakṣaṇadvayamevātra yuktasyocyate  || bhāṣya 5.2:5
* na  || bhāṣya 5.2:6
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 5.2:7

**********************************************

nityātmā  || 5.3 ||

* atra nityatvaviśeṣaṇenānityatvaṃ nivartate  || bhāṣya 5.3:1
* nityatvaṃ nāma sati vibhutve puruṣeśvarayormanasā saha gatasyātmatābhāvasya vṛttyākārasya viṣayaṃ prati kramo'kṣopo'vasthānaṃ vṛkṣaśakunivat || bhāṣya 5.3:2
* tasminnirvṛtte maheśvare yukto nitya ityucyate  || bhāṣya 5.3:3
* ātmā iti kṣetrajñamāha  || bhāṣya 5.3:4
* kathaṃ gamyate  || bhāṣya 5.3:5
* cittasthityupadeśādyogārthaṃ vidyācaraṇopadeśādasaṅgayogiyuktātmājamaitrādīnāṃ cetane sambhavātna tvacetaneṣu kāryakaraṇapradhānādiṣu  || bhāṣya 5.3:6
* tasmiṃścetane ātmaśabdaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.3:7
* ātmā ca kasmāt || bhāṣya 5.3:8
* atatītyātmā  || bhāṣya 5.3:9
