* āpūrya kāryakaraṇaṃ viṣayāṃścetayatītyātmā  || bhāṣya 5.3:10
* uktaṃ hi | yadāpnoti yadādatte yaccātti viṣayān punaḥ | yaccāsya satataṃ bhāvaḥ tasmādātmeti saṃjñitaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.3:11
* sa ca śrotā spraṣṭā draṣṭā rasayitā ghrātā mantā vaktā boddhā ityevamādiḥ  || bhāṣya 5.3:12
* uktaṃ hi | puruṣaścetano bhoktā kṣetrajñaḥ pudgalo janaḥ | aṇurvedo'mṛtaḥ sākṣī jīvātmā paribhūḥ paraḥ  || bhāṣya 5.3:13
* iti | tasya sukhaduḥkhecchādveṣaprayatnacaitanyādibhirliṅgairadhigamaḥ kriyata ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.3:14
* āha kiṃ lakṣaṇatrayamevāsya yuktasyocyate  || bhāṣya 5.3:15
* na yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 5.3:16

**********************************************

ajaḥ  || 5.4 ||

* atra aja ityarthāntaraprādurbhāvapratiṣedho'bhidhīyate  || bhāṣya 5.4:1
* atrārthāntaraṃ nāma śabdasparśarūparasagandhāntaramadhyayanadhyānasmaraṇādayaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.4:2
* teṣu na jāyata iti ajaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.4:3
* āha kiṃ lakṣaṇacatuṣkamevāsya yuktasyocyate  || bhāṣya 5.4:4
* na  || bhāṣya 5.4:5
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 5.4:6

**********************************************

maitraḥ  || 5.5 ||

* atra maitra iti samatāyāṃ bhavati  || bhāṣya 5.5:1
* yathā maitra ādityaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.5:2
* sarvabhūtasthite ca maheśvare sthitacittaḥ icchādveṣavinivṛtto'pravṛttimānmaitra ityucyate  || bhāṣya 5.5:3
* tasmātkāryakaraṇavāneva cittasthitisamakālamevāsaṅgādibhāvena jāyate  || bhāṣya 5.5:4
* āha atha kathaṃ punaretadgamyate  || bhāṣya 5.5:5
* yathā kāryakaraṇavāneva cittasthitisamakālamevāsaṅgādibhāvena jāyate  || bhāṣya 5.5:6
* ucyate gamyate  || bhāṣya 5.5:7
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 5.5:8

**********************************************

abhijāyate  || 5.6 ||

* atra abhiśabdo viśeṣaṇe  || bhāṣya 5.6:1
* ko viśeṣa iti cet || bhāṣya 5.6:2
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 5.6:3
* yasmādayaṃ saṅgī ayogī anityātmā anajo'maitraśca bhūtvā asaṅgādibhāvena jāyata ityeṣa viśeṣaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.6:4
* jāyate iti  || bhāṣya 5.6:5
* janī prādurbhāve  || bhāṣya 5.6:6
* tasmātkāryakaraṇavāneva cittasthitisamakālamevāsaṅgādibhāvena yugapajjāyate avaśyādivadityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.6:7
* asaṅgādibhāve ko'sāvabhyupāyo yena jāyate  || bhāṣya 5.6:8
* ucyate  || bhāṣya 5.6:9

**********************************************

indriyāṇāmabhijayāt || 5.7 ||

* atra jitatā jayaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.7:1
* tasmājjayādasaṅgatādi bhavati  || bhāṣya 5.7:2
* atra parigrahatayeśvarāṇi indriyāṇi buddhyādīni vāgantāni trayodaśa karaṇāni  || bhāṣya 5.7:3
* teṣāmabhijayādityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.7:4
* āha kathaṃ buddhisiddhiriti cet || bhāṣya 5.7:5
* taducyate siddhatvāt || bhāṣya 5.7:6
* atra matibuddhipidhānasthāpanoddeśādghaṭapaṭavatsiddhatvācca buddhiḥ siddhā tathā paropadeśātsvātmaparātmaprativibhāgadarśanātsuro'haṃ naro'hamiti bhinnavṛttitvāccāhaṃkāraḥ siddhaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.7:7
* tathā manaḥ pravartate manojavī mano'mana iti saṃkalpavikalpavṛttinānātvaṃ ca siddham  || bhāṣya 5.7:8
* evaṃ trikālavṛttyantaḥkaraṇaṃ puruṣasya vyākhyātam  || bhāṣya 5.7:9
* tathā buddhīndriyāṇāṃ śrotraṃ vyākhyātam  || bhāṣya 5.7:10
* paraparivādādivacanāduccairubhayathā pramukhe dviradhiṣṭhāne saṃniviṣṭaṃ sāmantācchabdavyañjanasamarthaṃ siddham  || bhāṣya 5.7:11
* tathātitapopadeśāttvagantarbahiśca śarīraṃ vyāpya saṃniviṣṭā sparśavyañjanasamarthā siddhā  || bhāṣya 5.7:12
* tathā mūtrapurīṣadarśanapratiṣedhātkṛtānnādivacanācca cakṣuḥ uccairubhayathā pramukhe dviradhiṣṭhāne saṃniviṣṭaṃ ghaṭarūpādi vyañjanasamarthaṃ siddham  || bhāṣya 5.7:13
* tathā māṃsalavaṇopadeśājjihvā tanmukhe māṃsapeśyāṃ saṃniviṣṭā rasajñānajananasamarthā siddhā  || bhāṣya 5.7:14
* tathā prāṇāyāmopadeśādghrāṇaṃ pramukhe uccairubhayathā dviradhiṣṭhāne saṃniviṣṭaṃ gandhagrahaṇasamarthaṃ siddham  || bhāṣya 5.7:15
* evamadhikārivṛttibhirbudhyatyebhiḥ puruṣa iti buddhīndriyāṇi  || bhāṣya 5.7:16
* tathā karmendriyāṇi  || bhāṣya 5.7:17
* maṇṭanaviharaṇopadeśātpādendriyamadhastāddviradhiṣṭhāne saṃniviṣṭaṃ gamanakriyāsamarthaṃ siddham  || bhāṣya 5.7:18
* tathā mūtrapurīṣadarśanapratiṣedhātpāyvindriyaṃ guhyapradeśe saṃniviṣṭamutsargakriyāsamarthaṃ siddham  || bhāṣya 5.7:19
* tathā strīpratiṣedhādupasthendriyaṃ trivalīguhyapradeśasaṃniviṣṭamānandakriyāsamarthaṃ siddham  || bhāṣya 5.7:20
* tathā api tatkarmopadeśāthastendriyamuccairubhayathā dviradhiṣṭhāne bhujāntardeśe saṃniviṣṭamādānakriyāsamarthaṃ siddham  || bhāṣya 5.7:21
* tathā api tadbhāṣaṇopadeśādvāgindriyaṃ vāktālujihvādiṣu sthāneṣu saṃniviṣṭaṃ vacanakriyāsamarthaṃ siddham  || bhāṣya 5.7:22
* atra vikāratadvṛttibhiḥ karmotpattiḥ puruṣe iti karmendriyāṇi evametāni trayodaśa karaṇānīndriyāṇi sūtrato vyākhyātāni  || bhāṣya 5.7:23
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 5.7:24
* indriyāṇāmiti sāmānyagrahaṇādvikaraṇavatsāmānyapratiṣedhācca  || bhāṣya 5.7:25
* indriyāṇāmiti ṣaṣṭhībahuvacanam  || bhāṣya 5.7:26
* uktaṃ hi | ādānādgrahaṇāttyāgādraṅgaṇādgamanāttathā | iṅganādravaṇāccaiva tasmādindriyamucyate  || bhāṣya 5.7:27
* abhijayāditi  || bhāṣya 5.7:28
* abhiśabdaḥ atyantavijaye vaśīkaraṇe ca  || bhāṣya 5.7:29
* ākramya vaśīkartavyāni  || bhāṣya 5.7:30
* vāyukāmakrodhapāṭaliputravat || bhāṣya 5.7:31
* tasmādakuśalebhyo vyāvartayitvā kāmataḥ kuśalaṃ yojitāni tadā jitāni bhavanti  || bhāṣya 5.7:32
* tasmāduktamindriyāṇāmabhijayāditi asaṅgādijanmanimittatvātpañcamī draṣṭavyā  || bhāṣya 5.7:33
* āha anyatra sāṃkhyayogādīnāmasaṅgādiyuktāḥ muktāḥ śāntiṃ prāptāḥ  || bhāṣya 5.7:34
* nirabhilapyā muktā ityucyante mukta eva na yukta iti kva siddham  || bhāṣya 5.7:35
* taducyate iha yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 5.7:36

**********************************************

rudraḥ provāca tāvat || 5.8 ||

* tatra rudra iti kāraṇāpadeśe  || bhāṣya 5.8:1
* rudratvaṃ pūrvoktam  || bhāṣya 5.8:2
* pra ityabhidhānaviśuddhau  || bhāṣya 5.8:3
* prasannendriyavat || bhāṣya 5.8:4
* vaca vyaktāyāṃ vāci  || bhāṣya 5.8:5
* provāca iti  || bhāṣya 5.8:6
* evaṃ yatsāṃkhyaṃ yogaśca varṇayati asaṅgādiyuktāḥ muktāḥ śāntiṃ prāptā iti tadaviśuddhaṃ teṣāṃ darśanam  || bhāṣya 5.8:7
* taimirikasya cakṣuṣaścandradarśanavat || bhāṣya 5.8:8
* ayaṃ tu yukta eva na mukta iti viśuddhametaddarśanaṃ draṣṭavyam  || bhāṣya 5.8:9
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 5.8:10
* sarvajñavacanādavisaṃvāditvāccaitadgamyam  || bhāṣya 5.8:11
* evametannānyathetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.8:12
* kiṃcānyadidamathaśabdādi śivāntaṃ pravacanaṃ rudraproktaṃ tāvatsarvatantrāṇāṃ śreṣṭham  || bhāṣya 5.8:13
* tasmātkāraṇaśāstrayoḥ parapramāṇabhāvo'vadhāryata ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.8:14
* atra śloko nirvacanaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.8:15
* āha kimetānīndriyāṇi parijñānamātrādeva jitāni bhavanti pradhānavat || bhāṣya 5.8:16
* taducyate na jñānena vacanādibhireṣāṃ jayaḥ kartavyaḥ yasmādeṣāṃ jaye bhagavatā vasatyarthavṛttibalakriyālābhāya vasatā ityatastajjaye vasatyartha eva tāvaducyate  || bhāṣya 5.8:17
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 5.8:18

**********************************************

śūnyāgāraguhāvāsī  || 5.9 ||

* iti  || bhāṣya 5.9:1
* atra śūnyamevāgāraṃ śūnyāgāram  || bhāṣya 5.9:2
* śūnyaṃ viviktaṃ nirjanamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.9:3
* āgāramiti gṛhaparyāyaḥ āgāraṃ gṛhaṃ veśma sadanamiti paryāyaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.9:4
* guhū saṃvaraṇe  || bhāṣya 5.9:5
* praviṣṭaṃ sādhakamāvarayati gopayatīti guhā  || bhāṣya 5.9:6
* āha āvarakatvāviśeṣācchūnyāgāraguhayoraviśeṣa iti cettaducyate mṛttṛṇakāṣṭhādikṛtamagāraṃ parvataguhādyā guhā  || bhāṣya 5.9:7
* tasmānnāviśeṣa iti  || bhāṣya 5.9:8
* yathā sati vibhutve jñatvaṃ sādharmyaṃ puruṣeśvarayoḥ sarvajñatvato viśeṣaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.9:9
* tasmādāyatane'viviktadoṣaṃ dṛṣṭvā śūnyāgāre guhāyāṃ vā yathopapattito vicārya viviktaṃ vivecya yanmātrasthānāsanaśayanādibhirupajīvati tanmātraṃ saṃskaraṇamaryādayopayogakriyābhiniviṣṭena vastavyam  || bhāṣya 5.9:10
* vasatisaṃyogādguhāvāsī bhavati  || bhāṣya 5.9:11
* pulinavāsivat || bhāṣya 5.9:12
* āha tatkathaṃ jñeyaṃ yathā jitānīndriyāṇi  || bhāṣya 5.9:13
* teṣāṃ jitānāṃ vā kiṃ lakṣaṇam  || bhāṣya 5.9:14
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 5.9:15

**********************************************

devanityaḥ  || 5.10 ||

* atra devo bhagavān  || bhāṣya 5.10:1
* tatra yadāsya bhagavati deve nityatā katham  || bhāṣya 5.10:2
* adhyayanadhyānābhyāṃ deve'dhikṛtasya prādhānyena niścalatā vartate  || bhāṣya 5.10:3
* svalpataravyavadhāne'pi atiyogābhyāsanirantaraprāptiḥ  || bhāṣya 5.10:4
* smṛtistu devanityatetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.10:5
* āha devanityatāyāḥ kiṃ lakṣaṇam  || bhāṣya 5.10:6
* taducyate jitendriyatvam  || bhāṣya 5.10:7
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 5.10:8

**********************************************

jitendriyaḥ  || 5.11 ||

* atra jitendriyatvaṃ nāma utsarganigrahayogyatvam  || bhāṣya 5.11:1
* indriyāṇi buddhyādīni vāgantāni trayodaśa karaṇāni pūrvoktāni  || bhāṣya 5.11:2
* tāni yadā akuśalebhyo vyāvartayitvā kāmataḥ kuśale yojitāni hataviṣadarvīkaravadavasthitāni bhavanti tadā devanityo jitendriya ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.11:3
* āha kiṃ devanityataivāsya paro niṣṭhāyogaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.11:4
* ucyate na  || bhāṣya 5.11:5
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 5.11:6

**********************************************

ṣaṇmāsānnityayuktasya  || 5.12 ||

* athavānyo dūrasthaḥ sambandhaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.12:1
* yasmāduktam | yasya yenārthasambandho dūrasthamapi tena hi | arthato'nyasamānānāmānantarye'pyasaṃgatiḥ  || bhāṣya 5.12:2
* evamihāpi dūrasthaḥ sambandhaḥ kasmāt || bhāṣya 5.12:3
* iha purastāduktaṃ vijñānāni cāsya pravartante iti etairguṇairyukta iti ca  || bhāṣya 5.12:4
* kiyatā kālenāsya te guṇāḥ pravartante  || bhāṣya 5.12:5
* kiṃ yuktasya kiṃ viyuktasya kiṃ yugapatkramaśo vā kiṃ sakalasya niṣkalasya veti  || bhāṣya 5.12:6
* ityeṣāmarthānāmanirvacanānāṃ nirvacanārthamidamārabhyate  || bhāṣya 5.12:7
* yasmādāha ṣaṇmāsānnityayuktasya  || bhāṣya 5.12:8
* atra ṣaḍiti saṃkhyā māsāniti kālanirdeśaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.12:9
* manuṣyagaṇanayā triṃśaddivaso māsaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.12:10
* dvādaśa māsāḥ saṃvatsaraḥ  || bhāṣya 5.12:11
* dvādaśa pakṣā ardhasaṃvatsaraḥ  || bhāṣya 5.12:12
* ṣaṇmāsāniti  || bhāṣya 5.12:13
* tasmātṣaṣṭhaprathamamāsayorabhyantare  || bhāṣya 5.12:14
* nityayuktasya  || bhāṣya 5.12:15
* saṃtatamavicchinnamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.12:16
* yukta iti  || bhāṣya 5.12:17
* ātmeśvarasaṃyogo yogaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.12:18
* nityayuktasya iti ṣaṣṭhī  || bhāṣya 5.12:19
* āha asya yuktasya kiṃ bhavati  || bhāṣya 5.12:20
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 5.12:21

**********************************************

bhūyiṣṭhaṃ sampravartate  || 5.13 ||

* atra bhūyiṣṭhamiti krame prāye ca bhavati  || bhāṣya 5.13:1
* yathā kramaśo dadāti ādityo vā gato bhūyiṣṭham  || bhāṣya 5.13:2
* tasmātsūcyagreṇotpalapattraśatabhedanakramavatkramāddūradarśanādayaḥ pravartanta ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.13:3
* samityekībhāve  || bhāṣya 5.13:4
* niṣkalasya kāryakaraṇarahitasyetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.13:5
* pra ityādikarmaṇyārambhe bhavati  || bhāṣya 5.13:6
* yuktottare prabhāvādguṇāḥ pravartante ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.13:7
* vartate kasmin  || bhāṣya 5.13:8
* darśanaṃ dṛśye śravaṇādi śravyādiṣvityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.13:9
* tasmātṣaṣṭhaprathamamāsayorabhyantare nityayuktasya kramaśo guṇāḥ sampravartante  || bhāṣya 5.13:10
* kutaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.13:11
* maheśvaraprasādāt || bhāṣya 5.13:12
* aśivatvasaṃjñake vinivṛtte śivatvaprasādābhyāṃ guṇāḥ pravartante  || bhāṣya 5.13:13
* guṇaśabdo dūradarśanādivacanaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.13:14
* āha kāṃ vṛttimāsthāya śūnyāgāre guhāyāṃ vāsaḥ kāryaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.13:15
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 5.13:16

**********************************************

bhaikṣyam  || 5.14 ||

* bhikṣāṇāṃ samūho bhaikṣyaṃ kāpotavat || bhāṣya 5.14:1
* tacca nagaragrāmādibhyo gṛhādgṛhaṃ paryaṭato bhakṣyabhojyādīnāmanyatamaṃ yatprāpyate kṛtānnādivacanādbhaikṣyam  || bhāṣya 5.14:2
* bhayakṣapaṇādbhaikṣyam  || bhāṣya 5.14:3
* bhikṣāvacanādabhaikṣyapratiṣedhaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.14:4
* āha ādhārāttu kṛtvā saṃdehaḥ atha kutra tadbhaikṣyaṃ grāhyam  || bhāṣya 5.14:5
* taducyate pātre  || bhāṣya 5.14:6
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 5.14:7

**********************************************

pātrāgatam  || 5.15 ||

* atra bhaikṣyavatprasiddhaṃ pātram  || bhāṣya 5.15:1
* alābudāruvastrādīnāmanyatamaṃ yatprāpyate tatkhalu hiṃsāsteyādirahitena krameṇāhāre yatparyāptaṃ grāhyam  || bhāṣya 5.15:2
* tasmin tadaphalake pātre āgataṃ pātrāgatamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.15:3
* āha brahmacārikalpe madhumāṃsalavaṇavarjamiti  || bhāṣya 5.15:4
* tatkiṃ madhumāṃsādīnyekāntenaiva duṣṭānīti  || bhāṣya 5.15:5
* taducyate na  || bhāṣya 5.15:6
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 5.15:7

**********************************************

māṃsamaduṣyaṃ lavaṇena vā  || 5.16 ||

* tatra bhaikṣyavatprasiddhaṃ māṃsam  || bhāṣya 5.16:1
* yasya māhiṣavārāhādīnāmanyatamaṃ yatprāpyate tatkhalu hiṃsāsteyarahitatvāt || bhāṣya 5.16:2
* lavaṇena vā  || bhāṣya 5.16:3
* atra lavaṇaṃ nāma saindhavasauvarcalādyaṃ māṃsavatprasiddham  || bhāṣya 5.16:4
* tadetanmāṃsasamasaṃsṛṣṭaṃ vā bhaikṣyavidhinā prāptam  || bhāṣya 5.16:5
* aduṣyamakutsitamagarhitamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.16:6
* vā vikalpe  || bhāṣya 5.16:7
* māṃsena vā lavaṇena vā ubhābhyāmapi sākṣādvā aduṣyamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.16:8
* āha bhaikṣyālābhakāle aparyāptikāle vā kimanena kartavyam  || bhāṣya 5.16:9
* taducyate apaḥ pītvā stheyam  || bhāṣya 5.16:10
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 5.16:11

**********************************************

āpo vāpi yathākālamaśnīyādanupūrvaśaḥ  || 5.17 ||

* atra āṇāpaḥ āpaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.17:1
* āṅiti atra saṃvṛtaparipūtādimaryādāmadhikurute kṛtānnotsṛṣṭavadapadāntaritatvāt || bhāṣya 5.17:2
* dvitīyāsthāne prathamā draṣṭavyā  || bhāṣya 5.17:3
* āpo'tra lokādiprasiddhāḥ  || bhāṣya 5.17:4
* tṛṇādivyāvṛttamudakamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.17:5
* vā vibhāge  || bhāṣya 5.17:6
* anyadbhaikṣyamanyā āpa iti  || bhāṣya 5.17:7
* apiśabdaḥ sambhāvane  || bhāṣya 5.17:8
* apyapaḥ pītvā stheyaṃ na tu śāstravyapetena krameṇa vṛttyarjanaṃ kartavyamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.17:9
* yathā itiśabdaḥ samānārthe  || bhāṣya 5.17:10
* yathā bhaikṣyopadeśaṃ kṛtvā yogakarmaṇyudyamaḥ kartavya iti vyākhyātaṃ tathā apaḥ pītveti  || bhāṣya 5.17:11
* kālo'tra dvividhaḥ alābhakālaḥ aparyāptikālaśca  || bhāṣya 5.17:12
* tatra yadā grāmaṃ nagaraṃ vā kṛtsnamaṭitvā na kiṃcidāsādayati saḥ alābhakālaḥ aparyāptikālo nāma yadā bhikṣāṃ bhikṣādvayaṃ vā āsādayati tadā apaḥ pītvāpi stheyam  || bhāṣya 5.17:13
* āha evaṃ sthitena kimanena kartavyam  || bhāṣya 5.17:14
* taducyate upayoktavyam  || bhāṣya 5.17:15
* yasmādāha aśnīyādanupūrvaśaḥ iti  || bhāṣya 5.17:16
* aśnīyāditi yogakriyānuparodhenāhāralāghavamaryādāmadhikurute  || bhāṣya 5.17:17
* aśa bhojane  || bhāṣya 5.17:18
* aśnīyādanupūrvaśaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.17:19
* anu pṛṣṭhakarmakriyāyām  || bhāṣya 5.17:20
* anupūrvaśa iti atikrāntāpekṣaṇe prakāravacane ca  || bhāṣya 5.17:21
* yathāpūrvaṃ grāmādi praviśya bhaikṣyārjanaṃ kṛtvālābhakāle aparyāptikāle vā tadanu paścādapaḥ pītvā stheyamiti kṛtvā bhagavatā etaduktamaśnīyādanupūrvaśa iti  || bhāṣya 5.17:22
* atra śloko nirvacanaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.17:23
* āha śūnyāgāraguhāvasthasyendriyajayena vartato'sya balaṃ kiṃ cintyate kimakaluṣatvameva  || bhāṣya 5.17:24
* taducyate na  || bhāṣya 5.17:25
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 5.17:26

**********************************************

godharmā mṛgadharmā vā  || 5.18 ||

* atra gaurlokādiprasiddho mṛgavatkhurakakudaviṣāṇasāsnādimāniti  || bhāṣya 5.18:1
* tathā mṛgo'pi godravyavallokādiprasiddhaḥ kṛṣṇamṛgādīnāmanyatamaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.18:2
* tayostu sati dharmabahutve samāno dharmo gṛhyate ādhyātmikādidvaṃdvasahiṣṇutvam  || bhāṣya 5.18:3
* taduttaratra vakṣyāmaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.18:4
* gomṛgadharmagrahaṇaṃ tu parasparaviśeṣaṇārtham  || bhāṣya 5.18:5
* vāśabdo vikalpārthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.18:6
* kriyāsāmānyadṛṣṭyā raudrībahurūpīvadekadharmeṇa caikadharmeṇa vā stheyamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.18:7
* āha kena balenāsya kāryaniṣpattiḥ  || bhāṣya 5.18:8
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 5.18:9

**********************************************

adbhireva śucirbhavet || 5.19 ||

* atra adbhiḥ āṅiva adbhireva āpo jalamityādiprasiddhāḥ pūrvoktāḥ  || bhāṣya 5.19:1
* adbhiriti tṛtīyā  || bhāṣya 5.19:2
* āṅiti pūrvaprasiddhamātrādimaryādāmadhikurute  || bhāṣya 5.19:3
* gomṛgavaddvaṃdvasahiṣṇutvamaryādāyāṃ ca  || bhāṣya 5.19:4
* iva iti upamāyām  || bhāṣya 5.19:5
* yathā adbhiśca mṛdbhiśca prakṣālitāni vastrādīni śuddhāni bhavanti tadvat || bhāṣya 5.19:6
* gomṛgadharmitvena balena śucirbhavatīti  || bhāṣya 5.19:7
* ucyate na  || bhāṣya 5.19:8
* yasmādāha gomṛgayorakuśaladharmapratiṣedhaṃ kuśaladharme ca niyogaṃ siddhaśaktipraśaṃsayā asiddhaśaktipratiṣedhaṃ ca vakṣyāmaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.19:9
* tadāha  || bhāṣya 5.19:10

**********************************************

siddhayogī na lipyate karmaṇā pātakena vā  || 5.20 ||

* asiddhastu sarvathāpi vartamāno lipyata ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.20:1
* ato yogī siddha ityevaṃ prāpte sukhamukhoccāraṇārthamuktaṃ siddhayogī iti  || bhāṣya 5.20:2
* atra  || bhāṣya 5.20:3
* yogo nāmātmeśvarayoryogaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.20:4
* tenāyaṃ yogī  || bhāṣya 5.20:5
* siddho nāma darśanādyaiśvaryaṃ prāptaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.20:6
* sa khalu vaśīkaraṇāveśanapālanādipravīṇaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.20:7
* na lipyate na saṃyujyata ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.20:8
* āha kena na lipyate  || bhāṣya 5.20:9
* taducyate karmaṇā  || bhāṣya 5.20:10
* atra karmaṇetyucyate  || bhāṣya 5.20:11
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 5.20:12
* kṛtakatvāt || bhāṣya 5.20:13
* karmaṇeti tṛtīyā  || bhāṣya 5.20:14
* iṣṭasthānaśarīrendriyaviṣayasambandhakṛtena karmaṇā na lipyate na saṃyujyata ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.20:15
* āha aninditena śubhena karmaṇā na saṃyujyata ityucyate āho atha kimaśubhena karmaṇā lipyate neti  || bhāṣya 5.20:16
* taducyate na  || bhāṣya 5.20:17
* yasmādāha pātakena  || bhāṣya 5.20:18
* atra pāpākhyena pātakena vāniṣṭasthānaśarīrendriyaviṣayagato'śubhaṃ bhuṅkte tenāpyaśubhena karmaṇā na lipyate na yujyata ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.20:19
* vā trikalpe  || bhāṣya 5.20:20
* pātakena vā apātakena vā samastābhyāṃ vā vaśīkaraṇāveśanapālanādiṣu pravartamāno na lipyate na saṃyujyata ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.20:21
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 5.20:22
* siddhisāmarthyāt || bhāṣya 5.20:23
* asiddhaścāyaṃ yogī brāhmaṇo gomṛgadharmāvastho yadi sarvathāpi gomṛgavatpravartate tato lipyate  || bhāṣya 5.20:24
* tasmādgomṛgayorakuśaladharmo na grāhyaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.20:25
* kuśaladharmaśca svādhyātmikādidvaṃdvasahiṣṇutvaṃ parigṛhyate  || bhāṣya 5.20:26
* tenāyaṃ śucirbhavati  || bhāṣya 5.20:27
* āha kimasyāśaucam  || bhāṣya 5.20:28
* taducyate dvaṃdvairyogavyāsaṅgakaraiḥ kāmakrodhaśirorogādinimittaiḥ śītādibhiranyairvā  || bhāṣya 5.20:29
* na lipyate na saṃyujyata ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.20:30
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 5.20:31
* prāptabalatvādityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.20:32
* atra śloko nirvacanaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.20:33
* āha śūnyāgāraguhāvasthasyendriyajaye vartataḥ kāḥ kriyāḥ kartavyāḥ  || bhāṣya 5.20:34
* kiṃ snānahasitādyāḥ krāthanaspandanamaṇṭanādyā vā  || bhāṣya 5.20:35
* taducyate na  || bhāṣya 5.20:36
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 5.20:37

ṛcamiṣṭāmadhīyīta gāyatrīmātmayantritaḥ  || 5.21 ||

* atra ṛcamṛcāmityapyaduṣṭaḥ pāṭhaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.21:1
* atra ṛcā nāmāghorā  || bhāṣya 5.21:2
* kathaṃ gamyate  || bhāṣya 5.21:3
* ṛṅmadhyāt || bhāṣya 5.21:4
* sadyojātatatpuruṣeśānavadarcivarcagāḥ  || bhāṣya 5.21:5
* iṣṭā ceyaṃ tatra tatra japtavyatvena guṇīkṛtatvāt || bhāṣya 5.21:6
* pūrvottarasūtreṣu japtavyatvena gāyatryā sahādhyānādāśubhāvasamādhyāsādanācca iṣṭā  || bhāṣya 5.21:7
* adhyayanamiti japyaparyāyaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.21:8
* īta ityājñāyāṃ niyoge ca  || bhāṣya 5.21:9
* mānasamevādhīyītetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.21:10
* āha kimṛcaivaikādhyetavyā  || bhāṣya 5.21:11
* ucyate na  || bhāṣya 5.21:12
* yasmādāha gāyatrīmiti  || bhāṣya 5.21:13
* gāyatrī nāma tatpuruṣā  || bhāṣya 5.21:14
* niruktamasyāḥ pūrvoktam  || bhāṣya 5.21:15
* mānasamevādhīyītetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.21:16
* āha kīdṛśo'dhīyīteti  || bhāṣya 5.21:17
* taducyate ātmayantritaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.21:18
* ātmayantraṇamiti pratyāhāraparyāyaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.21:19
* ātmeti kṣetrajña ucyate  || bhāṣya 5.21:20
* ātmatvamasya caitanyam  || bhāṣya 5.21:21
* āptavyaṃ kāryaṃ karaṇaṃ viṣayāśca  || bhāṣya 5.21:22
* ātmayantraṇamityatra sati trike yujyate  || bhāṣya 5.21:23
* yantraṇaṃ nāma yathāyamātmabhāvo brahmaṇyakṣarapadapaṅktyāṃ yukto vartate tadātmā yantrito bhavati  || bhāṣya 5.21:24
* katham  || bhāṣya 5.21:25
* nṛtyaprasaktacittadṛṣṭāntātkasmāt || bhāṣya 5.21:26
* ātmātmabhāvayoravyucchedāt || bhāṣya 5.21:27
* guṇaguṇinorapi tathā yugapadbhāvaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.21:28
* katham  || bhāṣya 5.21:29
* yaṣṭuṃ pravṛtto yantrayituṃ ca pravṛtta eva bhavati  || bhāṣya 5.21:30
* bhikṣuvat || bhāṣya 5.21:31
* tasmādyantraṇamevaiṣa pratyāhāra iti  || bhāṣya 5.21:32
* āha atra gāyatrībahutvātsaṃdehaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.21:33
* kathamavagamyate ṛcā aghoreṇa vā tatpuruṣeṇeti  || bhāṣya 5.21:34
* ucyate gamyate  || bhāṣya 5.21:35
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 5.21:36

raudrīṃ vā bahurūpīṃ vā  || 5.22 ||

* atra raudrī nāma tatpuruṣā  || bhāṣya 5.22:1

* vā vikalpe  || bhāṣya 5.22:5

* taducyate  || bhāṣya 5.22:10

ato yogaḥ pravartate  || 5.23 ||

* atra ata iti kāraṇāpadeśe  || bhāṣya 5.23:1
* ātmayantrito'dhīyītetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.23:2
* tasmādanena kāraṇena hetunā nimittenetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.23:3
* yoga iti  || bhāṣya 5.23:4
* ātmeśvarasaṃyogo yoga iti mantavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.23:5

* atra śloko nirvacanaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.23:8

* ucyate dṛṣṭā  || bhāṣya 5.23:11
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 5.23:12

oṃkāramabhidhyāyīta  || 5.24 ||

* atra omityeṣa japyaparyāyo vāmadevādivat || bhāṣya 5.24:1
* kāraśabdo'vadhāraṇe draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.24:2
* kiṃkāraṇam  || bhāṣya 5.24:3
* uktaṃ hi | praṇave nityayuktasya vyāhṛtiṣu ca saptasu | tripadāyāṃ ca gāyatryāṃ na mṛtyurvindate param  || bhāṣya 5.24:4
* ityata oṃkāra evāvadhāryate dhyeyatvena na tu gāyatryādayaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.24:5
* abhirabhyāse  || bhāṣya 5.24:6
* oṃkārasaṃnikṛṣṭacittena bhavitavyam  || bhāṣya 5.24:7
* dhyai cintāyām  || bhāṣya 5.24:8
* dhyānaṃ cintanamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.24:9
* uktaṃ hi | dhyai cintālakṣaṇaṃ dhyānaṃ brahma cauṃkāralakṣaṇam | dhīyate līyate vāpi tasmāddhyānamiti smṛtam  || bhāṣya 5.24:10
* muhūrtārdhaṃ muhūrtaṃ vā prāṇāyāmāntare'pi vā  || bhāṣya 5.24:11
* dhyeyaṃ cintayamānastu pāpaṃ kṣapayate naraḥ  || bhāṣya 5.24:12
* īta ityājñāyāṃ niyoge ca  || bhāṣya 5.24:13
* oṃkāra eva dhyeyo nānya ityarthaḥ | āha oṃkāro dhyeyaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.24:14
* ko vā dhyānadeśaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.24:15
* kasmin vā deśe dhāraṇā kartavyā  || bhāṣya 5.24:16
* dhyāyamānena vā kiṃ kartavyam  || bhāṣya 5.24:17
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 5.24:18

**********************************************

hṛdi kurvīta dhāraṇām  || 5.25 ||

* tatra hṛdi ityātmaparyāyaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.25:1
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 5.25:2
* pūrvottarasāmarthyāt || bhāṣya 5.25:3
* yo'rtho yatra milati sa tatra sthāpayitavyaḥ sa evārtho dhārayitavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.25:4
* kiṃca vedaprāmāṇyāduktam | aṅgādaṅgātsambhavasi hṛdayādadhijāyase | ātmā vai putranāmāsi sa jīva śaradaḥ śatam  || bhāṣya 5.25:5
* anyatrāpi ātmā vijāyate putra ātmā vai ātmanaḥ pitā | ātmaprajo bhaviṣyāmi paramaṃ hṛdayaṃ hi saḥ  || bhāṣya 5.25:6
* ato hṛdayamātmetyuktam  || bhāṣya 5.25:7
* prakurute bhāvaṃ buddhiradhyavasāyitām  || bhāṣya 5.25:8
* hṛdayaṃ priyāpriye vetti trividhā karaṇasthitiḥ  || bhāṣya 5.25:9
* tathā loke'pi santi vaktāro hṛdayaṃ te jñāsyati  || bhāṣya 5.25:10
* kimuktaṃ bhavati  || bhāṣya 5.25:11
* ātmā te jñāsyatīti  || bhāṣya 5.25:12
* ato'vagamyate hṛdītyātmaparyāyaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.25:13
* hṛdīti aupaśleṣikaṃ saṃnidhānam  || bhāṣya 5.25:14
* atra tu oṃkāro dhāryo nātmā kiṃtu ya evātmanyātmabhāvaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.25:15
* tasyoṃkārātpracyutasya viṣayebhyo vṛttivikāramātreṇa gatasya pratyānayanaṃ pratyāhāraḥ  || bhāṣya 5.25:16
* pratyāhṛtya hṛdi dhāraṇā kartavyā  || bhāṣya 5.25:17
* dhāryaṃ coṃkārānucintanam  || bhāṣya 5.25:18
* tatraiva sudīrghakālamavasthānamadhyayanam  || bhāṣya 5.25:19
* taddhāraṇāhitaṃ paraṃ dhyānam  || bhāṣya 5.25:20
* niṣṭhāyogastu sthāpayitveti vakṣyāmaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.25:21
* kurvīta iti  || bhāṣya 5.25:22
* ḍukṛñkaraṇe  || bhāṣya 5.25:23
* tasya saptamyante kurvīteti bhavati  || bhāṣya 5.25:24
* hṛdi dhāraṇā kartavyā  || bhāṣya 5.25:25
* īta ityājñāyāṃ niyoge ca  || bhāṣya 5.25:26
* pādajānukaṭināsikādisthāneṣu dhāraṇākartavyatāpratiṣedhārtho niyogaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.25:27
* hṛdi dhāryā nānyatretyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.25:28
* āha oṃkāraḥ kiṃ parapa viṣṇurumā kumāraśca catasro'rdhamātrā vā  || bhāṣya 5.25:29
* uta samānapuruṣa iti  || bhāṣya 5.25:30
* ucyate na  || bhāṣya 5.25:31
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 5.25:32

**********************************************

ṛṣirvipro mahāneṣaḥ  || 5.26 ||

* atra ṛṣiḥ ityetadbhagavatā nāmadheyam  || bhāṣya 5.26:1
* ṛṣiḥ kasmāt || bhāṣya 5.26:2
* ṛṣiḥ kriyāyām  || bhāṣya 5.26:3
* ṛṣitvaṃ nāma kriyāśaṃsanādṛṣiḥ  || bhāṣya 5.26:4
* tathā kṛtsnaṃ kāryaṃ vidyādyamīśata ityataḥ ṛṣiḥ  || bhāṣya 5.26:5
* tathā vipra ityetadapi bhagavato nāma  || bhāṣya 5.26:6
* vipraḥ kasmāt || bhāṣya 5.26:7
* vida jñāne  || bhāṣya 5.26:8
* vipratvaṃ nāma jñānaśaktiḥ  || bhāṣya 5.26:9
* vyāptamanena bhagavatā jñānaśaktyā kṛtsnaṃ jñeyamityato vipra iti  || bhāṣya 5.26:10
* tathā mahānityabhyadhikatve  || bhāṣya 5.26:11
* yadetaddṛkkriyālakṣaṇamasti anāgantukamakṛtakamaiśvaryaṃ tadguṇasadbhāvaḥ satattvaṃ tattvadharmaḥ tadakṛtakaṃ puruṣacaitanyavattannānyasyetyato'bhyadhikaḥ utkṛṣṭo'tiriktaśceti mahān  || bhāṣya 5.26:12
* eṣa iti pratyakṣe  || bhāṣya 5.26:13
* eṣa yo mayā pūrvamomiti śrotrapratyakṣīkṛto'rthaḥ asau viṣṇūmākumārādīnāmanyatamo na bhavati  || bhāṣya 5.26:14
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 5.26:15
* ṛṣitvādvipratvānmahattvāccetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.26:16
* āha ṛṣitvaṃ vipratvaṃ ca kīdṛśe maheśvare cintanīyam  || bhāṣya 5.26:17
* kīdṛśo vā oṃkāro dhyeyaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.26:18
* taducyate  || bhāṣya 5.26:19

**********************************************

vāgviśuddhaḥ  || 5.27 ||

* atrāpi vāgviśuddha ityapi bhagavato nāmadheyam  || bhāṣya 5.27:1
* na amī ityanyo bhagavān  || bhāṣya 5.27:2
* sa yathā hyatho hitvā vāṇīṃ manasā saha rūparasagandhavidyāpuruṣādiparo niṣkalo dhyeyaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.27:3
* yasmāduktam | ākṛtimapi parihṛtya dhyānaṃ nityaṃ pare rudre | yena prāptaṃ yoge muhūrtamapi tatparo yogaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.27:4
* paramayoga ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.27:5
* āha atha yathāyaṃ bālavanniṣkalastathā kiṃ samānapuruṣaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.27:6
* taducyate na  || bhāṣya 5.27:7
* yasmādāha tadāpyayam  || bhāṣya 5.27:8

**********************************************

maheśvaraḥ  || 5.28 ||

* atrānādyajñānadyatinā ṛṣitvavipratvasaṃjñakena mahatā aiśvaryeṇa maheśvara iti siddhamiha tu yadāyaṃ vāgviśuddho niṣkalastadā kiṃ samānapuruṣavadanīśvara ityasya saṃśayasya saṃvyudāsārthamucyate maheśvara iti  || bhāṣya 5.28:1
* yasmādasyaiśvaryaṃ niṣkalasyāpi svaguṇasadbhāvaḥ satattvaṃ tattvadharmaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.28:2
* tadakṛtakatvaṃ puruṣacaitanyavat || bhāṣya 5.28:3
* atastadāpyayaṃ mahāneveśvaro maheśvaraḥ  || bhāṣya 5.28:4
* tasmādakṛtaka eva mahacchabda ityato maheśvara iti  || bhāṣya 5.28:5
* evamoṃkāramiti dhyeyamuktam  || bhāṣya 5.28:6
* dhyeyaguṇīkaraṇamuktamṛṣirvipro mahāneṣa iti  || bhāṣya 5.28:7
* dhyeyāvadhāraṇamuktaṃ vāgviśuddho niṣkala iti  || bhāṣya 5.28:8
* dhyeyaśaktipraśaṃsā coktā maheśvara iti  || bhāṣya 5.28:9
* evaṃ yasmādindriyajaye vartate ato vasatyarthavṛttibalakriyālābhādayaśca vyākhyātā iti  || bhāṣya 5.28:10
* ato'tra yuktaṃ vaktum  || bhāṣya 5.28:11
* śūnyāgāraguhāprakaraṇaṃ parisamāptamiti  || bhāṣya 5.28:12
* āha śūnyāgāraguhāyāṃ yadā jitānīndriyāṇi devanityatā ca prāptā bhavati tadā kiṃ tadeva bhaikṣyaṃ vṛttimāsthāya tatraivānena duḥkhāntaprāpteḥ stheyam  || bhāṣya 5.28:13
* āhosviddṛṣṭo'syāpi vasatyartho vṛttirbalakriyālābhāśceti  || bhāṣya 5.28:14
* ucyate dṛṣṭaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.28:15
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 5.28:16

**********************************************

(...)  || 5.29 ||

* āha śūnyāgāraguhāmutsṛjya prayojanābhāvātśmaśāne saṃkrāntirayukteti cet || bhāṣya 5.29:1
* taducyate na  || bhāṣya 5.29:2
* yogavyāsaṅgaparihārārthatvāt || bhāṣya 5.29:3
* ihāvasthānādavasthānaṃ prāpya brāhmaṇasya sarvatra vasatyarthavṛttibalakriyālābhādayo'yutasiddhā vaktavyāḥ  || bhāṣya 5.29:4
* tatrādidharmā apyasya tāvadāyatane vasatyarthaḥ vṛttirbhaikṣyaṃ balamaṣṭāṅgaṃ brahmacaryaṃ kriyāḥ sthānahasitādyāḥ snānaṃ kaluṣāpohaḥ śuddhiḥ jñānāvāptiḥ akaluṣatvaṃ ca lābhā iti  || bhāṣya 5.29:5
* tathā asanmānaparibhavopadeśādāyatane vasatyarthaḥ vṛttirutsṛṣṭaṃ balamakaluṣatvamindriyadvārapidhānaṃ ca kriyā indriyāṇi pidhāya unmattavadavasthānaṃ pāpakṣayācchuddhiḥ lābhastu kṛtsno dharmastulyendriyajaye vartate  || bhāṣya 5.29:6
* tathā vasatyarthaḥ śūnyāgāraguhā vṛttirbhaikṣyaṃ balaṃ gomṛgayoḥ sahadharmitvaṃ kriyā adhyayanadhyānādyā ajitendriyavṛttitāpohaḥ śuddhiḥ lābhastu devanityatā jitendriyatvaṃ ceti  || bhāṣya 5.29:7
* tathehāpi śmaśāne vasatyarthaḥ vasan dharmātmā  || bhāṣya 5.29:8
* yathālabdhamiti vṛttiḥ kriyā smṛtiḥ asmṛtyapohaḥ śuddhiḥ lābhastu sāyujyam  || bhāṣya 5.29:9
* tathottaratra ṛṣiriti vasatyarthaḥ balamapramādaḥ prasāda upāyaḥ duḥkhāpohaḥ śuddhiḥ guṇāvāptiśca lābha iti  || bhāṣya 5.29:10
* tathoktaṃ ca | pañca lābhānmalān pañca pañcopāyān viśeṣataḥ | yastu budhyati pañcārthe sa vidvānnātra saṃśayaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.29:11
* prathamo vidyālābhastapaso lābho'tha devanityatvam | yogo guṇapravṛttirlābhāḥ pañceha vijñeyāḥ  || bhāṣya 5.29:12
* ajñānamadharmaśca viṣayābhyāsaḥ sthiteralābhaśca | anaiśvaryaṃ ca malā vijñeyāḥ pañca pañcārthe  || bhāṣya 5.29:13
* vāso dhyānamakhilakaraṇanirodhastathā smṛtiścaiva | prasāda iti copāyā vijñeyāḥ pañca pañcārthe  || bhāṣya 5.29:14
* vāsārtho lokaśca śūnyāgāraṃ tathā śmaśānaṃ ca | rudraśca pañca deśā niyataṃ siddhyarthamākhyātāḥ  || bhāṣya 5.29:15
* tasmādyuktamuktam  || bhāṣya 5.29:16
* sa eva prāguktaḥ sambandhaḥ śmaśānavāsī iti  || bhāṣya 5.29:17
* atra śmaśānaṃ nāma yadetallokādiprasiddhaṃ laukikānāṃ mṛtāni śavāni parityajanti tat || bhāṣya 5.29:18
* śavasambandhātśmaśānaṃ tasminnākāśe vṛkṣamūle yathānabhiṣvaṅgamaryādayā jitadvaṃdvena smṛtikriyāniviṣṭena vastavyam  || bhāṣya 5.29:19
* vasatisaṃyogātśmaśānavāsī bhavati pulinavāsivadityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.29:20
* āha kimasya gomṛgayoḥ sahadharmitvameva balam  || bhāṣya 5.29:21
* taducyate na  || bhāṣya 5.29:22
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 5.29:23

**********************************************

śmaśānavāsī  || 5.30 ||

* atra dharmo nāma ya eṣa yamaniyamapūrvako'bhivyakto māhātmyādidharmaḥ sa pūrvoktaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.30:1
* so'syātmani pracitaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.30:2
* tena dharmeṇa dharmātmā bhavatītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.30:3
* āha kimasya bhaikṣyameva vṛttiḥ  || bhāṣya 5.30:4
* ucyate na  || bhāṣya 5.30:5
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 5.30:6

**********************************************

dharmātmā  || 5.31 ||

* atra yathā iti samānārthe amlādiṣu jitendriyatvāt || bhāṣya 5.31:1
* labdhamāsāditamaprārthitamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.31:2
* upa iti samīpadhāraṇe  || bhāṣya 5.31:3
* tadyathālabdhamannapānaṃ śmaśānādanirgacchatā divase divase jīvanāya sthityarthaṃ tadupajīvan yathālabdhopajīvako bhavatītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.31:4
* āha kiṃ jīvanameva paro lābha iti  || bhāṣya 5.31:5
* ucyate na  || bhāṣya 5.31:6
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 5.31:7

**********************************************

yathālabdhopajīvakaḥ labhate rudrasāyujyam  || 5.32 ||

* atra labhate vindate āsādayatītyarthaḥ rudra iti kāraṇāpadeśe  || bhāṣya 5.32:1
* rudrasya rudratvaṃ pūrvoktam  || bhāṣya 5.32:2
* sākṣādrudreṇa saha saṃyogaḥ sāyujyam  || bhāṣya 5.32:3
* bhāvagrahaṇamātmeśvarābhyāmanyatra pratiṣedhārtham  || bhāṣya 5.32:4
* yogasya samyaktvaṃ sāyujyamiti yogaparyāyo'vagamyate  || bhāṣya 5.32:5
* dharmātmavacanādatigatyānantyavadityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.32:6
* āha tatkenopāyena labhate  || bhāṣya 5.32:7
* kimadhyayanadhyāpanābhyāmeva  || bhāṣya 5.32:8
* taducyate na  || bhāṣya 5.32:9
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 5.32:10

**********************************************

sadā rudramanusmaret || 5.33 ||

* atra sadā nityaṃ satatamavyucchinnamiti rudramiti kāraṇāpadeśe  || bhāṣya 5.33:1
* rudrasya rudratvaṃ pūrvoktam  || bhāṣya 5.33:2
* rudramiti dvitīyā karmaṇi  || bhāṣya 5.33:3
* anu pṛṣṭhakarmakriyāyām  || bhāṣya 5.33:4
* pūrvokto dhyeyo'rthaḥ satatamanusmartavyaḥ | smṛtiḥ cintāyām  || bhāṣya 5.33:5
* ūṣmavadavasthitasya karmaṇaścyutihetoḥ kṣapaṇārthaṃ satatamanusmartavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.33:6
* smṛtistu devanityatetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.33:7
* tataḥ kṣīṇe karmaṇi taddoṣahetujālamūlaviśiṣṭasya pratyāsaikanimittābhāvātsāyujyaprāptau na punaḥ saṃsāraḥ  || bhāṣya 5.33:8
* atra śloko nirvacanaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.33:9
* āha ajñānakaluṣapāpavāsanādiprasaṅgaprasaraṇasambhavātsaṃdehaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.33:10
* sūkṣmavadavasthite karmaṇi kṣīṇe'tyantaviśuddhaḥ sāyujyamāsādayati āhosvidaviśuddha iti  || bhāṣya 5.33:11
* ucyate viśuddhaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.33:12
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 5.33:13

**********************************************

chittvā doṣāṇāṃ hetujālasya mūlam  || 5.34 ||

* atra vyutkramābhidhānācchedaḥ kramaśo yojanīyaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.34:1
* yantraṇadhāraṇātmakaśchedo draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.34:2
* kimartham  || bhāṣya 5.34:3
* sūkṣmavadavasthitasya karmaṇaḥ kṣayārthaṃ vasatyarthādinirdeśārthatvāt || bhāṣya 5.34:4
* kiṃca arthānāmanirvacanārthatvāttatra japa eva kevalo'bhihitaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.34:5
* iha tu yata indriyāṇi jetavyāni yo jetā yayā jetavyāni yathā jetavyāni yatprayojanaṃ jetavyāni yasmiṃśca jite jitāni bhavanti tadvakṣyāmaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.34:6
* tathā yasyātmavṛttiradhyayanadhyānasmaraṇādi ca vakṣyāmaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.34:7
* tathā yata ātmā chettavyaḥ chettāraṃ chedakaraṇaṃ chedaprayojanaṃ chedyaṃ chittiṃ yasmin chinne chinnaṃ bhavati tadvakṣyāmaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.34:8
* taducyate chittvā  || bhāṣya 5.34:9
* atra chedo nāma ātmabhāvaviśleṣaṇamātram  || bhāṣya 5.34:10
* vicchedavacanādgamyate  || bhāṣya 5.34:11
* tvā iti śūnyāgāraguhāvasthitasyādhyayanadhyānadhāraṇayantraṇādikaṃ gamyate  || bhāṣya 5.34:12
* āha kiṃ tat || bhāṣya 5.34:13
* kebhyo vā chettavyam  || bhāṣya 5.34:14
* taducyate doṣāṇāṃ hetujālasya mūlamiti  || bhāṣya 5.34:15
* atra doṣāḥ śabdasparśarūparasagandhāḥ  || bhāṣya 5.34:16
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 5.34:17
* kāmārjanādimūlatvāt || bhāṣya 5.34:18
* yasmāduktam | kāmaḥ krodhastathā lobho bhayaṃ svapnaśca pañcamaḥ | rāgo dveṣaśca mohaśca  || bhāṣya 5.34:19
* tathā arjanarakṣaṇakṣayasaṅgahiṃsādayo doṣāḥ  || bhāṣya 5.34:20
* arjanaṃ nāma pratigrahajayakrayavikrayanirveśyādiṣu varṇināṃ viṣayārjanopāyāḥ  || bhāṣya 5.34:21
* eteṣu ca viṣayāṇāmarjane vartatātmapīḍā parapīḍā vā avarjanīye bhavataḥ  || bhāṣya 5.34:22
* tatra yadyātmānaṃ pīḍayati tena ihaiva loke duḥkhī bhavati  || bhāṣya 5.34:23
* syātparaṃ pīḍayati tatrāpyasyādharmo duḥkhādiphalaḥ saṃcīyate  || bhāṣya 5.34:24
* tacca duḥkhaṃ nānyo'nubhavati kartaivānubhavati  || bhāṣya 5.34:25
* api ca kimpākaphalopamā viṣayāḥ  || bhāṣya 5.34:26
* tadyathā śrūyate lavaṇasāgarasaṃnikarṣe kālayavanadvīpe kimpākā nāma viṣavṛkṣāḥ  || bhāṣya 5.34:27
* tatphalānyāsvādenāmṛtopamāni ca kecidajñānādguḍavadbhakṣayanti  || bhāṣya 5.34:28

buddhyā  || 5.35 ||

* antaḥkaraṇākhyā buddhirityuktā  || bhāṣya 5.35:1
* tayā dharmasmṛticodanādisahitayā vidyāgṛhītayā buddhyā chedyaṃ sthāpyaṃ cetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.35:2
* āha kiṃ doṣādisahagatavadhādiviśliṣṭamapi tatparatantramucyate  || bhāṣya 5.35:3
* na  || bhāṣya 5.35:4
* yasmādāha mūlākhyāyāṃ nivṛttāyām  || bhāṣya 5.35:5

saṃcittam  || 5.36 ||

* atra samiti doṣādiviśliṣṭaṃ svayameva svaguṇatvena parigṛhyate agnyuṣṇatvavadityānubandhitvāccetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.36:1
* āha kiṃ taditi  || bhāṣya 5.36:2
* ucyate cittam  || bhāṣya 5.36:3
* atra citī saṃjñāne cetayati cinoti vā aneneti cittam  || bhāṣya 5.36:4
* cetayati sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ padārthān cinoti dharmādharmau arjayatītyataḥ cetayati cinoti vā aneneti cittam  || bhāṣya 5.36:5
* cittaṃ mano'ntaḥkaraṇamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.36:6
* atra tvetebhyo doṣahetutvādibhyo yugapacchettavyaṃ vidyamānebhyastu kramaśaḥ kṣapaṇamiti  || bhāṣya 5.36:7
* āha chittvā taccittaṃ kiṃ kartavyam  || bhāṣya 5.36:8
* ucyate rudrastham  || bhāṣya 5.36:9
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 5.36:10

sthāpayitvā ca rudre  || 5.37 ||

* atra ṣṭhā gatinivṛttau  || bhāṣya 5.37:1
* cittasya rudrādavyavadhānaṃ sthitirityucyate  || bhāṣya 5.37:2
* vā iti śmaśānādyavasthasya smṛtikarmaṇo niṣṭhā  || bhāṣya 5.37:3
* caśabdaḥ samuccaye  || bhāṣya 5.37:4
* na kevalaṃ chittvā stheyaṃ kiṃtu sthāpayitavyaṃ cetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.37:5
* rudre iti kāraṇāpadeśe  || bhāṣya 5.37:6
* rudrasya rudratvaṃ pūrvoktaṃ rudra ityaupaśleṣikaṃ saṃnidhānam  || bhāṣya 5.37:7
* rudre cittamupaśleṣayitavyaṃ nānyatretyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.37:8
* evaṃ viṣayebhya indriyāṇāṃ jayaḥ kartavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.37:9
* atra yo jetā ātmā  || bhāṣya 5.37:10
* yayā jetavyāni buddhyā  || bhāṣya 5.37:11
* yathā jetavyāni kramaśaśca  || bhāṣya 5.37:12
* yatprayojanaṃ jetavyāni cittasthityartham  || bhāṣya 5.37:13
* yasmiṃśca jite jitāni bhavanti cittamityetadapi vyākhyātam  || bhāṣya 5.37:14
* evaṃ japayantraṇadhāraṇātmakacchedādiṣvapi yojyam  || bhāṣya 5.37:15
* tathāntaḥkaraṇavṛttimāsthāya kālaviśeṣanimittaraśmimaṇidīpavattathātmavṛttiradhyayanadhyānasmaraṇādīni cittasthitiśca vyākhyātā  || bhāṣya 5.37:16
* śloko nirvacanaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.37:17
* āha kāryakaraṇaṃ ca taccittasthitisamakālameva rudre sthitāni tāni yuktāni  || bhāṣya 5.37:18
* atha kiṃ tānyeva yuktasya lakṣaṇāni iti  || bhāṣya 5.37:19
* ucyate na  || bhāṣya 5.37:20
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 5.37:21

ekaḥ kṣemī san vītaśokaḥ  || 5.38 ||

* atra dharmādharmayorvṛttyoruparame avasitaprayojanatvātpakvaphalavatsarpakañcukavadgataprāyeṣu kāryakaraṇeṣu rudre sthitacitto niṣkala eka ityabhidhīyate  || bhāṣya 5.38:1
* tathā yogavyāsaṅgakare'dharme nivṛtte doṣādiviśliṣṭo nistīrṇakāntāravadavasthito rudre sthitacittaḥ kṣemī ityabhidhīyate  || bhāṣya 5.38:2
* tathā sūkṣmasthūlasabāhyābhyantarasalakṣaṇavilakṣaṇāsu kriyāsu vinivṛttāsu rudre sthitacitto niṣkriyaḥ sanityabhidhīyate  || bhāṣya 5.38:3
* āha atha niṣkriyo'yamiti kathamavagamyate  || bhāṣya 5.38:4
* kiṃ cātra yuktasya lakṣaṇatrayameva  || bhāṣya 5.38:5
* ucyate na  || bhāṣya 5.38:6
* yasmādāha vītaśokaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.38:7
* atra śokaścintetyanarthāntaram  || bhāṣya 5.38:8
* sā ca cintā dvividhā bhavati  || bhāṣya 5.38:9
* kuśalā cākuśalā ca  || bhāṣya 5.38:10
* tatra kuśalā nāma adhyayanadhyānasmaraṇādyā  || bhāṣya 5.38:11
* akuśalā nāma anadhyayanādhyānāsmaraṇādyā  || bhāṣya 5.38:12
* evaṃ japayantraṇadhāraṇādīṃśca kariṣyāmi na kariṣyāmītyevamanekavidhāyāmapi cintāyāṃ vinivṛttāyāṃ vyapagataśoko vītaśoka ityabhidhīyate  || bhāṣya 5.38:13
* evamatra yogapadārthaḥ samāptaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.38:14
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 5.38:15
* arthānāṃ nirvacanatvāt || bhāṣya 5.38:16
* yasmādasyādhyāyasyādāvuddiṣṭā ye padārthāste doṣacchedāsaṅgasthityādiṣu vyākhyātāḥ  || bhāṣya 5.38:17
* evamanena yuktena brahmādayo devā viśeṣitā bhavanti  || bhāṣya 5.38:18
* tadasaṅgādivacanāt || bhāṣya 5.38:19
* āha atha sāṃkhyayogamuktāḥ kiṃ na viśeṣitāḥ  || bhāṣya 5.38:20
* ucyate viśeṣitāḥ  || bhāṣya 5.38:21
* katham  || bhāṣya 5.38:22
* tajjñānātiśayāt || bhāṣya 5.38:23
* katham  || bhāṣya 5.38:24
* sāṃkhyayogamuktāḥ kaivalyagatāḥ svātmaparātmajñānarahitāḥ saṃmūrchitavatsthitāḥ  || bhāṣya 5.38:25
* asya tu jñānamasti  || bhāṣya 5.38:26
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 5.38:27

apramādī gacchedduḥkhānāmantamīśaprasādāt || 5.39 ||

* iti  || bhāṣya 5.39:1
* evaṃ kurvan sarvajño'syāsaṃmohaṃ jñāpayati  || bhāṣya 5.39:2
* uktam | kāryakaraṇāñjanebhyo nirañjanebhyaśca sarvapuruṣebhyaḥ | aprāptāntaṃ puruṣaṃ yo'bhyadhikaṃ varṇayetsa budhaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.39:3
* āha gataṃ yadgantavyam  || bhāṣya 5.39:4
* atha kimayamupacāraḥ  || bhāṣya 5.39:5
* ucyate na  || bhāṣya 5.39:6
* aparijñānānnāsmākaṃ yoganiṣṭhaṃ tantram  || bhāṣya 5.39:7
* apitu tatkaivalyavyatirikto'pi sarvajñenocyate  || bhāṣya 5.39:8
* apramādādgacchedduḥkhānāmantamīśaprasādāt || bhāṣya 5.39:9
* atra pramādaśabdo'nāgatānavadhānagatatvaṃ pāratantryaṃ ca khyāpayatītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.39:10
* tadaṅkuraparirakṣaṇavadanāgatakālapratīkārakaraṇena caivāyamapramādīśabdo draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.39:11
* tasmādyuktenaivāpramādinā stheyam  || bhāṣya 5.39:12
* tathā vartamānena māheśvaramaiśvaryaṃ prāptamevetyuktam  || bhāṣya 5.39:13
* gacchediti gatiḥ prāptirbhavati  || bhāṣya 5.39:14
* gamḷ sṛpḷ gatau  || bhāṣya 5.39:15
* prāpnotītyātmeti pattrapāṇḍutāphalapākavat || bhāṣya 5.39:16
* katham  || bhāṣya 5.39:17
* tasminneva pravartato yo'yaṃ duḥkhāpoho guṇastatreyaṃ gatiriti saṃjñā kriyata ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.39:18
* duḥkhānāmityatra prasiddhāni duḥkhānyādhyātmikādhibhautikādhidaivikāni  || bhāṣya 5.39:19
* tatrādhyātmikaṃ dvividhaṃ duḥkhaṃ śārīraṃ mānasaṃ ca  || bhāṣya 5.39:20
* tatra manasi bhavaṃ mānasaṃ krodhalobhamohabhayaviṣāderṣyāsūyādveṣamadamānamātsaryāratyādyaviśeṣadarśanādinimittaṃ tadduḥkham  || bhāṣya 5.39:21
* tathā śārīramapi śirorogadantarogākṣirogajvarapratimatsyātisārakāsaśvāsodarāmayādinimittotpannaṃ duḥkham  || bhāṣya 5.39:22
* tathānyadapi pañcavidhaṃ duḥkhaṃ bhavati  || bhāṣya 5.39:23
* tadyathā garbhajanmājñānajarāmaraṇamiti  || bhāṣya 5.39:24
* tatra garbhe tāvadyadāyaṃ puruṣo māturudare nyastagātraḥ khaṇḍaśakaṭastha iva pumānniyamaśramamanubhavamāno'vakāśarahitaḥ ākuñcanaprasāraṇādiṣvaparyāptāvakāśaḥ sarvakriyāsu niruddha ityevamadvārake andhatamasi mūḍho bandhanastha iva pumānavaśyaṃ samanubhavati  || bhāṣya 5.39:25
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 5.39:26
* cetanatvādbhoktṛtvāttanmayatvācca na tu kāryakaraṇāni  || bhāṣya 5.39:27
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 5.39:28
* acetanāvādabhoktṛtvādatanmayatvācca  || bhāṣya 5.39:29
* tathā janmaduḥkhamapi  || bhāṣya 5.39:30
* yadāyaṃ puruṣo jāyamānaḥ purīṣapaṅkamagnavadano mūtradhārābhirabhiṣicyamāno dehe saṃvṛtadvārake yoniniḥsaraṇasaṃkaṭe'tyarthaṃ pīḍyamāno'sthimarmabandhanaiḥ praghṛṣyamāṇo vikrośanninadaṃśca jāyate  || bhāṣya 5.39:31
* paścātpunastasyānucitena bāhyena vāyunā jananāvartena spṛṣṭasya tīvraṃ duḥkhamabhivyajyate  || bhāṣya 5.39:32
* rājapuṣṭakādivat || bhāṣya 5.39:33
* tena cāsya jātyantarādismṛtihetusaṃskāralopo bhavati  || bhāṣya 5.39:34
* eva janmaduḥkhaṃ puruṣa evānubhavati  || bhāṣya 5.39:35
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 5.39:36
* cetanatvādbhoktṛtvāttanmayatvācca  || bhāṣya 5.39:37
* na tu kāryakaraṇāni  || bhāṣya 5.39:38
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 5.39:39
* acetanatvādabhoktṛtvādatanmayatvācca  || bhāṣya 5.39:40
* tathā ajñānaduḥkhamapi ahaṃkārasatkṛtagātro na jānan ko'haṃ kuto'haṃ kasyāhaṃ kena vā bandhanena baddho'hamiti kiṃ kāraṇaṃ kimakāraṇaṃ kiṃ bhakṣyaṃ kimabhakṣyaṃ kiṃ peyaṃ kimapeyaṃ kiṃ satyaṃ kimasatyaṃ kiṃ jñānaṃ kimajñānamityajñānaduḥkhaṃ puruṣa evānubhavati  || bhāṣya 5.39:41
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 5.39:42
* cetanatvādbhoktṛtvāttanmayatvācca  || bhāṣya 5.39:43
* na tu kāryakaraṇāni  || bhāṣya 5.39:44
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 5.39:45
* acetanatvādabhoktṛtvādatanmayatvācca  || bhāṣya 5.39:46
* tathā jarāduḥkhamapi  || bhāṣya 5.39:47
* yadāyaṃ puruṣo jarājarjaritaḥ kṛśaśarīraḥ śithilīkṛtanayanakapolanāsikābhrūdaśanāvaraṇaḥ krauñcajānuriva nirviṇṇo'kṣidūṣikādiṣvapakarṣaṇādiṣvasamartho vihaṃga iva lūnapakṣo laṅghanaplavanadhāvanādiṣvasamarthaḥ pūrvātītāni bhogavyāyāmaśilpakarmāṇyanusmaramāṇaḥ smṛtivaikalyamāpanno'vaśyaṃ kleśamanubhavati  || bhāṣya 5.39:48
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 5.39:49
* cetanatvādbhoktṛtvāttanmayatvācca  || bhāṣya 5.39:50
* na tu kāryakaraṇāṇi  || bhāṣya 5.39:51
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 5.39:52
* acetanatvādabhoktṛtvādatanmayatvācca  || bhāṣya 5.39:53
* tathā mṛtyuduḥkhamapi  || bhāṣya 5.39:54
* yadāyaṃ puruṣo maraṇasamaye ślathakaraṇaḥ śirodharamavalambamānaḥ śvāsanocchvasanatatparaḥ khurukhurāyamāṇakaṇṭhaḥ svopārjitamaṇikanakadhanadhānyapatnīputrapaśusaṃghātaḥ kasya bhaviṣyatītyanutapyamānaḥ viṣayānanu dodūyamānaḥ salilādi yācamāno viraktavadano marmabhiśchidyamānairavaśyaṃ kleśamanubhavati  || bhāṣya 5.39:55
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 5.39:56
* cetanatvādbhoktṛtvāttanmayatvācca  || bhāṣya 5.39:57
* na tu kāryakaraṇāni  || bhāṣya 5.39:58
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 5.39:59
* acetanatvādabhoktṛtvādatanmayatvācca  || bhāṣya 5.39:60
* uktaṃ hi | garbhe praviśan duḥkhaṃ nivasan duḥkhaṃ viniṣkraman duḥkham | jātaśca duḥkhamṛcchati tasmādapunarbhavaḥ śreyān  || bhāṣya 5.39:61
* iti  || bhāṣya 5.39:62
* tathānyadapi pañcavidhaṃ duḥkhaṃ bhavati  || bhāṣya 5.39:63
* tadyathā ihalokabhayaṃ paralokabhayamahitasaṃprayogaḥ hitaviprayogaḥ icchāvyāghātaśceti  || bhāṣya 5.39:64
* tathānyadapi trividhaṃ duḥkhaṃ bhavati  || bhāṣya 5.39:65
* ādhyātmikamajñānaṃ puruṣe ādhibhautikaṃ viṣayitvamādhidaivikaṃ ca paśutvaṃ trividhamaparaṃ prāhuḥ  || bhāṣya 5.39:66
* ityevamādīni bādhanāyā aprītiphalāyā janmanimittatvādduḥkhānītyupacaryante  || bhāṣya 5.39:67
* āha caraṇādhikāre'natiprasādādaśivatvasaṃjñake sarvāṇyanatiprasādabījatvātkuto nātyantanivṛttāni bhavanti  || bhāṣya 5.39:68
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 5.39:69
* saṃhāraṃ prāptasya nigaḍamuktādhikāravanmuktāvatiśayitaguṇaprāptyarthamucyate gacchedduḥkhānāmantam  || bhāṣya 5.39:70
* duḥkhānāmatyantaṃ paramāpoho guṇāvāptiśca paraṃ bhavatīti  || bhāṣya 5.39:71
* tadubhayamapi ita eva bhavatīti  || bhāṣya 5.39:72
* tadāha īśaprasādāt || bhāṣya 5.39:73
* atreśa ityetadbhagavato nāmadheyam  || bhāṣya 5.39:74
* īśaḥ kasmāt || bhāṣya 5.39:75
* vidyādikāryasyeśanādīśaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.39:76
* prasādo nāma sampradānecchā  || bhāṣya 5.39:77
* tasmātprasādātsarvaduḥkhāpoho guṇāvāptiścadimupādhyantarātparaparivādādivacanātśuddhiriva yugapadityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.39:78
* evamayamathaśabdaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.39:79
* paśupaterityuddiṣṭayorduḥkhāntaprasādayorgacchedduḥkhānāmantamīśaprasādāditi duḥkhāntaṃ parisamāptamiti  || bhāṣya 5.39:80
* evamadhyāyaparisamāptiṃ kṛtvā yuktaṃ vaktum  || bhāṣya 5.39:81

**********************************************

atredaṃ brahma japet || 5.40 ||

* iti  || bhāṣya 5.40:1
* asya pūrvokto'rthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.40:2
* vidhinaiva pūrvoktena vidhinā japtavyam  || bhāṣya 5.40:3
* na tu duḥkhāntagatena gaṇapativadityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.40:4
* āha kāmitvātkṛpayā bhagavatā duḥkhānto dattaḥ svecchayaiva na punaraduḥkhāntaṃ kariṣyati  || bhāṣya 5.40:5
* athāśaktastathāpyasya śaktivyāghātaḥ pācakavadakarmāpekṣatvaṃ cocyate  || bhāṣya 5.40:6
* atra yathā nityo duḥkhāntastathā vakṣyāmaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.40:7
* yathā ca kāṅkṣato lipsataśca sādhakādhikāranivṛttistathā vakṣyāmaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.40:8
* padārthanigamanārthe cocyate  || bhāṣya 5.40:9

**********************************************

īśānaḥ sarvavidyānām  || 5.41 ||

* atra īśanādīśānaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.41:1
* atreśanādīśāna ityuktaṃ kāraṇam  || bhāṣya 5.41:2
* īśānaḥ prabhuḥ dhātetyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.41:3
* āha kasyāyamīśānaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.41:4
* taducyate sarvasyeśānaḥ | sarvaśabdo vidyāprakṛterniravaśeṣavācī draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.41:5
* vidyānāṃ dharmārthakāmakaivalyatatsādhanaparāṇāmīśānaḥ vidyānāmiti ṣaṣṭhībahuvacanam  || bhāṣya 5.41:6
* āha kiṃ vidyānāmeveśānaḥ na tu vidyābhirye vidanti  || bhāṣya 5.41:7
* ucyate  || bhāṣya 5.41:8

**********************************************

īśvaraḥ sarvabhūtānām  || 5.42 ||

* atra niratiśaya aiśvaryeṇa īśvaraḥ  || bhāṣya 5.42:1
* puruṣaḥ caitanyavadityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.42:2
* āha kasyāyamīśvaraḥ  || bhāṣya 5.42:3
* taducyate sarvabhūtānām | atra cetanācetaneṣu sarvaśabdaḥ na kevalaṃ pṛthivyādiṣu kiṃtu siddheśvaravarjaṃ cetaneṣveva sarvabhūtaprakṛterniravaśeṣavācī sarvaśabdo draṣṭavyaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.42:4
* kasmādbhūtāni  || bhāṣya 5.42:5
* bhāvanatvādbhūtānītyuktaṃ bhūtānāmiti ṣaṣṭhībahuvacanam  || bhāṣya 5.42:6
* āha atra kecidvidyābhūtavyatiriktaṃ brahmāṇamicchanti  || bhāṣya 5.42:7
* tasyāyaṃ kiṃ prabhurbhavati neti  || bhāṣya 5.42:8
* ucyate prabhuḥ  || bhāṣya 5.42:9
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 5.42:10

**********************************************

brahmaṇo'dhipatirbrahmā  || 5.43 ||

* atra yo'yaṃ viriñciḥ paramaḥ patiḥ sarvacetanavyatiriktaḥ kṣetrajñaḥ tasmin brahmasaṃjñā  || bhāṣya 5.43:1
* na tu pradhānādiṣu  || bhāṣya 5.43:2
* kasmāt || bhāṣya 5.43:3
* adhipativacanavirodhāt || bhāṣya 5.43:4
* brahma ca kasmāt || bhāṣya 5.43:5
* bṛṃhaṇatvādbṛhattvādbrahmā  || bhāṣya 5.43:6
* bṛṃhayate yasmādvidyākalābhūtāni bṛhacca tebhya ityato'dhipatirbrahmā  || bhāṣya 5.43:7
* brahmaṇa iti ṣaṣṭhī  || bhāṣya 5.43:8
* adhiradhiṣṭhātṛtve  || bhāṣya 5.43:9
* tatsvābhāvyātsaṃhṛte cāsaṃhṛte ca kārya ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.43:10
* patyuḥ patiḥ adhipatiḥ  || bhāṣya 5.43:11
* rājarājavat || bhāṣya 5.43:12
* patiḥ pālane patirdarśane bhoge ca  || bhāṣya 5.43:13
* pālayate yasmādbrahmādīnīśvaraḥ  || bhāṣya 5.43:14
* pāti brahmādikāryam | adhipatiḥ brahmā  || bhāṣya 5.43:15
* adhipatirīśvaraḥ  || bhāṣya 5.43:16
* evaṃ bṛṃhayate yasmādvidyādikāryaṃ bṛhacca tebhya ityato'dhipatirbrahmā bhagavāniti  || bhāṣya 5.43:17
* āha atra kāryakaraṇamahābhāgyamevātra brahmaṇi cintyate na tu sādhakasya lipsā lābho veti  || bhāṣya 5.43:18
* ucyate na  || bhāṣya 5.43:19
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 5.43:20

**********************************************

śivo me astu  || 5.44 ||

* atra yeṣāṃ sādhikāratvādanatiprasannasteṣāmaśivatvaṃ dṛṣṭvā duḥkhāntaṃ gateṣu ca śivatvaṃ dṛṣṭvā āha śivo me astu iti  || bhāṣya 5.44:1
* me ityātmāpadeśe mametyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.44:2
* astviti kāṅkṣāyām  || bhāṣya 5.44:3
* kāṅkṣati lipsati mṛgayatītyarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.44:4
* āha kiyantaṃ kālaṃ bhagavānasya śivo bhavati  || bhāṣya 5.44:5
* taducyate nityam  || bhāṣya 5.44:6
* yasmādāha  || bhāṣya 5.44:7

**********************************************

sadā  || 5.45 ||

* atra sadā nityaṃ saṃtatamavyucchinnamityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.45:1
* āha kamevamāha  || bhāṣya 5.45:2
* ko vāsya śivo bhavatīti  || bhāṣya 5.45:3
* ucyate  || bhāṣya 5.45:4

**********************************************

śivaḥ  || 5.46 ||

* atra śiva ityetadapi bhagavato nāma  || bhāṣya 5.46:1
* śivaḥ kasmāt || bhāṣya 5.46:2
* paripūrṇaparitṛptatvācchivaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.46:3
* tasmātsadāśivopadeśānnityo duḥkhāntaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.46:4
* kāraṇādhikāranivṛttiḥ  || bhāṣya 5.46:5
* tadarthaṃ nityo duḥkhānta iti siddham  || bhāṣya 5.46:6
* evamete pañca padārthāḥ kāryakāraṇayogavidhiduḥkhāntāḥ samāsavistaravibhāgaviśeṣopasaṃhāranigamanataśca vyākhyātāḥ  || bhāṣya 5.46:7
* uktaṃ hi  || bhāṣya 5.46:8
* ādau yadbhavati samāsoktaṃ madhye tasya vistarataśca vibhāgataścopanayanigamanena satāmapyeṣa niścayaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.46:9
* iti  || bhāṣya 5.46:10
* atra tāvatpatiriti kāraṇapadārthasyopadeśaḥ samāsena  || bhāṣya 5.46:11
* vistarastu vāmo devo jyeṣṭho rudraḥ kāmaḥ śaṃkaraḥ kālaḥ kalavikaraṇo balavikaraṇo'ghoro ghorataraḥ sarvaḥ śarva tatpuruṣo mahādeva oṃkāra ṛṣirvipro mahānīśa īśāna īśvaro'dhipatirbrahmā śiva ityevamādyo vistaraḥ  || bhāṣya 5.46:12
* vibhāgo'pi anyatpatitvamanyadajātatvamanyadbhavodbhavatvamityādyo vibhāgaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.46:13
* viśeṣaḥ anyeṣāṃ pradhānādīni asmākaṃ tadvyatirikto bhagavānīśvaraḥ  || bhāṣya 5.46:14
* kāraṇādhikāre yasmādāha īśvaraḥ sarvabhūtānāmiti  || bhāṣya 5.46:15
* eṣa upasaṃhāraḥ sārvakāmika ityācakṣate  || bhāṣya 5.46:16
* nigamanamīśa īśāna īśvaro'dhipatirbrahmā śiva iti  || bhāṣya 5.46:17
* tathā paśuriti kāryapadārthasyoddeśaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.46:18
* tasya vistaro vidyā kalā paśavaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.46:19
* utpādyā anugrāhyāstirobhāvyakālpyavikāryamaspadasya bodhyadhiṣṭheyatve cetyevamādyaḥ sūtravidyādharmārthakāmairbhedairduḥkhāntaḥ vidyā  || bhāṣya 5.46:20
* kalā dvividhāḥ  || bhāṣya 5.46:21
* kāryākhyāḥ karaṇākhyāśca  || bhāṣya 5.46:22
* tatra kāryākhyāḥ pṛthivyādyāḥ  || bhāṣya 5.46:23
* karaṇākhyā buddhyādyāḥ  || bhāṣya 5.46:24
* paśavaśca trividhāḥ  || bhāṣya 5.46:25
* devā manuṣyāstiryañcaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.46:26
* tatra devā aṣṭavidhā brahmādyāḥ  || bhāṣya 5.46:27
* mānuṣyaṃ cānekavidhaṃ brāhmaṇādyam  || bhāṣya 5.46:28
* tiryagyoni ca pañcavidhaṃ paśumṛgādyam  || bhāṣya 5.46:29
* paśavaḥ sāñjanā nirañjanāśca  || bhāṣya 5.46:30
* evamādyo vistaraḥ  || bhāṣya 5.46:31
* vibhāgo'pi anyā vidyā anyāḥ kalāḥ anye ca paśava ityevamādyo vibhāgaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.46:32
* viśeṣaḥ anyeṣāṃ pradhānādīni kāraṇāni tānīha śāstre kāryatvena vyākhyātāni  || bhāṣya 5.46:33
* tatra pradhānaṃ kāraṇamanyeṣāṃ tadiha śāstre paśyanātpāśakatvātkāryatvena vyākhyātam  || bhāṣya 5.46:34
* tathā puruṣaḥ kāraṇamanyatra iha śāstre paśutvātkāryatvena vyākhyātaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.46:35
* tathā karmamadhyatvātkālaḥ svabhāvaḥ upasaṃhāravat || bhāṣya 5.46:36
* bhūtāni vikāryatvātkāryatvena vyākhyātāni  || bhāṣya 5.46:37
* ityeṣa viśeṣaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.46:38
* upasaṃhāraḥ sārvakāmika ityarthaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.46:39
* nigamanaṃ vidyākalābhūtāni brahmeti  || bhāṣya 5.46:40
* tathā yogamiti yogapadārthasyoddeśaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.46:41
* tasyaivaṃ carataḥ yogaḥ pravartate ubhayathā yaṣṭavyaḥ atyāgatiṃ gamayate nānyabhaktistu śaṃkare evaṃ devanityatānityayuktatā adhyayanaṃ dhyānaṃ smaraṇaṃ nityasāyujyamiti vistaraḥ vibhāgaḥ kriyālakṣaṇaṃ kriyoparamalakṣaṇaṃ dūradarśanaśravaṇamananavijñānāni gaṇapatiḥ bhūyiṣṭhaṃ sampravartate siddhaḥ gacchedduḥkhānāmantamityevamādyo vibhāgaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.46:42
* jñānaśaktiḥ kriyāśaktiśca  || bhāṣya 5.46:43
* tatra jñānaśaktiḥ śravaṇādyā  || bhāṣya 5.46:44
* kriyāśaktiḥ manojavitvādyā  || bhāṣya 5.46:45
* ityevamādyo vibhāgaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.46:46
* viśeṣaḥ anyeṣāṃ kaivalyamiha tu viśeṣo vikaraṇamiti  || bhāṣya 5.46:47
* pratikaraṇa iti kaivalyadharmātiśaktirniṣkalamaiśvaryamityeṣa viśeṣaḥ  || bhāṣya 5.46:48
* upasaṃhāraḥ ityebhirguṇairyukta iti  || bhāṣya 5.46:49
* ato yāvanti vākyaviśeṣāṇi saṃnikṛṣṭaviprakṛṣṭāni nirvacanāni tāni ca sarvanirvacanānīti kṛtvā yuktamuktam  || bhāṣya 5.46:50
* evamatra śrībhagavatkauṇḍinyaviracite śrīmadyogapāśupataśāstrasūtravyākhyāne pañcārthabhāṣye pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ saha brahmaṇā granthato'rthataśca parisamāpta iti  || bhāṣya 5.46:51
* śubham  || bhāṣya 5.46:52
